import com.unilend.backend.entity.AccountToken;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.AccountToken.TokenType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.AccountTokenRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.security.MessageDigest;
import java.security.NoSuchAlgorithmException;
import java.security.SecureRandom;
import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Base64;
import java.util.HexFormat;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AccountTokenService {

    private static final SecureRandom SECURE_RANDOM = new SecureRandom();

    private final AccountTokenRepository accountTokenRepository;

    @Value("${app.security.verify-email-ttl-minutes:1440}")
    private long verifyEmailTtlMinutes;

    @Value("${app.security.reset-password-ttl-minutes:15}")
    private long resetPasswordTtlMinutes;

    @Transactional
    public String createEmailVerificationToken(User user) {
        return createToken(user, TokenType.EMAIL_VERIFICATION, Duration.ofMinutes(verifyEmailTtlMinutes));
    }

    @Transactional
    public String createPasswordResetToken(User user) {
        return createToken(user, TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET, Duration.ofMinutes(resetPasswordTtlMinutes));
    }

    @Transactional
    public User consumeToken(String rawToken, TokenType expectedType) {
        if (rawToken == null || rawToken.isBlank()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Token khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡");
        }
        String hash = hashToken(rawToken.trim());
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        if (accountTokenRepository.consumeValidToken(hash, expectedType, now) == 0) {
            AccountToken accountToken = accountTokenRepository.findByTokenHashAndType(hash, expectedType)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new BusinessException(
                            ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Token khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ hoáº·c khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i"));
            if (accountToken.isUsed()) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Token nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c sá»­ dá»¥ng");
            }
            if (accountToken.isExpired()) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Token Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n");
            }
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Token khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡");
        }

        return accountTokenRepository.findByTokenHashAndType(hash, expectedType)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new BusinessException(
                        ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Token khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ hoáº·c khÃ´ng tá»“n táº¡i"))
                .getUser();
    }

    private String createToken(User user, TokenType type, Duration ttl) {
        accountTokenRepository.invalidateUnusedTokens(user.getId(), type, Instant.now());

        byte[] randomBytes = new byte[32];
        SECURE_RANDOM.nextBytes(randomBytes);
        String rawToken = Base64.getUrlEncoder().withoutPadding().encodeToString(randomBytes);

        AccountToken tokenEntity = AccountToken.builder()
                .user(user)
                .type(type)
                .tokenHash(hashToken(rawToken))
                .expiresAt(Instant.now().plus(ttl))
                .build();
        accountTokenRepository.save(tokenEntity);
        return rawToken;
    }

    public String hashToken(String rawToken) {
        try {
            MessageDigest digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256");
            byte[] hashed = digest.digest(rawToken.getBytes(StandardCharsets.UTF_8));
            return HexFormat.of().formatHex(hashed);
        } catch (NoSuchAlgorithmException e) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("SHA-256 algorithm not available", e);
        }
    }
}
===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/AdminStatsService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.dto.admin.AdminStatsResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.admin.AdminStatsResponse.CategoryLoanCount;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest.LoanStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ItemRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.LoanRequestRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminStatsService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final ItemRepository itemRepository;
    private final LoanRequestRepository loanRequestRepository;

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AdminStatsResponse getStats() {
        Map<String, Long> loansByStatus = new LinkedHashMap<>();
        for (LoanStatus status : LoanStatus.values()) {
            loansByStatus.put(status.name(), 0L);
        }
        for (Object[] row : loanRequestRepository.countLoansByStatus()) {
            LoanStatus status = (LoanStatus) row[0];
            loansByStatus.put(status.name(), ((Number) row[1]).longValue());
        }

        List<CategoryLoanCount> categories = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Object[] row : loanRequestRepository.findMostBorrowedCategories(PageRequest.of(0, 5))) {
            categories.add(new CategoryLoanCount(
                    ((Number) row[0]).longValue(),
                    (String) row[1],
                    ((Number) row[2]).longValue()));
        }
        return new AdminStatsResponse(
                userRepository.count(), itemRepository.count(), loansByStatus, categories);
    }
}

===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/AdminUserService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.user.UserProfileResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AdminUserService {

    private static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;

    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final CurrentUserService currentUserService;

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<UserProfileResponse> findAll(String keyword, Boolean enabled, int page, int size) {
        currentUserService.requireAdmin();
        String kw = (keyword == null || keyword.isBlank()) ? null : keyword.trim();
        return PageResponse.from(
                userRepository.searchUsers(kw, enabled, pageable(page, size)),
                UserProfileResponse::from);
    }

    @Transactional
    public UserProfileResponse lockUser(Long targetUserId) {
        User admin = currentUserService.requireAdmin();
        if (admin.getId().equals(targetUserId)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ tá»± khÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n cá»§a chÃ­nh mÃ¬nh");
        }
        User target = getOrThrow(targetUserId);
        if (target.isEnabled() && target.getRoles().contains("ADMIN")) {
            boolean anotherEnabledAdminExists = userRepository.findAllByEnabledTrue().stream()
                    .anyMatch(user -> !user.getId().equals(targetUserId)
                            && user.getRoles().contains("ADMIN"));
            if (!anotherEnabledAdminExists) {
                throw new BusinessException(
                        ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                        "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ khÃ³a quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn Ä‘ang hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng cuá»‘i cÃ¹ng");
            }
        }
        if (target.isEnabled()) {
            target.setEnabled(false);
            target.incrementTokenVersion();
        }
        return UserProfileResponse.from(userRepository.save(target));
    }

    @Transactional
    public UserProfileResponse unlockUser(Long targetUserId) {
        currentUserService.requireAdmin();
        User target = getOrThrow(targetUserId);
        if (!target.isEnabled()) {
            target.setEnabled(true);
            target.incrementTokenVersion();
        }
        return UserProfileResponse.from(userRepository.save(target));
    }

    private User getOrThrow(Long id) {
        return userRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", id));
    }

    private Pageable pageable(int page, int size) {
        int safePage = Math.max(page, 0);
        int safeSize = Math.min(Math.max(size, 1), MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        return PageRequest.of(safePage, safeSize, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "createdAt", "id"));
    }
}

===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/AuthService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.auth.*;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.AccountToken.TokenType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.AuthenticationManager;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.security.core.AuthenticationException;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class AuthService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    private final AuthenticationManager authenticationManager;
    private final JwtService jwtService;
    private final CurrentUserService currentUserService;
    private final ReviewService reviewService;
    private final AccountTokenService accountTokenService;
    private final EmailService emailService;
    private final RateLimitService rateLimitService;

    @Transactional
    public AuthResponse register(RegisterRequest request) {
        String email = normalize(request.email());
        rateLimitService.checkEmailRateLimit("register", email);

        if (userRepository.existsByEmail(email)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "Email Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c sá»­ dá»¥ng");
        }

        User user = User.builder()
                .email(email)
                .password(passwordEncoder.encode(request.password()))
                .fullName(request.fullName().trim())
                .emailVerified(false)
                .build();

        user = userRepository.save(user);
        String rawToken = accountTokenService.createEmailVerificationToken(user);
        emailService.sendVerificationEmail(user.getEmail(), user.getFullName(), rawToken);

        return AuthResponse.of(user, jwtService.generateToken(
                user.getId(), user.getEmail(), user.getRoles().stream().toList(), user.getTokenVersion()));
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AuthResponse login(LoginRequest request) {
        String email = normalize(request.email());
        rateLimitService.checkLoginRateLimit(email);

        try {
            authenticationManager.authenticate(
                    new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken(email, request.password()));
        } catch (AuthenticationException ex) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, "Email hoáº·c máº­t kháº©u khÃ´ng Ä‘Ãºng");
        }

        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new BusinessException(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, "Email hoáº·c máº­t kháº©u khÃ´ng Ä‘Ãºng"));
        if (!user.isEnabled()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "TÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ khÃ³a");
        }
        return AuthResponse.of(user, jwtService.generateToken(
                user.getId(), user.getEmail(), user.getRoles().stream().toList(), user.getTokenVersion()));
    }

    @Transactional
    public void verifyEmail(VerifyEmailRequest request) {
        User user = accountTokenService.consumeToken(request.token(), TokenType.EMAIL_VERIFICATION);
        if (!user.isEnabled()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "TÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ khÃ³a");
        }
        user.setEmailVerified(true);
        userRepository.save(user);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void resendVerificationEmail(ForgotPasswordRequest request) {
        String email = normalize(request.email());
        rateLimitService.checkEmailRateLimit("resend-verify", email);

        userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .filter(User::isEnabled)
                .filter(u -> !u.isEmailVerified())
                .ifPresent(user -> {
                    String rawToken = accountTokenService.createEmailVerificationToken(user);
                    emailService.sendVerificationEmail(user.getEmail(), user.getFullName(), rawToken);
                });
    }

    @Transactional
    public void forgotPassword(ForgotPasswordRequest request) {
        String email = normalize(request.email());
        rateLimitService.checkEmailRateLimit("forgot-password", email);

        userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .filter(User::isEnabled)
                .ifPresent(user -> {
                    String rawToken = accountTokenService.createPasswordResetToken(user);
                    emailService.sendPasswordResetEmail(user.getEmail(), user.getFullName(), rawToken);
                });
    }

    @Transactional
    public void resetPassword(ResetPasswordRequest request) {
        User user = accountTokenService.consumeToken(request.token(), TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET);
        if (!user.isEnabled()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "TÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ khÃ³a");
        }
        user.setPassword(passwordEncoder.encode(request.newPassword()));
        user.incrementTokenVersion();
        userRepository.save(user);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public AuthResponse getCurrentUser() {
        User user = currentUserService.getCurrentUser();
        double avgRating = reviewService.getAverageRating(user.getId());
        long reviewCount = reviewService.getReviewCount(user.getId());
        return AuthResponse.of(user, avgRating, reviewCount);
    }

    private String normalize(String email) {
        return email.trim().toLowerCase();
    }
}
===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/CategoryService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.category.CategoryRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.category.CategoryResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Category;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.CategoryRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CategoryService {

    private static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;

    private final CategoryRepository categoryRepository;

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<CategoryResponse> findAll(int page, int size) {
        return PageResponse.from(
                categoryRepository.findAll(pageable(page, size)),
                CategoryResponse::from);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public CategoryResponse findById(Long id) {
        return CategoryResponse.from(getOrThrow(id));
    }

    @Transactional
    public CategoryResponse create(CategoryRequest req) {
        String name = req.name().trim();
        if (categoryRepository.existsByNameIgnoreCase(name)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "Danh má»¥c '%s' Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i".formatted(name));
        }
        Category category = Category.builder()
                .name(name)
                .description(req.description())
                .build();
        return CategoryResponse.from(categoryRepository.save(category));
    }

    @Transactional
    public CategoryResponse update(Long id, CategoryRequest req) {
        Category category = getOrThrow(id);
        String name = req.name().trim();
        categoryRepository.findByNameIgnoreCase(name)
                .filter(other -> !other.getId().equals(id))
                .ifPresent(other -> {
                    throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "Danh má»¥c '%s' Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i".formatted(name));
                });
        category.setName(name);
        category.setDescription(req.description());
        return CategoryResponse.from(category);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Category category = getOrThrow(id);
        // Náº¿u cÃ²n mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ thuá»™c danh má»¥c, FK á»Ÿ DB sáº½ bÃ¡o lá»—i -> GlobalExceptionHandler tráº£ 409.
        categoryRepository.delete(category);
        categoryRepository.flush();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public Category getOrThrow(Long id) {
        return categoryRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("danh má»¥c", id));
    }

    private Pageable pageable(int page, int size) {
        int safePage = Math.max(page, 0);
        int safeSize = Math.min(Math.max(size, 1), MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        Sort sort = Sort.by(Sort.Direction.ASC, "name", "id");
        return PageRequest.of(safePage, safeSize, sort);
    }
}

===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/CustomUserDetailsService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetails;
import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UserDetailsService;
import org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.UsernameNotFoundException;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class CustomUserDetailsService implements UserDetailsService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    @Override
    public UserDetails loadUserByUsername(String email) throws UsernameNotFoundException {
        User user = userRepository.findByEmail(email)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new UsernameNotFoundException("KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y tÃ i khoáº£n: " + email));

        return org.springframework.security.core.userdetails.User
                .withUsername(user.getEmail())
                .password(user.getPassword())
                .roles(user.getRoles().toArray(String[]::new)) // USER -> ROLE_USER
                .disabled(!user.isEnabled())
                .build();
    }
}
===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/EmailService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.mail.MailException;
import org.springframework.mail.SimpleMailMessage;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.JavaMailSender;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class EmailService {

    private final ObjectProvider<JavaMailSender> mailSenderProvider;

    @Value("${app.mail.enabled:false}")
    private boolean mailEnabled;

    @Value("${app.mail.from:no-reply@unilend.local}")
    private String fromAddress;

    @Value("${app.frontend.url:http://localhost:3000}")
    private String frontendUrl;

    public void sendVerificationEmail(String toEmail, String fullName, String rawToken) {
        String verifyLink = frontendUrl + "/verify-email?token=" + rawToken;
        String subject = "[Uni-Lend] XÃ¡c minh Ä‘á»‹a chá»‰ email cá»§a báº¡n";
        String body = """
                Xin chÃ o %s,
                
                Cáº£m Æ¡n báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½ tÃ i khoáº£n Uni-Lend.
                Vui lÃ²ng truy cáº­p Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n dÆ°á»›i Ä‘Ã¢y Ä‘á»ƒ xÃ¡c minh email cá»§a báº¡n (cÃ³ hiá»‡u lá»±c trong 24 giá»):
                %s
                
                Náº¿u báº¡n khÃ´ng thá»±c hiá»‡n yÃªu cáº§u nÃ y, vui lÃ²ng bá» qua email nÃ y.
                """.formatted(fullName, verifyLink);

        sendEmail(toEmail, subject, body, "VERIFY_EMAIL");
    }

    public void sendPasswordResetEmail(String toEmail, String fullName, String rawToken) {
        String resetLink = frontendUrl + "/reset-password?token=" + rawToken;
        String subject = "[Uni-Lend] YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘áº·t láº¡i máº­t kháº©u";
        String body = """
                Xin chÃ o %s,
                
                ChÃºng tÃ´i nháº­n Ä‘Æ°á»£c yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘áº·t láº¡i máº­t kháº©u cho tÃ i khoáº£n Uni-Lend cá»§a báº¡n.
                Vui lÃ²ng truy cáº­p Ä‘Æ°á»ng dáº«n dÆ°á»›i Ä‘Ã¢y Ä‘á»ƒ Ä‘áº·t láº¡i máº­t kháº©u (cÃ³ hiá»‡u lá»±c trong 15 phÃºt vÃ  chá»‰ dÃ¹ng 1 láº§n):
                %s
                
                Náº¿u báº¡n khÃ´ng yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘áº·t láº¡i máº­t kháº©u, vui lÃ²ng bá» qua email nÃ y.
                """.formatted(fullName, resetLink);

        sendEmail(toEmail, subject, body, "RESET_PASSWORD");
    }

    private void sendEmail(String toEmail, String subject, String text, String type) {
        if (!mailEnabled) {
            log.info("[MAIL-DISABLED] type={}", type);
            return;
        }

        JavaMailSender mailSender = mailSenderProvider.getIfAvailable();
        if (mailSender == null) {
            log.error("[MAIL-ERROR] JavaMailSender is not configured. type={}", type);
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "Dá»‹ch vá»¥ gá»­i email hiá»‡n khÃ´ng kháº£ dá»¥ng");
        }

        try {
            SimpleMailMessage message = new SimpleMailMessage();
            message.setFrom(fromAddress);
            message.setTo(toEmail);
            message.setSubject(subject);
            message.setText(text);
            mailSender.send(message);
            log.info("[MAIL-SENT] type={}", type);
        } catch (MailException ex) {
            log.error("[MAIL-ERROR] KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ gá»­i email type={}. causeType={}",
                    type, ex.getClass().getSimpleName());
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ gá»­i email lÃºc nÃ y");
        }
    }
}
===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/ImageUploadValidator.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import javax.imageio.ImageReader;
import javax.imageio.stream.MemoryCacheImageInputStream;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.io.IOException;
import java.io.InputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Arrays;
import java.util.Iterator;
import java.util.Locale;

/** Decode and re-encode images; never store client metadata or appended payloads. */
@Component
public class ImageUploadValidator {
    static final int MAX_FILE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024;
    private static final int MAX_DIMENSION = 8192;
    private static final long MAX_PIXELS = 16_000_000L;
    private static final byte[] PNG_SIGNATURE = {(byte) 137, 80, 78, 71, 13, 10, 26, 10};

    public record ValidatedImage(byte[] bytes, String extension) { }

    public ValidatedImage validate(MultipartFile file) {
        if (file == null || file.isEmpty()) throw invalid("File áº£nh khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c rá»—ng");
        if (file.getSize() > MAX_FILE_BYTES) throw invalid("KÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c má»—i áº£nh khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 5MB");
        try (InputStream input = file.getInputStream()) {
            byte[] bytes = input.readNBytes(MAX_FILE_BYTES + 1);
            if (bytes.length > MAX_FILE_BYTES) throw invalid("KÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c má»—i áº£nh khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 5MB");
            String expected = signatureFormat(bytes);
            try (var imageInput = new MemoryCacheImageInputStream(new ByteArrayInputStream(bytes))) {
                Iterator<ImageReader> readers = ImageIO.getImageReaders(imageInput);
                if (!readers.hasNext()) throw invalid("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ giáº£i mÃ£ ná»™i dung áº£nh");
                ImageReader reader = readers.next();
                try {
                    if (!reader.getFormatName().toLowerCase(Locale.ROOT).equals(expected)) {
                        throw invalid("Chá»¯ kÃ½ áº£nh khÃ´ng khá»›p ná»™i dung");
                    }
                    reader.setInput(imageInput, true, true);
                    reader.addIIOReadWarningListener((source, warning) -> {
                        throw invalid("áº¢nh bá»‹ lá»—i hoáº·c thiáº¿u dá»¯ liá»‡u");
                    });
                    int width = reader.getWidth(0), height = reader.getHeight(0);
                    if (width <= 0 || height <= 0 || width > MAX_DIMENSION || height > MAX_DIMENSION
                            || (long) width * height > MAX_PIXELS) {
                        throw invalid("áº¢nh tá»‘i Ä‘a 16 triá»‡u Ä‘iá»ƒm áº£nh vÃ  8192 pixel má»—i chiá»u");
                    }
                    BufferedImage decoded = reader.read(0);
                    if (decoded == null) throw invalid("Ná»™i dung áº£nh khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡");
                    // Normalize WebP to static PNG; remove metadata and trailing payloads.
                    String format = expected.equals("jpeg") ? "jpeg" : "png";
                    try (ByteArrayOutputStream output = new ByteArrayOutputStream()) {
                        if (!ImageIO.write(decoded, format, output)) throw invalid("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ chuáº©n hoÃ¡ áº£nh");
                        if (output.size() > MAX_FILE_BYTES) {
                            throw invalid("áº¢nh sau chuáº©n hoÃ¡ vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 5MB, vui lÃ²ng giáº£m kÃ­ch thÆ°á»›c áº£nh");
                        }
                        return new ValidatedImage(output.toByteArray(), format.equals("jpeg") ? ".jpg" : ".png");
                    } finally {
                        decoded.flush();
                    }
                } finally {
                    reader.dispose();
                }
            }
        } catch (BusinessException ex) {
            throw ex;
        } catch (IOException | RuntimeException ex) {
            throw invalid("File khÃ´ng pháº£i áº£nh JPG, PNG, WEBP há»£p lá»‡ hoáº·c Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»ng");
        }
    }

    private String signatureFormat(byte[] bytes) {
        if (bytes.length >= 8 && Arrays.equals(Arrays.copyOf(bytes, 8), PNG_SIGNATURE)) return "png";
        if (bytes.length >= 3 && (bytes[0] & 255) == 255 && (bytes[1] & 255) == 216
                && (bytes[2] & 255) == 255) return "jpeg";
        if (bytes.length >= 12 && new String(bytes, 0, 4, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII).equals("RIFF")
                && new String(bytes, 8, 4, StandardCharsets.US_ASCII).equals("WEBP")) return "webp";
        throw invalid("Chá»‰ cháº¥p nháº­n ná»™i dung áº£nh JPG, PNG hoáº·c WEBP");
    }

    private BusinessException invalid(String message) {
        return new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, message);
    }
}

===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/ItemService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.item.ItemRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.item.ItemResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Category;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Item;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Item.ItemStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.ItemImage;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.CategoryRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ItemImageRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ItemRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.LoanRequestRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;
import org.springframework.web.multipart.MultipartFile;

import java.io.IOException;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.nio.file.Paths;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.UUID;

@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ItemService {

    private static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;
    private static final int MAX_IMAGES_PER_ITEM = 5;
    private final ItemRepository itemRepository;
    private final ItemImageRepository itemImageRepository;
    private final CategoryRepository categoryRepository;
    private final LoanRequestRepository loanRequestRepository;
    private final CurrentUserService currentUserService;
    private final ImageUploadValidator imageUploadValidator;

    @Value("${app.upload.dir:uploads}")
    private String uploadDir;

    @Transactional
    public ItemResponse create(ItemRequest req) {
        User owner = currentUserService.getCurrentUser();
        Item item = Item.builder()
                .title(req.title().trim())
                .description(req.description())
                .owner(owner)
                .category(resolveCategory(req.categoryId()))
                .status(ItemStatus.AVAILABLE)
                .build();
        return ItemResponse.from(itemRepository.save(item));
    }

    @Transactional
    public ItemResponse update(Long id, ItemRequest req) {
        Item item = getWithDetailsOrThrow(id);
        assertOwner(item);

        item.setTitle(req.title().trim());
        item.setDescription(req.description());
        item.setCategory(resolveCategory(req.categoryId()));

        if (req.status() != null && req.status() != item.getStatus()) {
            if (req.status() == ItemStatus.LENT_OUT) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                        "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ tá»± Ä‘áº·t tráº¡ng thÃ¡i LENT_OUT, tráº¡ng thÃ¡i nÃ y do luá»“ng mÆ°á»£n/tráº£ quáº£n lÃ½");
            }
            if (item.getStatus() == ItemStatus.LENT_OUT) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                        "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c cho mÆ°á»£n, khÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»•i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i");
            }
            item.setStatus(req.status());
        }
        return ItemResponse.from(item);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void delete(Long id) {
        Item item = getWithDetailsOrThrow(id);
        assertOwner(item);
        if (loanRequestRepository.findByItemId(id, PageRequest.of(0, 1)).hasContent()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT,
                    "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n, khÃ´ng thá»ƒ xoÃ¡. HÃ£y chuyá»ƒn sang UNAVAILABLE");
        }
        List<String> imageUrls = item.getImages().stream().map(ItemImage::getImageUrl).toList();
        itemRepository.delete(item);
        imageUrls.forEach(this::deletePhysicalFileQuietly);
    }

    @Transactional
    public ItemResponse uploadImages(Long itemId, List<MultipartFile> files) {
        // Serialize uploads of the same item before counting its existing images.
        Item item = itemRepository.findByIdForImageUpload(itemId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("mÃ³n Ä‘á»“", itemId));
        assertOwner(item);

        if (files == null || files.isEmpty()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Vui lÃ²ng chá»n Ã­t nháº¥t 1 file áº£nh");
        }
        long currentCount = itemImageRepository.countByItemId(itemId);
        if (files.size() > MAX_IMAGES_PER_ITEM || currentCount + files.size() > MAX_IMAGES_PER_ITEM) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Má»—i mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c tá»‘i Ä‘a %d áº£nh (hiá»‡n cÃ³ %d áº£nh)".formatted(MAX_IMAGES_PER_ITEM, currentCount));
        }

        // Validate the whole batch before writing any file; never trust client MIME or suffix.
        List<ImageUploadValidator.ValidatedImage> validated = files.stream()
                .map(imageUploadValidator::validate).toList();
        List<String> createdUrls = new ArrayList<>();
        if (!TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
            throw new IllegalStateException("Upload áº£nh pháº£i cháº¡y trong giao dá»‹ch");
        }
        TransactionSynchronizationManager.registerSynchronization(new TransactionSynchronization() {
            @Override
            public void afterCompletion(int status) {
                if (status != STATUS_COMMITTED) {
                    createdUrls.forEach(ItemService.this::deletePhysicalFileQuietly);
                }
            }
        });
        try {
            for (ImageUploadValidator.ValidatedImage image : validated) {
                String imageUrl = storeFile(itemId, image);
                createdUrls.add(imageUrl);
                ItemImage itemImage = ItemImage.builder().item(item).imageUrl(imageUrl).build();
                item.getImages().add(itemImageRepository.save(itemImage));
            }
            // Surface constraint failures now; afterCompletion also covers failures at commit.
            itemImageRepository.flush();
            return ItemResponse.from(item);
        } catch (RuntimeException ex) {
            createdUrls.forEach(this::deletePhysicalFileQuietly);
            throw ex;
        }
    }

    @Transactional
    public ItemResponse deleteImage(Long itemId, Long imageId) {
        Item item = getWithDetailsOrThrow(itemId);
        assertOwner(item);

        ItemImage image = itemImageRepository.findByIdAndItemId(imageId, itemId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("áº£nh mÃ³n Ä‘á»“", imageId));

        String url = image.getImageUrl();
        item.getImages().remove(image);
        itemImageRepository.delete(image);
        deletePhysicalFileQuietly(url);

        return ItemResponse.from(item);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ItemResponse findById(Long id) {
        return ItemResponse.from(getWithDetailsOrThrow(id));
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<ItemResponse> search(String keyword, Long categoryId, ItemStatus status,
                                             int page, int size) {
        String kw = (keyword == null || keyword.isBlank()) ? null : keyword.trim();
        return PageResponse.from(
                itemRepository.search(kw, categoryId, status, pageable(page, size)),
                ItemResponse::from);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<ItemResponse> findMine(int page, int size) {
        User me = currentUserService.getCurrentUser();
        return PageResponse.from(
                itemRepository.findByOwnerId(me.getId(), pageable(page, size)),
                ItemResponse::from);
    }

    // ---- helpers ----

    private String storeFile(Long itemId, ImageUploadValidator.ValidatedImage image) {
        String filename = UUID.randomUUID() + image.extension();
        Path itemDir = Paths.get(uploadDir, "items", String.valueOf(itemId)).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
        Path target = itemDir.resolve(filename);
        try {
            Files.createDirectories(itemDir);
            Files.write(target, image.bytes(), java.nio.file.StandardOpenOption.CREATE_NEW);
            return "/uploads/items/" + itemId + "/" + filename;
        } catch (IOException e) {
            try {
                Files.deleteIfExists(target);
            } catch (IOException cleanupFailure) {
                e.addSuppressed(cleanupFailure);
            }
            log.error("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ lÆ°u file áº£nh cho item {}", itemId, e);
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.INTERNAL_ERROR, "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ lÆ°u file áº£nh");
        }
    }

    private void deletePhysicalFileQuietly(String imageUrl) {
        if (imageUrl == null || !imageUrl.startsWith("/uploads/")) {
            return;
        }
        String relativePath = imageUrl.substring("/uploads/".length());
        Path root = Paths.get(uploadDir).toAbsolutePath().normalize();
        Path filePath = root.resolve(relativePath).normalize();
        if (!filePath.startsWith(root)) {
            log.warn("Tá»« chá»‘i xoÃ¡ file ngoÃ i thÆ° má»¥c upload");
            return;
        }
        try {
            Files.deleteIfExists(filePath);
        } catch (IOException e) {
            log.warn("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xoÃ¡ file váº­t lÃ½ {}: {}", filePath, e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    private Pageable pageable(int page, int size) {
        int safePage = Math.max(page, 0);
        int safeSize = Math.min(Math.max(size, 1), MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        return PageRequest.of(safePage, safeSize, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "createdAt", "id"));
    }

    private Item getWithDetailsOrThrow(Long id) {
        return itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("mÃ³n Ä‘á»“", id));
    }

    private Category resolveCategory(Long categoryId) {
        if (categoryId == null) {
            return null;
        }
        return categoryRepository.findById(categoryId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("danh má»¥c", categoryId));
    }

    private void assertOwner(Item item) {
        User me = currentUserService.getCurrentUser();
        if (!item.getOwner().getId().equals(me.getId())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "Chá»‰ chá»§ Ä‘á»“ má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c thá»±c hiá»‡n thao tÃ¡c nÃ y");
        }
    }
}

===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/JwtService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import io.jsonwebtoken.Claims;
import io.jsonwebtoken.Jwts;
import io.jsonwebtoken.io.Decoders;
import io.jsonwebtoken.security.Keys;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import javax.crypto.SecretKey;
import java.util.Date;
import java.util.List;

@Service
public class JwtService {

    private final SecretKey key;
    private final long expirationMs;

    public JwtService(@Value("${app.jwt.secret}") String base64Secret,
                      @Value("${app.jwt.expiration-ms}") long expirationMs) {
        this.key = Keys.hmacShaKeyFor(Decoders.BASE64.decode(base64Secret));
        this.expirationMs = expirationMs;
    }

    public String generateToken(Long userId, String email) {
        return generateToken(userId, email, List.of("USER"), 0);
    }

    public String generateToken(Long userId, String email, List<String> roles) {
        return generateToken(userId, email, roles, 0);
    }

    public String generateToken(Long userId, String email, List<String> roles, int tokenVersion) {
        Date now = new Date();
        return Jwts.builder()
                .setSubject(email)
                .claim("uid", userId)
                .claim("roles", roles)
                .claim("tv", tokenVersion)
                .setIssuedAt(now)
                .setExpiration(new Date(now.getTime() + expirationMs))
                .signWith(key)
                .compact();
    }

    public String extractEmail(String token) {
        return parseClaims(token).getSubject();
    }

    public int extractTokenVersion(String token) {
        Number tv = parseClaims(token).get("tv", Number.class);
        return tv == null ? 0 : tv.intValue();
    }

    private Claims parseClaims(String token) {
        return Jwts.parserBuilder().setSigningKey(key).build()
                .parseClaimsJws(token).getBody();
    }
}
===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/LoanRequestService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.loan.BookedPeriodResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.loan.LoanRequestCreateRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.loan.LoanRequestResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Item;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Item.ItemStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest.LoanStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Notification.NotificationType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ItemRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.LoanRequestRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ReviewRepository;
import jakarta.persistence.EntityManager;
import jakarta.persistence.LockModeType;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Set;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class LoanRequestService {

    private static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;
    public static final ZoneId LOAN_ZONE = ZoneId.of("Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh");

    private final LoanRequestRepository loanRequestRepository;
    private final ItemRepository itemRepository;
    private final ReviewRepository reviewRepository;
    private final EntityManager entityManager;
    private final NotificationService notificationService;

    @Transactional
    public LoanRequestResponse create(User borrower, LoanRequestCreateRequest req) {
        validateLoanDates(req.borrowFrom(), req.dueDate());

        Item lockedItem = entityManager.find(Item.class, req.itemId(), LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE);
        if (lockedItem == null) {
            throw new ResourceNotFoundException("Item", req.itemId());
        }

        Item item = itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(req.itemId())
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("Item", req.itemId()));

        if (item.getOwner().getId().equals(borrower.getId())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Báº¡n khÃ´ng thá»ƒ mÆ°á»£n Ä‘á»“ cá»§a chÃ­nh mÃ¬nh");
        }
        if (item.getStatus() == ItemStatus.UNAVAILABLE) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ hiá»‡n khÃ´ng sáºµn sÃ ng Ä‘á»ƒ mÆ°á»£n");
        }
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now(LOAN_ZONE);
        if (item.getStatus() == ItemStatus.LENT_OUT && !req.borrowFrom().isAfter(today)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ hiá»‡n khÃ´ng sáºµn sÃ ng Ä‘á»ƒ mÆ°á»£n");
        }
        if (loanRequestRepository.existsByItemIdAndBorrowerIdAndStatus(
                item.getId(), borrower.getId(), LoanStatus.PENDING)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "Báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘ang chá» duyá»‡t cho mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ nÃ y");
        }
        if (loanRequestRepository.existsOverlappingApproved(item.getId(), req.borrowFrom(), req.dueDate())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ ngÆ°á»i mÆ°á»£n trong khoáº£ng thá»i gian nÃ y");
        }

        LoanRequest loan = LoanRequest.builder()
                .item(item)
                .borrower(borrower)
                .borrowFrom(req.borrowFrom())
                .dueDate(req.dueDate())
                .note(req.note())
                .build();

        LoanRequest saved = loanRequestRepository.save(loan);
        notificationService.notify(
                item.getOwner().getId(),
                NotificationType.LOAN_CREATED,
                "%s Ä‘Ã£ gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ \"%s\"".formatted(borrower.getFullName(), item.getTitle()),
                "/loans/" + saved.getId()
        );
        return LoanRequestResponse.from(saved);
    }

    @Transactional
    public LoanRequestResponse approve(Long loanId, User actor) {
        LoanRequest peekLoan = findLoan(loanId);
        requireOwner(peekLoan, actor);

        // KhÃ³a theo thá»© tá»± Item -> LoanRequest Ä‘á»ƒ chá»‘ng deadlock vÃ  tuáº§n tá»± hÃ³a má»i thao tÃ¡c duyá»‡t trÃªn cÃ¹ng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“
        Item item = peekLoan.getItem();
        lockAndRefresh(item);

        LoanRequest loan = findLoanForUpdate(loanId);
        requireOwner(loan, actor);
        requirePending(loan);

        if (item.getStatus() == ItemStatus.UNAVAILABLE) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ hiá»‡n khÃ´ng sáºµn sÃ ng Ä‘á»ƒ cho mÆ°á»£n");
        }
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now(LOAN_ZONE);
        if (item.getStatus() == ItemStatus.LENT_OUT && !loan.getBorrowFrom().isAfter(today)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ hiá»‡n khÃ´ng sáºµn sÃ ng Ä‘á»ƒ cho mÆ°á»£n");
        }
        if (loanRequestRepository.existsOverlappingApproved(item.getId(), loan.getBorrowFrom(), loan.getDueDate())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ä‘Ã£ cÃ³ ngÆ°á»i mÆ°á»£n trong khoáº£ng thá»i gian nÃ y");
        }

        Instant respondedAt = Instant.now();
        loan.setStatus(LoanStatus.APPROVED);
        loan.setRespondedAt(respondedAt);
        if (isPeriodActive(loan.getBorrowFrom(), loan.getDueDate(), today)) {
            item.setStatus(ItemStatus.LENT_OUT);
        }

        // Tá»± Ä‘á»™ng tá»« chá»‘i cÃ¡c yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘ang chá» duyá»‡t (PENDING) bá»‹ trÃ¹ng lá»‹ch
        List<LoanRequest> conflictingPendings = loanRequestRepository.findOverlappingPendingForUpdate(
                item.getId(), loan.getId(), loan.getBorrowFrom(), loan.getDueDate());
        if (conflictingPendings != null) {
            for (LoanRequest pending : conflictingPendings) {
                pending.setStatus(LoanStatus.REJECTED);
                pending.setRespondedAt(respondedAt);
                notificationService.notify(
                        pending.getBorrower().getId(),
                        NotificationType.LOAN_REJECTED,
                        "YÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ \"%s\" cá»§a báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ tá»« chá»‘i do trÃ¹ng lá»‹ch vá»›i lÆ°á»£t mÆ°á»£n khÃ¡c"
                                .formatted(item.getTitle()),
                        "/loans/" + pending.getId()
                );
            }
        }

        notificationService.notify(
                loan.getBorrower().getId(),
                NotificationType.LOAN_APPROVED,
                "YÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ \"%s\" cá»§a báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c duyá»‡t".formatted(item.getTitle()),
                "/loans/" + loan.getId()
        );
        return LoanRequestResponse.from(loan);
    }

    @Transactional
    public LoanRequestResponse reject(Long loanId, User actor) {
        LoanRequest loan = findLoanForUpdate(loanId);
        requireOwner(loan, actor);
        requirePending(loan);

        loan.setStatus(LoanStatus.REJECTED);
        loan.setRespondedAt(Instant.now());

        notificationService.notify(
                loan.getBorrower().getId(),
                NotificationType.LOAN_REJECTED,
                "YÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ \"%s\" cá»§a báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ tá»« chá»‘i".formatted(loan.getItem().getTitle()),
                "/loans/" + loan.getId()
        );
        return LoanRequestResponse.from(loan);
    }

    @Transactional
    public LoanRequestResponse cancel(Long loanId, User actor) {
        LoanRequest loan = findLoanForUpdate(loanId);
        requireBorrower(loan, actor);
        requirePending(loan);

        loan.setStatus(LoanStatus.CANCELLED);

        notificationService.notify(
                loan.getItem().getOwner().getId(),
                NotificationType.LOAN_CANCELLED,
                "%s Ä‘Ã£ há»§y yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ \"%s\"".formatted(loan.getBorrower().getFullName(), loan.getItem().getTitle()),
                "/loans/" + loan.getId()
        );
        return LoanRequestResponse.from(loan);
    }

    @Transactional
    public LoanRequestResponse markReturned(Long loanId, User actor) {
        LoanRequest loan = findLoanForUpdate(loanId);
        requireOwner(loan, actor);
        if (loan.getStatus() != LoanStatus.APPROVED && loan.getStatus() != LoanStatus.OVERDUE) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "Chá»‰ xÃ¡c nháº­n tráº£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c vá»›i khoáº£n mÆ°á»£n Ä‘Ã£ duyá»‡t");
        }

        Item item = loan.getItem();
        lockAndRefresh(item);

        loan.setStatus(LoanStatus.RETURNED);
        loan.setReturnedAt(Instant.now());
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now(LOAN_ZONE);
        if (item.getStatus() == ItemStatus.LENT_OUT
                && !loanRequestRepository.existsActiveLoanOnDate(item.getId(), today)) {
            item.setStatus(ItemStatus.AVAILABLE);
        }

        notificationService.notify(
                loan.getBorrower().getId(),
                NotificationType.LOAN_RETURNED,
                "Chá»§ Ä‘á»“ Ä‘Ã£ xÃ¡c nháº­n báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ tráº£ mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ \"%s\"".formatted(item.getTitle()),
                "/loans/" + loan.getId()
        );
        return toResponse(loan, actor);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<LoanRequestResponse> getMyRequests(User borrower, LoanStatus status, Pageable pageable) {
        Pageable boundedPageable = boundedPageable(pageable);
        Page<LoanRequest> page = (status == null)
                ? loanRequestRepository.findByBorrowerId(borrower.getId(), boundedPageable)
                : loanRequestRepository.findByBorrowerIdAndStatus(borrower.getId(), status, boundedPageable);
        return toPageResponse(page, borrower);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<LoanRequestResponse> getIncomingRequests(User owner, LoanStatus status, Pageable pageable) {
        Pageable boundedPageable = boundedPageable(pageable);
        Page<LoanRequest> page = (status == null)
                ? loanRequestRepository.findByItemOwnerId(owner.getId(), boundedPageable)
                : loanRequestRepository.findByItemOwnerIdAndStatus(owner.getId(), status, boundedPageable);
        return toPageResponse(page, owner);
    }

    // Khoáº£ng ngÃ y lá»c theo requestedAt, gá»“m cáº£ ngÃ y káº¿t thÃºc táº¡i mÃºi giá» Viá»‡t Nam.
    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<LoanRequestResponse> getMyHistory(
            User borrower, LoanStatus status, LocalDate fromDate, LocalDate toDate, Pageable pageable) {
        validateHistoryRange(fromDate, toDate);
        Page<LoanRequest> page = loanRequestRepository.findBorrowerHistory(
                borrower.getId(), status, historyStart(fromDate), historyEndExclusive(toDate),
                boundedPageable(pageable));
        return toPageResponse(page, borrower);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<LoanRequestResponse> getIncomingHistory(
            User owner, LoanStatus status, LocalDate fromDate, LocalDate toDate, Pageable pageable) {
        validateHistoryRange(fromDate, toDate);
        Page<LoanRequest> page = loanRequestRepository.findOwnerHistory(
                owner.getId(), status, historyStart(fromDate), historyEndExclusive(toDate),
                boundedPageable(pageable));
        return toPageResponse(page, owner);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<BookedPeriodResponse> getBookedPeriods(Long itemId) {
        if (!itemRepository.existsById(itemId)) {
            throw new ResourceNotFoundException("Item", itemId);
        }
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now(LOAN_ZONE);
        return loanRequestRepository.findActiveOrUpcomingApprovedByItemId(itemId, today).stream()
                .map(BookedPeriodResponse::from)
                .toList();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<LoanRequestResponse> getApprovedScheduleForItem(
            Long itemId, User actor, LocalDate fromDate, LocalDate toDate) {
        validateHistoryRange(fromDate, toDate);
        Item item = itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(itemId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("Item", itemId));
        if (!item.getOwner().getId().equals(actor.getId()) && !isAdmin(actor)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "Chá»‰ chá»§ mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c xem lá»‹ch mÆ°á»£n chi tiáº¿t");
        }
        List<LoanRequest> loans = loanRequestRepository.findApprovedScheduleByItemId(itemId, fromDate, toDate);
        return toResponseList(loans, actor);
    }

    private boolean isPeriodActive(LocalDate borrowFrom, LocalDate dueDate, LocalDate today) {
        return borrowFrom != null && dueDate != null
                && !borrowFrom.isAfter(today) && !dueDate.isBefore(today);
    }

    private void validateLoanDates(LocalDate borrowFrom, LocalDate dueDate) {
        if (borrowFrom == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "NgÃ y báº¯t Ä‘áº§u mÆ°á»£n khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng");
        }
        if (dueDate == null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Háº¡n tráº£ khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘á»ƒ trá»‘ng");
        }
        LocalDate today = LocalDate.now(LOAN_ZONE);
        if (borrowFrom.isBefore(today)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "NgÃ y báº¯t Ä‘áº§u mÆ°á»£n khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c á»Ÿ quÃ¡ khá»©");
        }
        if (dueDate.isBefore(borrowFrom)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Háº¡n tráº£ pháº£i sau hoáº·c báº±ng ngÃ y báº¯t Ä‘áº§u mÆ°á»£n");
        }
    }

    private void validateHistoryRange(LocalDate fromDate, LocalDate toDate) {
        if (fromDate != null && toDate != null && fromDate.isAfter(toDate)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "fromDate khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c sau toDate");
        }
    }

    private Instant historyStart(LocalDate fromDate) {
        return fromDate == null ? null : fromDate.atStartOfDay(LOAN_ZONE).toInstant();
    }

    private Instant historyEndExclusive(LocalDate toDate) {
        return toDate == null ? null : toDate.plusDays(1).atStartOfDay(LOAN_ZONE).toInstant();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public LoanRequestResponse getById(Long loanId, User actor) {
        LoanRequest loan = findLoan(loanId);
        boolean isBorrower = loan.getBorrower().getId().equals(actor.getId());
        boolean isOwner = loan.getItem().getOwner().getId().equals(actor.getId());
        if (!isBorrower && !isOwner && !isAdmin(actor)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN);
        }
        return toResponse(loan, actor);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<LoanRequestResponse> getOverdue(User actor, Pageable pageable) {
        if (!isAdmin(actor)) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN);
        }
        return toPageResponse(
                loanRequestRepository.findOverdue(LocalDate.now(LOAN_ZONE), boundedPageable(pageable)),
                actor);
    }

    private LoanRequestResponse toResponse(LoanRequest loan, User actor) {
        boolean isParticipant = actor != null && (
                loan.getBorrower().getId().equals(actor.getId())
                        || loan.getItem().getOwner().getId().equals(actor.getId())
        );
        if (!isParticipant || loan.getStatus() != LoanStatus.RETURNED) {
            return LoanRequestResponse.from(loan, false, false);
        }
        boolean hasReviewed = loan.getId() != null
                && reviewRepository.existsByLoanIdAndReviewerId(loan.getId(), actor.getId());
        return LoanRequestResponse.from(loan, hasReviewed, !hasReviewed);
    }

    private PageResponse<LoanRequestResponse> toPageResponse(Page<LoanRequest> page, User actor) {
        Set<Long> reviewedLoanIds = resolveReviewedLoanIds(page.getContent(), actor);
        return PageResponse.from(page, loan -> mapWithReviewFlags(loan, actor, reviewedLoanIds));
    }

    private List<LoanRequestResponse> toResponseList(List<LoanRequest> loans, User actor) {
        Set<Long> reviewedLoanIds = resolveReviewedLoanIds(loans, actor);
        return loans.stream()
                .map(loan -> mapWithReviewFlags(loan, actor, reviewedLoanIds))
                .toList();
    }

    private Set<Long> resolveReviewedLoanIds(List<LoanRequest> loans, User actor) {
        if (actor == null || loans == null || loans.isEmpty()) {
            return Collections.emptySet();
        }
        List<Long> returnedIds = loans.stream()
                .filter(l -> l.getStatus() == LoanStatus.RETURNED && l.getId() != null)
                .map(LoanRequest::getId)
                .toList();
        if (returnedIds.isEmpty()) {
            return Collections.emptySet();
        }
        Set<Long> reviewed = reviewRepository.findReviewedLoanIdsByReviewerIdAndLoanIds(actor.getId(), returnedIds);
        return reviewed == null ? Collections.emptySet() : reviewed;
    }

    private LoanRequestResponse mapWithReviewFlags(LoanRequest loan, User actor, Set<Long> reviewedLoanIds) {
        boolean isParticipant = actor != null && (
                loan.getBorrower().getId().equals(actor.getId())
                        || loan.getItem().getOwner().getId().equals(actor.getId())
        );
        if (!isParticipant || loan.getStatus() != LoanStatus.RETURNED) {
            return LoanRequestResponse.from(loan, false, false);
        }
        boolean hasReviewed = loan.getId() != null && reviewedLoanIds.contains(loan.getId());
        return LoanRequestResponse.from(loan, hasReviewed, !hasReviewed);
    }

    private Pageable boundedPageable(Pageable pageable) {
        if (!pageable.isPaged()) {
            return PageRequest.of(0, MAX_PAGE_SIZE, pageable.getSort());
        }
        int page = Math.max(pageable.getPageNumber(), 0);
        int size = Math.min(Math.max(pageable.getPageSize(), 1), MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        return PageRequest.of(page, size, pageable.getSort());
    }

    private LoanRequest findLoan(Long loanId) {
        return loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsById(loanId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("LoanRequest", loanId));
    }

    private LoanRequest findLoanForUpdate(Long loanId) {
        return loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(loanId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("LoanRequest", loanId));
    }

    private void requireOwner(LoanRequest loan, User actor) {
        if (!loan.getItem().getOwner().getId().equals(actor.getId())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "Chá»‰ chá»§ mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c thá»±c hiá»‡n thao tÃ¡c nÃ y");
        }
    }

    private void requireBorrower(LoanRequest loan, User actor) {
        if (!loan.getBorrower().getId().equals(actor.getId())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "Chá»‰ ngÆ°á»i gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c thá»±c hiá»‡n thao tÃ¡c nÃ y");
        }
    }

    private void requirePending(LoanRequest loan) {
        if (loan.getStatus() != LoanStatus.PENDING) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, "Chá»‰ xá»­ lÃ½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c yÃªu cáº§u Ä‘ang á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i chá» duyá»‡t");
        }
    }

    private boolean isAdmin(User user) {
        return user.getRoles() != null && user.getRoles().contains("ADMIN");
    }

    private void lockAndRefresh(Item item) {
        entityManager.lock(item, LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE);
        entityManager.refresh(item);
    }
}

===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/NotificationService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.notification.NotificationResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Notification;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Notification.NotificationType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.NotificationRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class NotificationService {

    private static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;

    private final NotificationRepository notificationRepository;
    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    @Transactional
    public NotificationResponse notify(Long userId, NotificationType type, String message, String link) {
        return notify(userId, type != null ? type.name() : NotificationType.SYSTEM.name(), message, link);
    }

    @Transactional
    public NotificationResponse notify(Long userId, String type, String message, String link) {
        User recipient = userRepository.findById(userId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("User", userId));

        String resolvedType = (type == null || type.isBlank())
                ? NotificationType.SYSTEM.name()
                : type.trim();

        Notification notification = Notification.builder()
                .user(recipient)
                .type(resolvedType)
                .message(message)
                .link(link)
                .read(false)
                .build();

        return NotificationResponse.from(notificationRepository.save(notification));
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<NotificationResponse> getMyNotifications(User user, Pageable pageable) {
        Pageable bounded = boundedPageable(pageable);
        Page<Notification> page = notificationRepository.findByUserId(user.getId(), bounded);
        return PageResponse.from(page, NotificationResponse::from);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public long getUnreadCount(User user) {
        return notificationRepository.countByUserIdAndReadFalse(user.getId());
    }

    @Transactional
    public NotificationResponse markAsRead(Long notificationId, User user) {
        Notification notification = notificationRepository.findById(notificationId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("Notification", notificationId));

        if (!notification.getUser().getId().equals(user.getId())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n truy cáº­p thÃ´ng bÃ¡o nÃ y");
        }

        if (!notification.isRead()) {
            notification.setRead(true);
            notification.setReadAt(Instant.now());
            notification = notificationRepository.save(notification);
        }

        return NotificationResponse.from(notification);
    }

    @Transactional
    public int markAllAsRead(User user) {
        return notificationRepository.markAllAsRead(user.getId(), Instant.now());
    }

    private Pageable boundedPageable(Pageable pageable) {
        Sort defaultSort = Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "createdAt", "id");
        if (pageable == null || !pageable.isPaged()) {
            return PageRequest.of(0, 10, defaultSort);
        }
        int page = Math.max(pageable.getPageNumber(), 0);
        int size = Math.min(Math.max(pageable.getPageSize(), 1), MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        Sort sort = pageable.getSort().isSorted() ? pageable.getSort() : defaultSort;
        return PageRequest.of(page, size, sort);
    }
}

===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/RateLimitService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Value;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.ArrayDeque;
import java.util.Deque;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

@Service
public class RateLimitService {

    private static final long CLEANUP_INTERVAL = 256;

    private final Map<String, Bucket> buckets = new ConcurrentHashMap<>();
    private final AtomicLong checks = new AtomicLong();

    @Value("${app.rate-limit.login-max-attempts:5}")
    private int loginMaxAttempts;

    @Value("${app.rate-limit.login-window-seconds:60}")
    private long loginWindowSeconds;

    @Value("${app.rate-limit.email-max-attempts:3}")
    private int emailMaxAttempts;

    @Value("${app.rate-limit.email-window-seconds:900}")
    private long emailWindowSeconds;

    public void checkLoginRateLimit(String key) {
        check("login:" + normalizeKey(key), loginMaxAttempts, Duration.ofSeconds(loginWindowSeconds),
                "QuÃ¡ nhiá»u láº§n thá»­ Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i sau Ã­t phÃºt");
    }

    public void checkEmailRateLimit(String action, String key) {
        check("email:" + action + ":" + normalizeKey(key), emailMaxAttempts, Duration.ofSeconds(emailWindowSeconds),
                "Báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ yÃªu cáº§u gá»­i email quÃ¡ nhiá»u láº§n. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i sau");
    }

    public void check(String bucketKey, int maxAttempts, Duration window, String errorMessage) {
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        Instant cutoff = now.minus(window);

        buckets.compute(bucketKey, (key, bucket) -> {
            Bucket currentBucket = bucket == null ? new Bucket(window) : bucket;
            currentBucket.expireBefore(cutoff);
            if (currentBucket.timestamps.size() >= maxAttempts) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, errorMessage);
            }
            currentBucket.timestamps.addLast(now);
            return currentBucket;
        });

        if (checks.incrementAndGet() % CLEANUP_INTERVAL == 0) {
            removeExpiredBuckets(now);
        }
    }

    public void reset(String prefixAndKey) {
        buckets.remove(prefixAndKey);
    }

    private void removeExpiredBuckets(Instant now) {
        buckets.forEach((key, bucket) -> buckets.computeIfPresent(key, (ignored, current) -> {
            current.expireBefore(now.minus(current.window));
            return current.timestamps.isEmpty() ? null : current;
        }));
    }

    private String normalizeKey(String key) {
        return key == null ? "anonymous" : key.trim().toLowerCase();
    }

    private static final class Bucket {
        private final Duration window;
        private final Deque<Instant> timestamps = new ArrayDeque<>();

        private Bucket(Duration window) {
            this.window = window;
        }

        private void expireBefore(Instant cutoff) {
            while (!timestamps.isEmpty() && timestamps.peekFirst().isBefore(cutoff)) {
                timestamps.removeFirst();
            }
        }
    }
}
===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/ReportService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.report.ReportCreateRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.report.ReportResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.report.ReportUpdateStatusRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Item;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Report;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Report.ReportStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Report.ReportTargetType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ItemRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ReportRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.time.Instant;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ReportService {

    private static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;

    private final ReportRepository reportRepository;
    private final ItemRepository itemRepository;
    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final CurrentUserService currentUserService;

    @Transactional
    public ReportResponse create(ReportCreateRequest req) {
        User reporter = currentUserService.getCurrentUser();
        TargetResolution target = resolveTarget(req);

        if (target.targetType == ReportTargetType.ITEM) {
            Item item = itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(target.targetId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("mÃ³n Ä‘á»“", target.targetId));
            if (item.getOwner().getId().equals(reporter.getId())) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Báº¡n khÃ´ng thá»ƒ tá»± bÃ¡o cÃ¡o mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ cá»§a chÃ­nh mÃ¬nh");
            }
            Report report = Report.builder()
                    .reporter(reporter)
                    .targetType(ReportTargetType.ITEM)
                    .reportedItem(item)
                    .reportedUser(item.getOwner())
                    .reason(req.reason())
                    .description(req.description().trim())
                    .status(ReportStatus.OPEN)
                    .build();
            return ReportResponse.from(reportRepository.save(report));
        } else {
            if (target.targetId.equals(reporter.getId())) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Báº¡n khÃ´ng thá»ƒ tá»± bÃ¡o cÃ¡o chÃ­nh mÃ¬nh");
            }
            User reportedUser = userRepository.findById(target.targetId)
                    .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng", target.targetId));
            Report report = Report.builder()
                    .reporter(reporter)
                    .targetType(ReportTargetType.USER)
                    .reportedUser(reportedUser)
                    .reason(req.reason())
                    .description(req.description().trim())
                    .status(ReportStatus.OPEN)
                    .build();
            return ReportResponse.from(reportRepository.save(report));
        }
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<ReportResponse> findAllForAdmin(
            ReportStatus status, ReportTargetType targetType, int page, int size) {
        currentUserService.requireAdmin();
        Pageable pageable = pageable(page, size);
        Page<Report> result;
        if (status != null && targetType != null) {
            result = reportRepository.findByStatusAndTargetType(status, targetType, pageable);
        } else if (status != null) {
            result = reportRepository.findByStatus(status, pageable);
        } else if (targetType != null) {
            result = reportRepository.findByTargetType(targetType, pageable);
        } else {
            result = reportRepository.findAllBy(pageable);
        }
        return PageResponse.from(result, ReportResponse::from);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public ReportResponse getByIdForAdmin(Long id) {
        currentUserService.requireAdmin();
        Report report = reportRepository.findWithDetailsById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("bÃ¡o cÃ¡o", id));
        return ReportResponse.from(report);
    }

    @Transactional
    public ReportResponse updateStatus(Long id, ReportUpdateStatusRequest req) {
        User admin = currentUserService.requireAdmin();
        Report report = reportRepository.findWithDetailsById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("bÃ¡o cÃ¡o", id));

        report.setStatus(req.status());
        String note = (req.resolutionNote() == null || req.resolutionNote().isBlank())
                ? null
                : req.resolutionNote().trim();
        report.setResolutionNote(note);

        if (req.status() == ReportStatus.RESOLVED || req.status() == ReportStatus.DISMISSED) {
            report.setResolvedBy(admin);
            report.setResolvedAt(Instant.now());
        } else {
            report.setResolvedBy(null);
            report.setResolvedAt(null);
        }

        return ReportResponse.from(reportRepository.save(report));
    }

    private TargetResolution resolveTarget(ReportCreateRequest req) {
        if (req.itemId() != null && req.reportedUserId() != null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Chá»‰ Ä‘Æ°á»£c chá»n bÃ¡o cÃ¡o má»™t tin Ä‘Äƒng hoáº·c má»™t ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng trong má»—i láº§n gá»­i");
        }
        if (req.targetType() == ReportTargetType.ITEM) {
            if (req.reportedUserId() != null
                    || (req.targetId() != null && req.itemId() != null && !req.targetId().equals(req.itemId()))) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                        "ThÃ´ng tin Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng bÃ¡o cÃ¡o tin Ä‘Äƒng khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡");
            }
            Long id = req.targetId() != null ? req.targetId() : req.itemId();
            if (id == null) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Vui lÃ²ng cung cáº¥p mÃ£ mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ cáº§n bÃ¡o cÃ¡o");
            }
            return new TargetResolution(ReportTargetType.ITEM, id);
        }
        if (req.targetType() == ReportTargetType.USER) {
            if (req.itemId() != null
                    || (req.targetId() != null && req.reportedUserId() != null
                    && !req.targetId().equals(req.reportedUserId()))) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                        "ThÃ´ng tin Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng bÃ¡o cÃ¡o ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡");
            }
            Long id = req.targetId() != null ? req.targetId() : req.reportedUserId();
            if (id == null) {
                throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Vui lÃ²ng cung cáº¥p mÃ£ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng cáº§n bÃ¡o cÃ¡o");
            }
            return new TargetResolution(ReportTargetType.USER, id);
        }
        if (req.targetId() != null) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                    "Vui lÃ²ng chá»‰ Ä‘á»‹nh loáº¡i Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng bÃ¡o cÃ¡o khi sá»­ dá»¥ng mÃ£ Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng");
        }
        if (req.itemId() != null) {
            return new TargetResolution(ReportTargetType.ITEM, req.itemId());
        }
        if (req.reportedUserId() != null) {
            return new TargetResolution(ReportTargetType.USER, req.reportedUserId());
        }
        throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                "Vui lÃ²ng chá»‰ Ä‘á»‹nh Ä‘á»‘i tÆ°á»£ng bÃ¡o cÃ¡o (tin Ä‘Äƒng hoáº·c ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng)");
    }

    private Pageable pageable(int page, int size) {
        int safePage = Math.max(page, 0);
        int safeSize = Math.min(Math.max(size, 1), MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        return PageRequest.of(safePage, safeSize, Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "createdAt", "id"));
    }

    private record TargetResolution(ReportTargetType targetType, Long targetId) {
    }
}
===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/ReviewService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.review.ReviewRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.review.ReviewResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest.LoanStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Review;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.LoanRequestRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ReviewRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Page;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.util.List;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class ReviewService {

    private static final int MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 50;

    private final ReviewRepository reviewRepository;
    private final LoanRequestRepository loanRequestRepository;
    private final UserRepository userRepository;

    @Transactional
    public ReviewResponse createReview(Long loanId, User actor, ReviewRequest req) {
        if (req.rating() == null || req.rating() < 1 || req.rating() > 5) {
