            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Sá»‘ sao Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡ pháº£i tá»« 1 Ä‘áº¿n 5");
        }

        LoanRequest loan = loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(loanId)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("LoanRequest", loanId));

        User borrower = loan.getBorrower();
        User owner = loan.getItem().getOwner();

        boolean isBorrower = borrower.getId().equals(actor.getId());
        boolean isOwner = owner.getId().equals(actor.getId());

        if (!isBorrower && !isOwner) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN,
                    "Chá»‰ ngÆ°á»i mÆ°á»£n hoáº·c chá»§ Ä‘á»“ cá»§a giao dá»‹ch nÃ y má»›i Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡");
        }

        if (loan.getStatus() != LoanStatus.RETURNED) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT,
                    "Chá»‰ cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡ khi khoáº£n mÆ°á»£n Ä‘Ã£ á»Ÿ tráº¡ng thÃ¡i Ä‘Ã£ tráº£ (RETURNED)");
        }

        if (reviewRepository.existsByLoanIdAndReviewerId(loanId, actor.getId())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.CONFLICT,
                    "Báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡ giao dá»‹ch mÆ°á»£n nÃ y rá»“i");
        }

        User reviewee = isBorrower ? owner : borrower;
        String comment = (req.comment() == null || req.comment().isBlank())
                ? null
                : req.comment().trim();

        Review review = Review.builder()
                .loan(loan)
                .reviewer(actor)
                .reviewee(reviewee)
                .rating(req.rating())
                .comment(comment)
                .build();

        return ReviewResponse.from(reviewRepository.save(review));
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public PageResponse<ReviewResponse> getReviewsByUserId(Long userId, Pageable pageable) {
        if (!userRepository.existsById(userId)) {
            throw new ResourceNotFoundException("User", userId);
        }
        Pageable bounded = boundedPageable(pageable);
        Page<Review> page = reviewRepository.findByRevieweeId(userId, bounded);
        return PageResponse.from(page, ReviewResponse::from);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public List<ReviewResponse> getReviewsByLoanId(Long loanId) {
        if (!loanRequestRepository.existsById(loanId)) {
            throw new ResourceNotFoundException("LoanRequest", loanId);
        }
        return reviewRepository.findByLoanIdOrderByCreatedAtDesc(loanId).stream()
                .map(ReviewResponse::from)
                .toList();
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public double getAverageRating(Long userId) {
        Double avg = reviewRepository.getAverageRatingByUserId(userId);
        if (avg == null || avg == 0.0) {
            return 0.0;
        }
        return Math.round(avg * 10.0) / 10.0;
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public long getReviewCount(Long userId) {
        return reviewRepository.countByRevieweeId(userId);
    }

    private Pageable boundedPageable(Pageable pageable) {
        Sort defaultSort = Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "createdAt", "id");
        if (pageable == null || !pageable.isPaged()) {
            return PageRequest.of(0, 10, defaultSort);
        }
        int page = Math.max(pageable.getPageNumber(), 0);
        int size = Math.min(Math.max(pageable.getPageSize(), 1), MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        Sort sort = pageable.getSort().isSorted() ? pageable.getSort() : defaultSort;
        return PageRequest.of(page, size, sort);
    }
}

===== backend/src/main/java/com/unilend/backend/service/UserService.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.user.ChangePasswordRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.user.UpdateProfileRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.user.UserProfileResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class UserService {

    private final UserRepository userRepository;
    private final CurrentUserService currentUserService;
    private final PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    private final ReviewService reviewService;

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public UserProfileResponse getMyProfile() {
        User user = currentUserService.getCurrentUser();
        return toProfileResponse(user);
    }

    @Transactional(readOnly = true)
    public UserProfileResponse getById(Long id) {
        User user = userRepository.findById(id)
                .orElseThrow(() -> new ResourceNotFoundException("User", id));
        return toProfileResponse(user);
    }

    @Transactional
    public UserProfileResponse updateMyProfile(UpdateProfileRequest req) {
        User user = currentUserService.getCurrentUser();

        user.setFullName(req.fullName().trim());
        user.setPhone(req.phone() == null || req.phone().isBlank() ? null : req.phone().trim());
        user.setUniversity(req.university() == null || req.university().isBlank() ? null : req.university().trim());
        if (req.avatarUrl() != null) {
            user.setAvatarUrl(req.avatarUrl().isBlank() ? null : req.avatarUrl().trim());
        }

        User saved = userRepository.save(user);
        return toProfileResponse(saved);
    }

    @Transactional
    public void changeMyPassword(ChangePasswordRequest req) {
        User user = currentUserService.getCurrentUser();
        if (!user.isEnabled()) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, "TÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ khÃ³a");
        }

        if (!passwordEncoder.matches(req.oldPassword(), user.getPassword())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Máº­t kháº©u cÅ© khÃ´ng chÃ­nh xÃ¡c");
        }

        if (req.oldPassword().equals(req.newPassword())) {
            throw new BusinessException(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, "Máº­t kháº©u má»›i khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c trÃ¹ng vá»›i máº­t kháº©u cÅ©");
        }

        user.setPassword(passwordEncoder.encode(req.newPassword()));
        user.incrementTokenVersion();
        userRepository.save(user);
    }

    private UserProfileResponse toProfileResponse(User user) {
        double avgRating = reviewService.getAverageRating(user.getId());
        long reviewCount = reviewService.getReviewCount(user.getId());
        return UserProfileResponse.from(user, avgRating, reviewCount);
    }
}

CREATE TABLE users (
    id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    created_at TIMESTAMP(6) WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    email VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    password VARCHAR(255) NOT NULL,
    full_name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    phone VARCHAR(20),
    university VARCHAR(150),
    avatar_url VARCHAR(500),
    enabled BOOLEAN NOT NULL,
    email_verified BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT FALSE,
    token_version INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
    CONSTRAINT uk_users_email UNIQUE (email)
);

CREATE TABLE user_roles (
    user_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    role VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (user_id, role),
    CONSTRAINT fk_user_roles_user FOREIGN KEY (user_id) REFERENCES users(id)
);

CREATE TABLE categories (
    id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    created_at TIMESTAMP(6) WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    name VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL,
    description VARCHAR(255),
    CONSTRAINT uk_categories_name UNIQUE (name)
);

CREATE TABLE items (
    id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    created_at TIMESTAMP(6) WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    title VARCHAR(150) NOT NULL,
    description TEXT,
    owner_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    category_id BIGINT,
    status ENUM('AVAILABLE', 'LENT_OUT', 'UNAVAILABLE') NOT NULL,
    CONSTRAINT fk_items_owner FOREIGN KEY (owner_id) REFERENCES users(id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_items_category FOREIGN KEY (category_id) REFERENCES categories(id)
);

CREATE TABLE item_images (
    id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    created_at TIMESTAMP(6) WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    item_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    image_url VARCHAR(500) NOT NULL,

);

    id BIGINT GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY,
    created_at TIMESTAMP(6) WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    item_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    borrower_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    status ENUM('PENDING', 'APPROVED', 'REJECTED', 'RETURNED', 'CANCELLED', 'OVERDUE') NOT NULL,
    borrow_from DATE,
    due_date DATE,
    note VARCHAR(500),
    requested_at TIMESTAMP(6) WITH TIME ZONE NOT NULL,
    responded_at TIMESTAMP(6) WITH TIME ZONE,
    returned_at TIMESTAMP(6) WITH TIME ZONE,
    CONSTRAINT fk_loan_item FOREIGN KEY (item_id) REFERENCES items(id),

);

);

    loan_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    reviewer_id BIGINT NOT NULL,

    rating INTEGER NOT NULL,
    comment VARCHAR(1000),
    CONSTRAINT uk_review_loan_reviewer UNIQUE (loan_id, reviewer_id),

    CONSTRAINT fk_reviews_reviewer FOREIGN KEY (reviewer_id) REFERENCES users(id),

);

);

    id BIGINT NOT NULL AUTO_INCREMENT,
    created_at DATETIME(6) NOT NULL,
    reporter_id BIGINT NOT NULL,
    target_type VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL,
    reported_item_id BIGINT NULL,
    reported_user_id BIGINT NULL,
    reason VARCHAR(30) NOT NULL,
    description VARCHAR(1000) NOT NULL,
    status VARCHAR(20) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'OPEN',
    resolved_by_id BIGINT NULL,
    resolution_note VARCHAR(500) NULL,
    resolved_at DATETIME(6) NULL,
    PRIMARY KEY (id),
    INDEX idx_reports_status (status),
    INDEX idx_reports_reporter (reporter_id),
    INDEX idx_reports_target_type (target_type),
    INDEX idx_reports_reported_item (reported_item_id),
    INDEX idx_reports_reported_user (reported_user_id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_reports_reporter FOREIGN KEY (reporter_id) REFERENCES users (id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_reports_reported_item FOREIGN KEY (reported_item_id) REFERENCES items (id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_reports_reported_user FOREIGN KEY (reported_user_id) REFERENCES users (id),
    CONSTRAINT fk_reports_resolved_by FOREIGN KEY (resolved_by_id) REFERENCES users (id)
);

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/controller/AuthAndLoanIntegrationTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.controller;

import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;

import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;

import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.security.test.web.servlet.setup.SecurityMockMvcConfigurers;
import org.springframework.test.web.servlet.MockMvc;
import org.springframework.web.context.WebApplicationContext;

import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.get;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.post;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.jsonPath;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.result.MockMvcResultMatchers.status;
import static org.springframework.test.web.servlet.setup.MockMvcBuilders.webAppContextSetup;

@SpringBootTest(properties = {
        "app.jwt.secret=dW5pbGVuZC10ZXN0LWp3dC1zZWNyZXQta2V5LWF0LWxlYXN0LTMyLWJ5dGVz",
        "spring.datasource.url=jdbc:h2:mem:unilend-integration;MODE=MySQL;DATABASE_TO_LOWER=TRUE;DB_CLOSE_DELAY=-1",
        "spring.datasource.username=sa",
        "spring.datasource.password=",
        "spring.datasource.driver-class-name=org.h2.Driver",
        "spring.jpa.hibernate.ddl-auto=validate",
        "spring.jpa.show-sql=false",
        "spring.flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/h2"
})
class AuthAndLoanIntegrationTest {

    @Autowired
    private WebApplicationContext context;

    private MockMvc mockMvc;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        mockMvc = webAppContextSetup(context)
                .apply(SecurityMockMvcConfigurers.springSecurity())
                .build();
    }

    @Test
    void registrationAndLoginReturnUsableBearerToken() throws Exception {
        String email = "integration-auth@example.test";
        String body = """
                {"email":"%s","password":"password123","fullName":"Integration User"}
                """.formatted(email);

        mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/register")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(body))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.success").value(true))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.token").isNotEmpty());

        mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/login")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {"email":"%s","password":"password123"}
                                """.formatted(email)))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.token").isNotEmpty());
    }

    @Test
    void loanHistoryRequiresAuthentication() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/loans/mine"))
                .andExpect(status().isUnauthorized())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.errorCode").value("UNAUTHORIZED"));
    }

    @Test
    void authenticatedUserCanReadLoanHistory() throws Exception {
        String body = """
                {"email":"loan-history@example.test","password":"password123","fullName":"Loan User"}
                """;
        String token = mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/register")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content(body))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        token = com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath.read(token, "$.data.token");

        mockMvc.perform(get("/loans/mine")
                        .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + token))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.success").value(true))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.content").isArray());
    }

    @Test
    void openApiDocsIncludeBearerAuthSecurityScheme() throws Exception {
        mockMvc.perform(get("/v3/api-docs"))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.components.securitySchemes.bearerAuth.type").value("http"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.components.securitySchemes.bearerAuth.scheme").value("bearer"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.components.securitySchemes.bearerAuth.bearerFormat").value("JWT"))
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.security[0].bearerAuth").isArray());
    }

    @Test
    void concurrentApprovalsPreventOverlappingLoansAndAutoRejectOtherPending() throws Exception {
        String ownerToken = registerAndGetToken("owner-concurrent@example.test", "Owner Concurrent");
        String borrower1Token = registerAndGetToken("borrower1-concurrent@example.test", "Borrower One");
        String borrower2Token = registerAndGetToken("borrower2-concurrent@example.test", "Borrower Two");

        String itemJson = mockMvc.perform(post("/items")
                        .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + ownerToken)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {"title":"Concurrent Camera","description":"DSLR"}
                                """))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        Integer itemIdInt = com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath.read(itemJson, "$.data.id");
        long itemId = itemIdInt.longValue();

        java.time.LocalDate start = java.time.LocalDate.now(java.time.ZoneId.of("Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh")).plusDays(1);
        java.time.LocalDate end = start.plusDays(3);

        String loan1Json = mockMvc.perform(post("/loans")
                        .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + borrower1Token)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {"itemId":%d,"borrowFrom":"%s","dueDate":"%s","note":"Request 1"}
                                """.formatted(itemId, start, end)))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        long loan1Id = ((Number) com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath.read(loan1Json, "$.data.id")).longValue();

        String loan2Json = mockMvc.perform(post("/loans")
                        .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + borrower2Token)
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {"itemId":%d,"borrowFrom":"%s","dueDate":"%s","note":"Request 2"}
                                """.formatted(itemId, start.plusDays(1), end.plusDays(1))))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        long loan2Id = ((Number) com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath.read(loan2Json, "$.data.id")).longValue();

        java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch ready = new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(2);
        java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch go = new java.util.concurrent.CountDownLatch(1);
        java.util.concurrent.ExecutorService pool = java.util.concurrent.Executors.newFixedThreadPool(2);

        java.util.concurrent.Callable<Integer> approve1 = () -> {
            ready.countDown();
            go.await();
            return mockMvc.perform(org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.patch("/loans/" + loan1Id + "/approve")
                            .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + ownerToken))
                    .andReturn().getResponse().getStatus();
        };
        java.util.concurrent.Callable<Integer> approve2 = () -> {
            ready.countDown();
            go.await();
            return mockMvc.perform(org.springframework.test.web.servlet.request.MockMvcRequestBuilders.patch("/loans/" + loan2Id + "/approve")
                            .header("Authorization", "Bearer " + ownerToken))
                    .andReturn().getResponse().getStatus();
        };

        java.util.concurrent.Future<Integer> f1 = pool.submit(approve1);
        java.util.concurrent.Future<Integer> f2 = pool.submit(approve2);
        ready.await();
        go.countDown();

        int s1 = f1.get();
        int s2 = f2.get();
        pool.shutdown();

        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue(
                (s1 == 200 && s2 == 409) || (s1 == 409 && s2 == 200),
                "Expected exactly one 200 OK and one 409 CONFLICT, got: " + s1 + " and " + s2);

        mockMvc.perform(get("/items/" + itemId + "/booked-dates"))
                .andExpect(status().isOk())
                .andExpect(jsonPath("$.data.length()").value(1));
    }

    private String registerAndGetToken(String email, String fullName) throws Exception {
        String res = mockMvc.perform(post("/auth/register")
                        .contentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON)
                        .content("""
                                {"email":"%s","password":"password123","fullName":"%s"}
                                """.formatted(email, fullName)))
                .andExpect(status().isCreated())
                .andReturn().getResponse().getContentAsString();
        return com.jayway.jsonpath.JsonPath.read(res, "$.data.token");
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/migration/DatabaseMigrationTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.migration;

import org.flywaydb.core.Flyway;
import org.flywaydb.core.api.FlywayException;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import java.sql.Connection;
import java.sql.DriverManager;
import java.sql.SQLException;
import java.util.UUID;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

/** Executes the H2 migration, not Hibernate-generated DDL. No MySQL or Docker needed. */
class DatabaseMigrationTest {
    private String url() {
        return "jdbc:h2:mem:migration_" + UUID.randomUUID().toString().replace("-", "")
                + ";MODE=MySQL;DATABASE_TO_LOWER=TRUE;DB_CLOSE_DELAY=-1";
    }

    private Flyway flyway(String url) {
        return Flyway.configure().dataSource(url, "sa", "")
                .locations("classpath:db/migration/h2")
                .baselineOnMigrate(false).cleanDisabled(true).validateMigrationNaming(true).load();
    }

    @Test
    void createsAllTablesAndMigratesOnlyOnce() throws Exception {
        String url = url();
        Flyway flyway = flyway(url);
        assertEquals(2, flyway.migrate().migrationsExecuted);
        flyway.validate();
        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(url, "sa", "")) {
            for (String table : new String[]{"users", "user_roles", "categories", "items",
                    "item_images", "loan_requests", "notifications", "reviews", "account_tokens",
                    "reports"}) {
                assertEquals(0, scalar(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM " + table));
            }
            assertEquals(19, scalar(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM information_schema.indexes "
                    + "WHERE index_name LIKE 'idx_%' AND table_schema = 'public'"));
            insertOwner(connection);
            connection.createStatement().executeUpdate("INSERT INTO user_roles(user_id, role) VALUES (1, 'USER')");
        }
        assertEquals(0, flyway.migrate().migrationsExecuted);
        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(url, "sa", "")) {
            assertEquals(1, scalar(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users"));
            assertEquals(1, scalar(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM user_roles"));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void enforcesUniqueForeignKeyAndNotNullConstraints() throws Exception {
        String url = url();
        flyway(url).migrate();
        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(url, "sa", "")) {
            insertOwner(connection);
            assertThrows(SQLException.class, () -> connection.createStatement().executeUpdate(
                    "INSERT INTO users(id, created_at, email, password, full_name, enabled) "
                    + "VALUES (2, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'owner@example.test', 'hash', 'Duplicate', TRUE)"));
            assertThrows(SQLException.class, () -> connection.createStatement().executeUpdate(
                    "INSERT INTO user_roles(user_id, role) VALUES (999, 'USER')"));
            assertThrows(SQLException.class, () -> connection.createStatement().executeUpdate(
                    "INSERT INTO categories(id, created_at, name) VALUES (1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, NULL)"));
            connection.createStatement().executeUpdate("INSERT INTO items(id, created_at, title, owner_id, status) "
                    + "VALUES (1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'Book', 1, 'AVAILABLE')");
            connection.createStatement().executeUpdate("INSERT INTO loan_requests(id, created_at, item_id, "
                    + "borrower_id, status, requested_at) VALUES (1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 1, 1, 'OVERDUE', CURRENT_TIMESTAMP)");
            connection.createStatement().executeUpdate("INSERT INTO reviews(id, created_at, loan_id, reviewer_id, "
                    + "reviewee_id, rating) VALUES (1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 1, 1, 1, 5)");
            assertThrows(SQLException.class, () -> connection.createStatement().executeUpdate(
                    "INSERT INTO reviews(id, created_at, loan_id, reviewer_id, reviewee_id, rating) "
                    + "VALUES (2, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 1, 1, 1, 4)"));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void refusesUnknownExistingSchemaWithoutModifyingItsData() throws Exception {
        String url = url();
        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(url, "sa", "")) {
            connection.createStatement().execute("CREATE TABLE legacy_record(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY)");
            connection.createStatement().executeUpdate("INSERT INTO legacy_record VALUES (42)");
        }
        assertThrows(FlywayException.class, () -> flyway(url).migrate());
        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(url, "sa", "")) {
            assertEquals(42, scalar(connection, "SELECT id FROM legacy_record"));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void explicitBaselineKeepsExistingRowsAndSkipsV1() throws Exception {
        String url = url();
        // Create a real V1 schema as a stand-in for an already initialized dev database.
        flyway(url).migrate();
        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(url, "sa", "")) {
            insertOwner(connection);
            // Test database only: simulate pre-Flyway ownership by removing only its history.
            connection.createStatement().execute("DROP TABLE \"flyway_schema_history\"");
        }
        Flyway adoption = Flyway.configure().dataSource(url, "sa", "")
                .locations("classpath:db/migration/h2").baselineVersion("1")
                .baselineOnMigrate(false).cleanDisabled(true).load();
        adoption.baseline();
        assertEquals(0, adoption.migrate().migrationsExecuted);
        try (Connection connection = DriverManager.getConnection(url, "sa", "")) {
            assertEquals(1, scalar(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM users"));
            assertEquals(1, scalar(connection, "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM \"flyway_schema_history\" "
                    + "WHERE \"type\" = 'BASELINE' AND \"version\" = '1'"));
        }
    }

    private void insertOwner(Connection connection) throws SQLException {
        connection.createStatement().executeUpdate("INSERT INTO users(id, created_at, email, password, "
                + "full_name, enabled) VALUES (1, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP, 'owner@example.test', 'hash', 'Owner', TRUE)");
    }

    private int scalar(Connection connection, String sql) throws SQLException {
        try (var statement = connection.createStatement(); var result = statement.executeQuery(sql)) {
            assertTrue(result.next());
            return result.getInt(1);
        }
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/migration/MigrationContextTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.migration;

import org.flywaydb.core.Flyway;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.test.context.SpringBootTest;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

/** Flyway creates the schema first; Hibernate must validate every entity without update/create. */
@SpringBootTest(properties = {
        "app.jwt.secret=dW5pbGVuZC10ZXN0LWp3dC1zZWNyZXQta2V5LWF0LWxlYXN0LTMyLWJ5dGVz",
        "spring.datasource.url=jdbc:h2:mem:flyway-entity-check;MODE=MySQL;DATABASE_TO_LOWER=TRUE;DB_CLOSE_DELAY=-1",
        "spring.datasource.driver-class-name=org.h2.Driver",
        "spring.datasource.username=sa",
        "spring.datasource.password=",
        "spring.flyway.enabled=true",
        "spring.flyway.locations=classpath:db/migration/h2",
        "spring.flyway.baseline-on-migrate=false",
        "spring.jpa.hibernate.ddl-auto=validate",
        "spring.jpa.show-sql=false"
})
class MigrationContextTest {
    @Autowired Flyway flyway;

    @Test
    void migrationsAndEntityMappingsAgree() {
        flyway.validate();
        assertNotNull(flyway.info().current());
        assertEquals("2", flyway.info().current().getVersion().toString());
        assertEquals(0, flyway.info().pending().length);
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/repository/AccountTokenRepositoryTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.repository;

import com.unilend.backend.entity.AccountToken;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.AccountToken.TokenType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.boot.data.jpa.test.autoconfigure.DataJpaTest;

import java.time.Instant;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNotNull;

@DataJpaTest(properties = {
        "spring.datasource.url=jdbc:h2:mem:account-token-repository;MODE=MySQL;DB_CLOSE_DELAY=-1",
        "spring.datasource.username=sa",
        "spring.datasource.password=",
        "spring.datasource.driver-class-name=org.h2.Driver",
        "spring.jpa.hibernate.ddl-auto=create-drop"
})
class AccountTokenRepositoryTest {

    @Autowired
    private AccountTokenRepository accountTokenRepository;

    @Autowired
    private UserRepository userRepository;

    @Test
    void validTokenCanBeConsumedOnlyOnceAndExpiredTokenCannotBeConsumed() {
        User user = userRepository.saveAndFlush(User.builder()
                .email("account-token@example.test")
                .password("encoded-password")
                .fullName("Account Token")
                .build());
        Instant now = Instant.now();
        AccountToken validToken = accountTokenRepository.saveAndFlush(AccountToken.builder()
                .user(user)
                .type(TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET)
                .tokenHash("valid-token-hash")
                .expiresAt(now.plusSeconds(60))
                .build());
        AccountToken expiredToken = accountTokenRepository.saveAndFlush(AccountToken.builder()
                .user(user)
                .type(TokenType.EMAIL_VERIFICATION)
                .tokenHash("expired-token-hash")
                .expiresAt(now.minusSeconds(1))
                .build());

        assertEquals(1, accountTokenRepository.consumeValidToken(
                validToken.getTokenHash(), validToken.getType(), now));
        assertEquals(0, accountTokenRepository.consumeValidToken(
                validToken.getTokenHash(), validToken.getType(), now));
        assertEquals(0, accountTokenRepository.consumeValidToken(
                expiredToken.getTokenHash(), expiredToken.getType(), now));
        assertNotNull(accountTokenRepository.findByTokenHashAndType(
                validToken.getTokenHash(), validToken.getType()).orElseThrow().getUsedAt());
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/scheduler/LoanOverdueSchedulerTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.scheduler;

import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.LoanRequestRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.NotificationRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.service.NotificationService;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageImpl;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.ZoneId;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.*;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

class LoanOverdueSchedulerTest {

    private LoanRequestRepository loanRepository;
    private NotificationRepository notificationRepository;
    private NotificationService notificationService;
    private LoanOverdueScheduler scheduler;

    private static final ZoneId ZONE = ZoneId.of("Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh");

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        loanRepository = mock(LoanRequestRepository.class);
        notificationRepository = mock(NotificationRepository.class);
        notificationService = mock(NotificationService.class);

        scheduler = new LoanOverdueScheduler(
                loanRepository, notificationRepository, notificationService);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldMarkPastDueLoanAsOverdueAndNotifyBorrower() {
        User borrower = new User();
        borrower.setId(10L);

        LoanRequest loan = new LoanRequest();
        loan.setId(20L);
        loan.setBorrower(borrower);
        loan.setStatus(LoanRequest.LoanStatus.APPROVED);
        loan.setDueDate(LocalDate.now(ZONE).minusDays(1));

        when(loanRepository.findDueOn(any(), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of()));

        when(loanRepository.findOverdue(any(), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(
                        new PageImpl<>(List.of(loan)),
                        new PageImpl<>(List.of())
                );

        when(notificationRepository
                .existsByUserIdAndTypeAndLinkAndCreatedAtBetween(
                        anyLong(), anyString(), anyString(),
                        any(Instant.class), any(Instant.class)))
                .thenReturn(false);

        scheduler.updateOverdueLoans();

        assertEquals(LoanRequest.LoanStatus.OVERDUE, loan.getStatus());
        verify(loanRepository).saveAllAndFlush(anyList());
        verify(notificationService).notify(
                eq(10L),
                eq(com.unilend.backend.entity.Notification.NotificationType.LOAN_OVERDUE),
                anyString(),
                eq("/loans/20")
        );
    }

    @Test
    void shouldNotSendDuplicateNotification() {
        User borrower = new User();
        borrower.setId(10L);

        LoanRequest loan = new LoanRequest();
        loan.setId(20L);
        loan.setBorrower(borrower);
        loan.setStatus(LoanRequest.LoanStatus.APPROVED);

        when(loanRepository.findDueOn(any(), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of(loan)));

        when(loanRepository.findOverdue(any(), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of()));

        when(notificationRepository
                .existsByUserIdAndTypeAndLinkAndCreatedAtBetween(
                        anyLong(), anyString(), anyString(),
                        any(Instant.class), any(Instant.class)))
                .thenReturn(true);

        scheduler.updateOverdueLoans();

        verifyNoInteractions(notificationService);
    }

    @Test
    void shouldNotifyBorrowerOneDayBeforeDueDate() {
        User borrower = new User();
        borrower.setId(10L);

        LocalDate tomorrow = LocalDate.now(ZONE).plusDays(1);

        LoanRequest loan = new LoanRequest();
        loan.setId(30L);
        loan.setBorrower(borrower);
        loan.setStatus(LoanRequest.LoanStatus.APPROVED);
        loan.setDueDate(tomorrow);

        when(loanRepository.findDueOn(eq(tomorrow), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of(loan)));

        when(loanRepository.findOverdue(any(), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of()));

        when(notificationRepository
                .existsByUserIdAndTypeAndLinkAndCreatedAtBetween(
                        anyLong(), anyString(), anyString(),
                        any(Instant.class), any(Instant.class)))
                .thenReturn(false);

        scheduler.updateOverdueLoans();

        verify(notificationService).notify(
                eq(10L),
                eq(com.unilend.backend.entity.Notification.NotificationType.LOAN_DUE_SOON),
                anyString(),
                eq("/loans/30")
        );

        assertEquals(LoanRequest.LoanStatus.APPROVED, loan.getStatus());
        verify(loanRepository, never()).saveAllAndFlush(anyList());
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/AccountTokenServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.AccountToken;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.AccountToken.TokenType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.AccountTokenRepository;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.Optional;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class AccountTokenServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private AccountTokenRepository accountTokenRepository;

    @InjectMocks
    private AccountTokenService accountTokenService;

    @Test
    void consumeTokenClaimsValidTokenAtomicallyBeforeReturningUser() {
        String rawToken = "one-time-token";
        User user = User.builder().id(1L).email("person@example.test").build();
        AccountToken token = AccountToken.builder().user(user).type(TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET).build();
        when(accountTokenRepository.consumeValidToken(
                eq(accountTokenService.hashToken(rawToken)), eq(TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET), any(Instant.class)))
                .thenReturn(1);
        when(accountTokenRepository.findByTokenHashAndType(
                accountTokenService.hashToken(rawToken), TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET))
                .thenReturn(Optional.of(token));

        User consumedUser = accountTokenService.consumeToken(rawToken, TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET);

        assertEquals(user, consumedUser);
        verify(accountTokenRepository).consumeValidToken(
                eq(accountTokenService.hashToken(rawToken)), eq(TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET), any(Instant.class));
    }

    @Test
    void consumeTokenRejectsTokenAlreadyClaimed() {
        String rawToken = "already-used-token";
        AccountToken token = AccountToken.builder()
                .type(TokenType.EMAIL_VERIFICATION)
                .usedAt(Instant.now())
                .build();
        when(accountTokenRepository.consumeValidToken(
                eq(accountTokenService.hashToken(rawToken)), eq(TokenType.EMAIL_VERIFICATION), any(Instant.class)))
                .thenReturn(0);
        when(accountTokenRepository.findByTokenHashAndType(
                accountTokenService.hashToken(rawToken), TokenType.EMAIL_VERIFICATION))
                .thenReturn(Optional.of(token));

        assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> accountTokenService.consumeToken(rawToken, TokenType.EMAIL_VERIFICATION));
    }

    @Test
    void consumeTokenRejectsExpiredToken() {
        String rawToken = "expired-token";
        AccountToken token = AccountToken.builder()
                .type(TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET)
                .expiresAt(Instant.now().minusSeconds(1))
                .build();
        when(accountTokenRepository.consumeValidToken(
                eq(accountTokenService.hashToken(rawToken)), eq(TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET), any(Instant.class)))
                .thenReturn(0);
        when(accountTokenRepository.findByTokenHashAndType(
                accountTokenService.hashToken(rawToken), TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET))
                .thenReturn(Optional.of(token));

        assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> accountTokenService.consumeToken(rawToken, TokenType.PASSWORD_RESET));
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/AdminStatsServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.controller.AdminStatsController;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest.LoanStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ItemRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.LoanRequestRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;

import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

class AdminStatsServiceTest {

    @Test
    void returnsTotalsStatusCountsAndMostBorrowedCategories() {
        UserRepository users = mock(UserRepository.class);
        ItemRepository items = mock(ItemRepository.class);
        LoanRequestRepository loans = mock(LoanRequestRepository.class);
        when(users.count()).thenReturn(12L);
        when(items.count()).thenReturn(8L);
        when(loans.countLoansByStatus()).thenReturn(List.<Object[]>of(
                new Object[]{LoanStatus.APPROVED, 4L},
                new Object[]{LoanStatus.RETURNED, 3L}));
        when(loans.findMostBorrowedCategories(any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(List.<Object[]>of(new Object[]{5L, "Electronics", 6L}));

        var result = new AdminStatsService(users, items, loans).getStats();

        assertEquals(12L, result.totalUsers());
        assertEquals(8L, result.totalItems());
        assertEquals(4L, result.loansByStatus().get("APPROVED"));
        assertEquals(3L, result.loansByStatus().get("RETURNED"));
        assertEquals(0L, result.loansByStatus().get("PENDING"));
        assertEquals(5L, result.mostBorrowedCategories().get(0).categoryId());
        assertEquals("Electronics", result.mostBorrowedCategories().get(0).categoryName());
        assertEquals(6L, result.mostBorrowedCategories().get(0).loanCount());
        verify(loans).findMostBorrowedCategories(any(Pageable.class));
    }

    @Test
    void emptyDatabaseReturnsZeroTotalsAndEmptyCategories() {
        UserRepository users = mock(UserRepository.class);
        ItemRepository items = mock(ItemRepository.class);
        LoanRequestRepository loans = mock(LoanRequestRepository.class);
        when(loans.countLoansByStatus()).thenReturn(List.of());
        when(loans.findMostBorrowedCategories(any(Pageable.class))).thenReturn(List.of());

        var result = new AdminStatsService(users, items, loans).getStats();
        assertEquals(0L, result.totalUsers());
        assertEquals(0L, result.totalItems());
        assertTrue(result.mostBorrowedCategories().isEmpty());
        assertTrue(result.loansByStatus().values().stream().allMatch(count -> count == 0L));
    }

    @Test
    void controllerChecksAdminRoleBeforeReturningStats() {
        CurrentUserService currentUser = mock(CurrentUserService.class);
        AdminStatsService stats = mock(AdminStatsService.class);
        doThrow(new SecurityException("Forbidden")).when(currentUser).requireAdmin();

        var controller = new AdminStatsController(currentUser, stats);
        assertThrows(SecurityException.class, controller::getStats);
        verifyNoInteractions(stats);
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/AdminUserServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageImpl;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Sort;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class AdminUserServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private UserRepository userRepository;

    @Mock
    private CurrentUserService currentUserService;

    @InjectMocks
    private AdminUserService adminUserService;

    private User admin;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        admin = User.builder().id(1L).email("admin@example.test").fullName("Admin").build();
        when(currentUserService.requireAdmin()).thenReturn(admin);
    }

    @Test
    void findAllTrimsKeywordAndCapsPageSize() {
        when(userRepository.searchUsers(eq("alice"), eq(false), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of(), PageRequest.of(0, 50), 0));

        adminUserService.findAll("  alice  ", false, -2, 500);

        ArgumentCaptor<Pageable> pageable = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(Pageable.class);
        verify(userRepository).searchUsers(eq("alice"), eq(false), pageable.capture());
        assertEquals(0, pageable.getValue().getPageNumber());
        assertEquals(50, pageable.getValue().getPageSize());
        assertEquals(Sort.by(Sort.Direction.DESC, "createdAt", "id"), pageable.getValue().getSort());
    }

    @Test
    void findAllRequiresAdmin() {
        when(currentUserService.requireAdmin()).thenThrow(new BusinessException(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN));

        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> adminUserService.findAll(null, null, 0, 10));

        verify(userRepository, never()).searchUsers(any(), any(), any());
    }

    @Test
    void lockUserDisablesTargetAccount() {
        User target = User.builder()
                .id(2L)
                .email("user@example.test")
                .fullName("User")
                .tokenVersion(4)
                .build();
        when(userRepository.findById(2L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(target));
        when(userRepository.save(target)).thenReturn(target);

        var response = adminUserService.lockUser(2L);

        assertFalse(target.isEnabled());
        assertFalse(response.enabled());
        assertEquals(5, target.getTokenVersion());
        verify(userRepository).save(target);
    }

    @Test
    void lockUserRejectsLockingCurrentAdmin() {
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> adminUserService.lockUser(1L));

        verify(userRepository, never()).findById(any());
        verify(userRepository, never()).save(any());
    }

    @Test
    void lockUserRejectsDisablingLastEnabledAdmin() {
        User lastAdmin = User.builder()
                .id(2L)
                .email("last-admin@example.test")
                .fullName("Last Admin")
                .roles(Set.of("ADMIN"))
                .build();
        when(userRepository.findById(2L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(lastAdmin));
        when(userRepository.findAllByEnabledTrue()).thenReturn(List.of(lastAdmin));

        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> adminUserService.lockUser(2L));

        assertTrue(lastAdmin.isEnabled());
        verify(userRepository, never()).save(any());
    }

    @Test
    void unlockUserEnablesTargetAccount() {
        User target = User.builder()
                .id(2L)
                .email("user@example.test")
                .fullName("User")
                .enabled(false)
                .tokenVersion(4)
                .build();
        when(userRepository.findById(2L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(target));
        when(userRepository.save(target)).thenReturn(target);

        var response = adminUserService.unlockUser(2L);

        assertTrue(target.isEnabled());
        assertTrue(response.enabled());
        assertEquals(5, target.getTokenVersion());
        verify(userRepository).save(target);
    }

    @Test
    void lockUserRejectsUnknownTarget() {
        when(userRepository.findById(404L)).thenReturn(Optional.empty());

        assertThrows(ResourceNotFoundException.class, () -> adminUserService.lockUser(404L));

        verify(userRepository, never()).save(any());
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/AuthServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.auth.AuthResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.auth.LoginRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.auth.RegisterRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.AuthenticationManager;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.BadCredentialsException;
import org.springframework.security.authentication.UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.List;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyInt;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyList;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyLong;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyString;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class AuthServiceTest {

    @Mock private UserRepository userRepository;
    @Mock private PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;
    @Mock private AuthenticationManager authenticationManager;
    @Mock private JwtService jwtService;
    @Mock private CurrentUserService currentUserService;
    @Mock private ReviewService reviewService;
    @Mock private AccountTokenService accountTokenService;
    @Mock private EmailService emailService;
    @Mock private RateLimitService rateLimitService;

    @InjectMocks private AuthService authService;

    @Test
    void registerNormalizesEmailAndReturnsToken() {
        when(userRepository.existsByEmail("person@example.test")).thenReturn(false);
        when(passwordEncoder.encode("password123")).thenReturn("encoded-password");
        when(userRepository.save(any(User.class))).thenAnswer(invocation -> {
            User saved = invocation.getArgument(0);
            saved.setId(7L);
            return saved;
        });
        when(accountTokenService.createEmailVerificationToken(any(User.class))).thenReturn("verification-token");
        when(jwtService.generateToken(eq(7L), eq("person@example.test"), any(), eq(0)))
                .thenReturn("signed-token");

        AuthResponse response = authService.register(
                new RegisterRequest(" PERSON@example.test ", "password123", " Person "));

        assertEquals("person@example.test", response.email());
        assertEquals("Person", response.fullName());
        assertEquals("signed-token", response.token());
        verify(passwordEncoder).encode("password123");
        verify(rateLimitService).checkEmailRateLimit("register", "person@example.test");
        verify(emailService).sendVerificationEmail(
                "person@example.test", "Person", "verification-token");
    }

    @Test
    void registerRejectsDuplicateEmail() {
        when(userRepository.existsByEmail("person@example.test")).thenReturn(true);

        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> authService.register(
                new RegisterRequest("PERSON@example.test", "password123", "Person")));

        assertEquals(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, exception.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void loginAuthenticatesNormalizedEmailAndReturnsToken() {
        User user = User.builder()
                .id(9L)
                .email("person@example.test")
                .fullName("Person")
                .build();
        when(authenticationManager.authenticate(any(UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken.class)))
                .thenReturn(new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken("person@example.test", "password123"));
        when(userRepository.findByEmail("person@example.test")).thenReturn(Optional.of(user));
        user.setTokenVersion(3);
        when(jwtService.generateToken(
                eq(9L), eq("person@example.test"), eq(List.of("USER")), eq(3)))
                .thenReturn("signed-token");

        AuthResponse response = authService.login(new LoginRequest(" PERSON@example.test ", "password123"));

        assertEquals("signed-token", response.token());
        verify(authenticationManager).authenticate(any(UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken.class));
        verify(rateLimitService).checkLoginRateLimit("person@example.test");
        verify(jwtService).generateToken(9L, "person@example.test", List.of("USER"), 3);
    }

    @Test
    void loginReturnsUnauthorizedForInvalidCredentials() {
        when(authenticationManager.authenticate(any(UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken.class)))
                .thenThrow(new BadCredentialsException("invalid credentials"));

        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> authService.login(new LoginRequest("person@example.test", "wrong")));

        assertEquals(ErrorCode.UNAUTHORIZED, exception.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void loginRejectsDisabledAccountEvenIfAuthenticationManagerReturnsSuccess() {
        User disabledUser = User.builder()
                .id(10L)
                .email("disabled@example.test")
                .fullName("Disabled User")
                .enabled(false)
                .build();
        when(authenticationManager.authenticate(any(UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken.class)))
                .thenReturn(new UsernamePasswordAuthenticationToken("disabled@example.test", "password123"));
        when(userRepository.findByEmail("disabled@example.test")).thenReturn(Optional.of(disabledUser));

        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> authService.login(new LoginRequest("disabled@example.test", "password123")));

        assertEquals(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, exception.getErrorCode());
        verify(jwtService, org.mockito.Mockito.never())
                .generateToken(anyLong(), anyString(), anyList(), anyInt());
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/EmailServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.ObjectProvider;
import org.springframework.mail.MailSendException;
import org.springframework.mail.SimpleMailMessage;
import org.springframework.mail.javamail.JavaMailSender;
import org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertNull;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.mock;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.doThrow;

class EmailServiceTest {

    private final ObjectProvider<JavaMailSender> mailSenderProvider = mock();
    private final JavaMailSender mailSender = mock();
    private final EmailService emailService = new EmailService(mailSenderProvider);

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        ReflectionTestUtils.setField(emailService, "mailEnabled", true);
        ReflectionTestUtils.setField(emailService, "fromAddress", "no-reply@example.test");
        ReflectionTestUtils.setField(emailService, "frontendUrl", "https://app.example.test");
        when(mailSenderProvider.getIfAvailable()).thenReturn(mailSender);
    }

    @Test
    void sendsVerificationEmailWithTokenOnlyInMessage() {
        emailService.sendVerificationEmail("user@example.test", "Test User", "raw-verification-token");

        var messageCaptor = org.mockito.ArgumentCaptor.forClass(SimpleMailMessage.class);
        verify(mailSender).send(messageCaptor.capture());
        SimpleMailMessage message = messageCaptor.getValue();
        assertEquals("no-reply@example.test", message.getFrom());
        assertEquals("user@example.test", message.getTo()[0]);
        assertTrue(
                message.getText().contains("https://app.example.test/verify-email?token=raw-verification-token"));
    }

    @Test
    void reportsMailFailureWithoutExposingProviderException() {
        doThrow(new MailSendException("sensitive smtp detail"))
                .when(mailSender).send(any(SimpleMailMessage.class));

        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> emailService.sendPasswordResetEmail(
                        "user@example.test", "Test User", "raw-reset-token"));

        assertEquals("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ gá»­i email lÃºc nÃ y", exception.getMessage());
        assertNull(exception.getCause());
    }

    @Test
    void reportsMissingMailSenderWhenSmtpIsEnabled() {
        when(mailSenderProvider.getIfAvailable()).thenReturn(null);

        assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> emailService.sendVerificationEmail(
                        "user@example.test", "Test User", "raw-token"));
        verify(mailSender, never()).send(any(SimpleMailMessage.class));
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/ImageUploadValidatorTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.ParameterizedTest;
import org.junit.jupiter.params.provider.ValueSource;
import org.springframework.mock.web.MockMultipartFile;
import javax.imageio.ImageIO;
import java.awt.image.BufferedImage;
import java.io.ByteArrayInputStream;
import java.io.ByteArrayOutputStream;
import java.nio.charset.StandardCharsets;
import java.util.Arrays;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;

class ImageUploadValidatorTest {
    private final ImageUploadValidator validator = new ImageUploadValidator();

    @ParameterizedTest
    @ValueSource(strings = {"valid.png", "valid.jpg", "valid.webp"})
    void acceptsRealImageDespiteFalseClientMetadata(String fixture) throws Exception {
        var result = validator.validate(new MockMultipartFile("files", "../../payload.exe",
                "application/octet-stream", fixture(fixture)));
        assertEquals(fixture.endsWith("jpg") ? ".jpg" : ".png", result.extension());
        var decoded = ImageIO.read(new ByteArrayInputStream(result.bytes()));
        assertNotNull(decoded);
        assertEquals(2, decoded.getWidth());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsTextRenamedAsJpeg() {
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> validator.validate(
                new MockMultipartFile("files", "fake.jpg", "image/jpeg", "<script>alert(1)</script>".getBytes())));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsForgedSignatureWithoutDecodablePixels() throws Exception {
        for (String name : new String[]{"valid.png", "valid.jpg", "valid.webp"}) {
            byte[] prefix = Arrays.copyOf(fixture(name), 12);
            assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> validator.validate(
                    new MockMultipartFile("files", name, "image/png", prefix)));
        }
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsUnsupportedGif() throws Exception {
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> validator.validate(
                new MockMultipartFile("files", "fake.png", "image/png", fixture("unsupported.gif"))));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNullEmptyAndOversizedFiles() {
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> validator.validate(null));
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> validator.validate(new MockMultipartFile("files", new byte[0])));
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> validator.validate(
                new MockMultipartFile("files", new byte[ImageUploadValidator.MAX_FILE_BYTES + 1])));
    }

    @Test
    void checksActualStreamLengthEvenWhenReportedSizeIsFalse() {
        var dishonest = new MockMultipartFile("files", new byte[ImageUploadValidator.MAX_FILE_BYTES + 1]) {
            @Override public long getSize() { return 1; }
        };
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> validator.validate(dishonest));
    }

    @Test
    void removesAppendedPayload() throws Exception {
        byte[] original = fixture("valid.png");
        byte[] payload = "UNILEND_APPENDED_SCRIPT_123".getBytes(StandardCharsets.US_ASCII);
        ByteArrayOutputStream stream = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        stream.write(original);
        stream.write(payload);
        var result = validator.validate(new MockMultipartFile("files", stream.toByteArray()));
        assertFalse(new String(result.bytes(), StandardCharsets.ISO_8859_1).contains("UNILEND_APPENDED_SCRIPT_123"));
        assertNotNull(ImageIO.read(new ByteArrayInputStream(result.bytes())));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsExcessiveDimensions() throws Exception {
        var output = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
        ImageIO.write(new BufferedImage(8193, 1, BufferedImage.TYPE_INT_RGB), "png", output);
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> validator.validate(new MockMultipartFile("files", output.toByteArray())));
    }

    private byte[] fixture(String name) throws Exception {
        try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/upload/" + name)) {
            assertNotNull(in);
            return in.readAllBytes();
        }
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/ItemServiceUploadTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Item;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.ItemImage;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.*;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.io.TempDir;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.mock.web.MockMultipartFile;
import org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronization;
import org.springframework.transaction.support.TransactionSynchronizationManager;
import java.nio.file.Files;
import java.nio.file.Path;
import java.util.Collections;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class ItemServiceUploadTest {
    @Mock ItemRepository items;
    @Mock ItemImageRepository images;
    @Mock CategoryRepository categories;
    @Mock LoanRequestRepository loans;
    @Mock CurrentUserService currentUser;
    @TempDir Path uploadDir;
    ItemService service;
    Item item;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        service = new ItemService(items, images, categories, loans, currentUser, new ImageUploadValidator());
        ReflectionTestUtils.setField(service, "uploadDir", uploadDir.toString());
        User owner = User.builder().id(1L).fullName("Owner").build();
        item = Item.builder().id(10L).title("Book").owner(owner).build();
        when(items.findByIdForImageUpload(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(item));
        when(currentUser.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(owner);
        TransactionSynchronizationManager.initSynchronization();
    }

    @AfterEach
    void tearDown() {
        if (TransactionSynchronizationManager.isSynchronizationActive()) {
            TransactionSynchronizationManager.clearSynchronization();
        }
    }

    @Test
    void successfulCommitKeepsNormalizedFile() throws Exception {
        saveNormally();
        var response = service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png()));
        complete(TransactionSynchronization.STATUS_COMMITTED);
        assertEquals(1, response.images().size());
        assertEquals(1, physicalFiles());
        verify(images).flush();
    }

    @Test
    void saveFailureCleansNewFiles() throws Exception {
        when(images.save(any(ItemImage.class))).thenThrow(new IllegalStateException("database failure"));
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png())));
        assertEquals(0, physicalFiles());
    }

    @Test
    void flushFailureCleansWholeBatch() throws Exception {
        saveNormally();
        doThrow(new IllegalStateException("flush failure")).when(images).flush();
        assertThrows(IllegalStateException.class, () -> service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png(), png())));
        assertEquals(0, physicalFiles());
    }

    @Test
    void rollbackAfterMethodReturnsAlsoCleansFiles() throws Exception {
        saveNormally();
        service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png(), png()));
        assertEquals(2, physicalFiles());
        complete(TransactionSynchronization.STATUS_ROLLED_BACK);
        assertEquals(0, physicalFiles());
    }

    @Test
    void validatesEntireBatchBeforeWriting() throws Exception {
        var fake = new MockMultipartFile("files", "fake.jpg", "image/jpeg", "fake image".getBytes());
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png(), fake)));
        assertEquals(0, physicalFiles());
        verify(images, never()).save(any());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsBatchOverRemainingQuota() throws Exception {
        when(images.countByItemId(10L)).thenReturn(4L);
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png(), png())));
        assertEquals(0, physicalFiles());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsMoreThanFiveFiles() throws Exception {
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> service.uploadImages(10L, Collections.nCopies(6, png())));
        assertEquals(0, physicalFiles());
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsNonOwner() throws Exception {
        when(currentUser.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(User.builder().id(2L).build());
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png())));
        verify(images, never()).save(any());
    }

    @Test
    void storageFailureDoesNotDeleteUnrelatedFiles() throws Exception {
        Path blocker = uploadDir.resolve("items");
        Files.writeString(blocker, "existing file");
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png())));
        assertEquals("existing file", Files.readString(blocker));
        verify(images, never()).save(any());
    }

    @Test
    void rollbackPreservesFilesFromEarlierUploads() throws Exception {
        Files.createDirectories(uploadDir.resolve("items/10"));
        Path previous = uploadDir.resolve("items/10/previous.png");
        Files.writeString(previous, "previous upload");
        when(images.countByItemId(10L)).thenReturn(1L);
        saveNormally();
        service.uploadImages(10L, List.of(png()));
        complete(TransactionSynchronization.STATUS_ROLLED_BACK);
        assertEquals(1, physicalFiles());
        assertEquals("previous upload", Files.readString(previous));
    }

    @Test
    void rejectsEmptyBatch() {
        assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> service.uploadImages(10L, List.of()));
    }

    private void saveNormally() {
        when(images.save(any(ItemImage.class))).thenAnswer(invocation -> {
            ItemImage image = invocation.getArgument(0);
            image.setId((long) item.getImages().size() + 1);
            return image;
        });
    }

    private void complete(int status) {
        TransactionSynchronizationManager.getSynchronizations().forEach(sync -> sync.afterCompletion(status));
    }

    private long physicalFiles() throws Exception {
        try (var paths = Files.walk(uploadDir)) {
            return paths.filter(Files::isRegularFile).count();
        }
    }

    private MockMultipartFile png() throws Exception {
        try (var in = getClass().getResourceAsStream("/upload/valid.png")) {
            assertNotNull(in);
            return new MockMultipartFile("files", "photo.png", "image/png", in.readAllBytes());
        }
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/LoanRequestServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
@MockitoSettings(strictness = Strictness.LENIENT)
class LoanRequestServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private LoanRequestRepository loanRequestRepository;

    @Mock
    private ItemRepository itemRepository;

    @Mock
    private ReviewRepository reviewRepository;

    @Mock
    private EntityManager entityManager;

    @Mock
    private NotificationService notificationService;

    @InjectMocks
    private LoanRequestService service;

    private User owner;
    private User borrower;
    private Item item;
    private LocalDate today;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        owner = User.builder().id(1L).email("owner@unilend.test").fullName("Chá»§ Ä‘á»“").build();
        borrower = User.builder().id(2L).email("borrower@unilend.test").fullName("NgÆ°á»i mÆ°á»£n").build();
        item = Item.builder()
                .id(10L)
                .title("MÃ¡y tÃ­nh Casio")
                .owner(owner)
                .status(ItemStatus.AVAILABLE)
                .build();
        today = LocalDate.now(LoanRequestService.LOAN_ZONE);
    }

    @Test
    void create_success() {
        when(entityManager.find(Item.class, 10L, LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)).thenReturn(item);
        when(itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(item));
        when(loanRequestRepository.existsByItemIdAndBorrowerIdAndStatus(10L, 2L, LoanStatus.PENDING))
                .thenReturn(false);
        when(loanRequestRepository.existsOverlappingApproved(10L, today, today.plusDays(3)))
                .thenReturn(false);
        when(loanRequestRepository.save(any(LoanRequest.class))).thenAnswer(invocation -> {
            LoanRequest saved = invocation.getArgument(0);
            saved.setId(100L);
            return saved;
        });

        LoanRequestResponse response = service.create(borrower, createReq(10L, today, today.plusDays(3)));

        assertEquals(100L, response.id());
        assertEquals(LoanStatus.PENDING, response.status());
        assertEquals(10L, response.item().id());
        assertEquals(2L, response.borrower().id());
        assertEquals(1L, response.owner().id());
        verify(notificationService).notify(eq(1L), eq(NotificationType.LOAN_CREATED), anyString(), eq("/loans/100"));
    }

    @Test
    void create_ownItem_badRequest() {
        when(entityManager.find(Item.class, 10L, LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)).thenReturn(item);
        when(itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(item));

        assertCode(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                () -> service.create(owner, createReq(10L, today, today.plusDays(1))));
    }

    @Test
    void create_itemUnavailable_conflict() {
        item.setStatus(ItemStatus.UNAVAILABLE);
        when(entityManager.find(Item.class, 10L, LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)).thenReturn(item);
        when(itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(item));

        assertCode(ErrorCode.CONFLICT,
                () -> service.create(borrower, createReq(10L, today, today.plusDays(1))));
    }

    @Test
    void create_itemLentOut_futureDateAllowed() {
        item.setStatus(ItemStatus.LENT_OUT);
        LocalDate futureStart = today.plusDays(5);
        LocalDate futureEnd = today.plusDays(7);
        when(entityManager.find(Item.class, 10L, LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)).thenReturn(item);
        when(itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(item));
        when(loanRequestRepository.existsByItemIdAndBorrowerIdAndStatus(10L, 2L, LoanStatus.PENDING))
                .thenReturn(false);
        when(loanRequestRepository.existsOverlappingApproved(10L, futureStart, futureEnd))
                .thenReturn(false);
        when(loanRequestRepository.save(any(LoanRequest.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> {
            LoanRequest saved = inv.getArgument(0);
            saved.setId(101L);
            return saved;
        });

        LoanRequestResponse res = service.create(borrower, createReq(10L, futureStart, futureEnd));
        assertEquals(101L, res.id());
        assertEquals(LoanStatus.PENDING, res.status());
    }

    @Test
    void create_itemLentOut_todayDate_conflict() {

    @Test

    @Test

    @Test

    @Test

    @Test
    void create_itemNotFound_throwsResourceNotFound() {
        when(entityManager.find(Item.class, 999L, LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE)).thenReturn(null);

        assertThrows(ResourceNotFoundException.class,
                () -> service.create(borrower, createReq(999L, today, today.plusDays(1))));
    }

    @Test
    void approve_success_marksItemLentOutWhenStartingToday() {
        LoanRequest loan = givenLoan(LoanStatus.PENDING);

        LoanRequestResponse response = service.approve(100L, owner);

        assertEquals(LoanStatus.APPROVED, response.status());
        assertNotNull(loan.getRespondedAt());
        assertEquals(ItemStatus.LENT_OUT, item.getStatus());
        verify(loanRequestRepository).findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(100L);
        verify(entityManager).lock(item, LockModeType.PESSIMISTIC_WRITE);
        verify(entityManager).refresh(item);
        verify(notificationService).notify(eq(2L), eq(NotificationType.LOAN_APPROVED), anyString(), eq("/loans/100"));
    }

    @Test

        LoanRequest conflicting = LoanRequest.builder()
                .id(101L)
                .item(item)
                .borrower(borrower2)
                .status(LoanStatus.PENDING)
                .borrowFrom(today.plusDays(1))
                .dueDate(today.plusDays(4))
                .build();
        when(loanRequestRepository.findOverlappingPendingForUpdate(10L, 100L, today, today.plusDays(3)))
                .thenReturn(List.of(conflicting));

        LoanRequestResponse response = service.approve(100L, owner);

        assertEquals(LoanStatus.APPROVED, response.status());
        assertEquals(LoanStatus.REJECTED, conflicting.getStatus());
        assertNotNull(conflicting.getRespondedAt());
        verify(notificationService).notify(eq(3L), eq(NotificationType.LOAN_REJECTED), anyString(), eq("/loans/101"));
    }
