        service.getMyRequests(borrower, null, PageRequest.of(0, 1000));

        verify(loanRequestRepository).findByBorrowerId(eq(2L),
                org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.argThat(pageable -> pageable.getPageSize() == 50));
    }

    @Test
    void getMyHistory_filtersRequestDatesAndStatusForBorrower() {
        LocalDate from = LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 1);
        LocalDate to = LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 5);
        ZoneId zone = ZoneId.of("Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh");
        Instant expectedStart = from.atStartOfDay(zone).toInstant();
        Instant expectedEnd = to.plusDays(1).atStartOfDay(zone).toInstant();
        when(loanRequestRepository.findBorrowerHistory(
                eq(borrower.getId()), eq(LoanStatus.RETURNED),
                eq(expectedStart), eq(expectedEnd), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of()));

        var result = service.getMyHistory(borrower, LoanStatus.RETURNED, from, to, PageRequest.of(0, 10));

        assertEquals(0, result.totalElements());
        verify(loanRequestRepository).findBorrowerHistory(
                eq(borrower.getId()), eq(LoanStatus.RETURNED),
                eq(expectedStart), eq(expectedEnd), any(Pageable.class));
    }

    @Test
    void getIncomingHistory_allowsOpenEndedDateRange() {
        LocalDate from = LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 1);
        Instant expectedStart = from.atStartOfDay(ZoneId.of("Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh")).toInstant();
        when(loanRequestRepository.findOwnerHistory(
                eq(owner.getId()), eq(null), eq(expectedStart), eq(null), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of()));

        var result = service.getIncomingHistory(owner, null, from, null, PageRequest.of(0, 10));

        assertEquals(0, result.totalElements());
        verify(loanRequestRepository).findOwnerHistory(
                eq(owner.getId()), eq(null), eq(expectedStart), eq(null), any(Pageable.class));
    }

    @Test
    void getMyHistory_rejectsReversedDates() {
        var from = LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 6);
        var to = LocalDate.of(2026, 10, 5);
        assertCode(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST,
                () -> service.getMyHistory(borrower, null, from, to, PageRequest.of(0, 10)));
        verify(loanRequestRepository, org.mockito.Mockito.never()).findBorrowerHistory(
                any(), any(), any(), any(), any());
    }

    @Test
    void getById_returnedLoan_populatesReviewFlags() {
        givenLoan(LoanStatus.RETURNED);
        when(reviewRepository.existsByLoanIdAndReviewerId(100L, 2L)).thenReturn(false);

        LoanRequestResponse unreviewed = service.getById(100L, borrower);
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse(unreviewed.hasReviewed());
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue(unreviewed.canReview());

        when(reviewRepository.existsByLoanIdAndReviewerId(100L, 2L)).thenReturn(true);
        LoanRequestResponse reviewed = service.getById(100L, borrower);
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue(reviewed.hasReviewed());
        org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertFalse(reviewed.canReview());
    }

    private LoanRequestCreateRequest createReq(Long itemId, LocalDate from, LocalDate to) {
        return new LoanRequestCreateRequest(itemId, from, to, "MÆ°á»£n Ä‘á»ƒ há»c nhÃ³m");
    }

    private LoanRequest givenLoan(LoanStatus status) {
        LoanRequest loan = LoanRequest.builder()
                .id(100L)
                .item(item)
                .borrower(borrower)
                .status(status)
                .borrowFrom(today)
                .dueDate(today.plusDays(3))
                .build();
        when(loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsById(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(loan));
        when(loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(loan));
        return loan;
    }

    private void assertCode(ErrorCode expected, Executable action) {
        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class, action);
        assertEquals(expected, exception.getErrorCode());
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/NotificationServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.notification.NotificationResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Notification;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Notification.NotificationType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.NotificationRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageImpl;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;

import java.time.Instant;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.*;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class NotificationServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private NotificationRepository notificationRepository;

    @Mock
    private UserRepository userRepository;

    @InjectMocks
    private NotificationService notificationService;

    private User recipient;
    private User otherUser;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        recipient = User.builder().id(1L).email("user1@unilend.test").fullName("NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng 1").build();
        otherUser = User.builder().id(2L).email("user2@unilend.test").fullName("NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng 2").build();
    }

    @Test
    void notify_savesNotification() {
        when(userRepository.findById(1L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(recipient));
        when(notificationRepository.save(any(Notification.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> {
            Notification n = inv.getArgument(0);
            n.setId(50L);
            return n;
        });

        NotificationResponse res = notificationService.notify(
                1L, NotificationType.LOAN_CREATED, "CÃ³ yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n má»›i", "/loans/10");

        assertEquals(50L, res.id());
        assertEquals(1L, res.userId());
        assertEquals("LOAN_CREATED", res.type());
        assertEquals("CÃ³ yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n má»›i", res.message());
        assertEquals("/loans/10", res.link());
        assertFalse(res.read());
    }

    @Test
    void notify_userNotFound_throwsResourceNotFound() {
        when(userRepository.findById(99L)).thenReturn(Optional.empty());

        assertThrows(ResourceNotFoundException.class,
                () -> notificationService.notify(99L, "LOAN_CREATED", "Msg", "/loans/1"));
    }

    @Test
    void getMyNotifications_returnsPagedResults() {
        Notification n = Notification.builder()
                .id(10L)
                .user(recipient)
                .type("LOAN_APPROVED")
                .message("ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t")
                .link("/loans/5")
                .read(false)
                .build();
        when(notificationRepository.findByUserId(eq(1L), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of(n), PageRequest.of(0, 10), 1));

        PageResponse<NotificationResponse> page = notificationService.getMyNotifications(
                recipient, PageRequest.of(0, 10));

        assertEquals(1, page.content().size());
        assertEquals(10L, page.content().get(0).id());
        assertEquals("LOAN_APPROVED", page.content().get(0).type());
    }

    @Test
    void getUnreadCount_returnsRepositoryCount() {
        when(notificationRepository.countByUserIdAndReadFalse(1L)).thenReturn(4L);

        assertEquals(4L, notificationService.getUnreadCount(recipient));
    }

    @Test
    void markAsRead_marksUnreadNotification() {
        Notification n = Notification.builder()
                .id(10L)
                .user(recipient)
                .type("LOAN_APPROVED")
                .message("ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t")
                .read(false)
                .build();
        when(notificationRepository.findById(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(n));
        when(notificationRepository.save(any(Notification.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> inv.getArgument(0));

        NotificationResponse res = notificationService.markAsRead(10L, recipient);

        assertTrue(res.read());
        assertNotNull(res.readAt());
        verify(notificationRepository).save(n);
    }

    @Test
    void markAsRead_otherUsersNotification_forbidden() {
        Notification n = Notification.builder()
                .id(10L)
                .user(recipient)
                .type("LOAN_APPROVED")
                .message("ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t")
                .read(false)
                .build();
        when(notificationRepository.findById(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(n));

        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> notificationService.markAsRead(10L, otherUser));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void markAllAsRead_updatesAllForUser() {
        when(notificationRepository.markAllAsRead(eq(1L), any(Instant.class))).thenReturn(3);

        int updated = notificationService.markAllAsRead(recipient);

        assertEquals(3, updated);
        verify(notificationRepository).markAllAsRead(eq(1L), any(Instant.class));
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/RateLimitServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.test.util.ReflectionTestUtils;

import java.time.Duration;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertTrue;

class RateLimitServiceTest {

    @Test
    void limitsLoginAttemptsByNormalizedEmail() {
        RateLimitService rateLimitService = new RateLimitService();
        ReflectionTestUtils.setField(rateLimitService, "loginMaxAttempts", 2);
        ReflectionTestUtils.setField(rateLimitService, "loginWindowSeconds", 60L);

        rateLimitService.checkLoginRateLimit(" USER@example.test ");
        rateLimitService.checkLoginRateLimit("user@example.test");

        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> rateLimitService.checkLoginRateLimit("user@example.test"));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, exception.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void limitsEmailRequestsSeparatelyByActionAndAddress() {
        RateLimitService rateLimitService = new RateLimitService();
        ReflectionTestUtils.setField(rateLimitService, "emailMaxAttempts", 1);
        ReflectionTestUtils.setField(rateLimitService, "emailWindowSeconds", 900L);

        rateLimitService.checkEmailRateLimit("forgot-password", "user@example.test");
        rateLimitService.checkEmailRateLimit("resend-verify", "user@example.test");

        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> rateLimitService.checkEmailRateLimit("forgot-password", "USER@example.test"));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.TOO_MANY_REQUESTS, exception.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void checkAllowsNewAttemptAfterWindowExpires() {
        RateLimitService rateLimitService = new RateLimitService();

        rateLimitService.check("bucket", 1, Duration.ofMillis(-1), "rate limited");
        rateLimitService.check("bucket", 1, Duration.ofMillis(-1), "rate limited");
    }

    @Test
    void periodicallyRemovesExpiredBuckets() {
        RateLimitService rateLimitService = new RateLimitService();

        for (int i = 0; i < 256; i++) {
            rateLimitService.check("expired-" + i, 1, Duration.ofMillis(-1), "rate limited");
        }

        var buckets = (java.util.Map<?, ?>) ReflectionTestUtils.getField(rateLimitService, "buckets");
        assertTrue(buckets.isEmpty());
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/ReportServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.report.ReportCreateRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.report.ReportResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.report.ReportUpdateStatusRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Item;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Report;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Report.ReportReason;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Report.ReportStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Report.ReportTargetType;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ItemRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ReportRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import java.util.Optional;
import java.util.Set;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class ReportServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private ReportRepository reportRepository;

    @Mock
    private ItemRepository itemRepository;

    @Mock
    private UserRepository userRepository;

    @Mock
    private CurrentUserService currentUserService;

    @InjectMocks
    private ReportService reportService;

    private User reporter;
    private User owner;
    private User admin;
    private Item item;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        reporter = User.builder().id(1L).email("reporter@unilend.test").fullName("NgÆ°á»i bÃ¡o cÃ¡o").build();
        owner = User.builder().id(2L).email("owner@unilend.test").fullName("Chá»§ mÃ³n Ä‘á»“").build();
        admin = User.builder().id(99L).email("admin@unilend.test").fullName("Quáº£n trá»‹ viÃªn")
                .roles(Set.of("USER", "ADMIN")).build();
        item = Item.builder().id(10L).title("MÃ¡y chiáº¿u mini").owner(owner).build();
    }

    @Test
    void create_itemReport_success() {
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(reporter);
        when(itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(item));
        when(reportRepository.save(any(Report.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> {
            Report saved = inv.getArgument(0);
            saved.setId(100L);
            return saved;
        });

        ReportCreateRequest req = new ReportCreateRequest(
                ReportTargetType.ITEM, 10L, null, null, ReportReason.INAPPROPRIATE, "  Tin Ä‘Äƒng sai sá»± tháº­t  ");

        ReportResponse res = reportService.create(req);

        assertEquals(100L, res.id());
        assertEquals(ReportTargetType.ITEM, res.targetType());
        assertEquals(ReportStatus.OPEN, res.status());
        assertEquals(ReportReason.INAPPROPRIATE, res.reason());
        assertEquals("Tin Ä‘Äƒng sai sá»± tháº­t", res.description());
        assertEquals(10L, res.reportedItem().id());
        assertEquals(2L, res.reportedUser().id());
    }

    @Test
    void create_ownItem_throwsBadRequest() {
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(owner);
        when(itemRepository.findWithDetailsById(10L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(item));

        ReportCreateRequest req = new ReportCreateRequest(
                ReportTargetType.ITEM, 10L, null, null, ReportReason.SPAM, "Tá»± bÃ¡o cÃ¡o");

        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> reportService.create(req));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void create_selfUserReport_throwsBadRequest() {
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(reporter);

        ReportCreateRequest req = new ReportCreateRequest(
                ReportTargetType.USER, 1L, null, null, ReportReason.HARASSMENT, "Tá»± bÃ¡o cÃ¡o báº£n thÃ¢n");

        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> reportService.create(req));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void create_conflictingTargetIds_throwsBadRequest() {
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(reporter);

        ReportCreateRequest req = new ReportCreateRequest(
                ReportTargetType.ITEM, 10L, 11L, null, ReportReason.SPAM, "BÃ¡o cÃ¡o tin Ä‘Äƒng");

        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> reportService.create(req));

        assertEquals(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void create_targetTypeConflictsWithLegacyId_throwsBadRequest() {
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(reporter);

        ReportCreateRequest req = new ReportCreateRequest(
                ReportTargetType.ITEM, 10L, null, 2L, ReportReason.SPAM, "BÃ¡o cÃ¡o tin Ä‘Äƒng");

        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class, () -> reportService.create(req));

        assertEquals(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void updateStatus_toResolved_setsResolvedAtAndResolvedBy() {
        Report report = Report.builder()
                .id(100L)
                .reporter(reporter)
                .targetType(ReportTargetType.USER)
                .reportedUser(owner)
                .reason(ReportReason.FRAUD)
                .description("Lá»«a Ä‘áº£o")
                .status(ReportStatus.OPEN)
                .build();

        when(currentUserService.requireAdmin()).thenReturn(admin);
        when(reportRepository.findWithDetailsById(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(report));
        when(reportRepository.save(any(Report.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> inv.getArgument(0));

        ReportResponse res = reportService.updateStatus(
                100L, new ReportUpdateStatusRequest(ReportStatus.RESOLVED, "ÄÃ£ kiá»ƒm tra vÃ  khÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n"));

        assertEquals(ReportStatus.RESOLVED, res.status());
        assertEquals("ÄÃ£ kiá»ƒm tra vÃ  khÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n", res.resolutionNote());
        assertNotNull(res.resolvedAt());
        assertEquals(99L, res.resolvedBy().id());
    }

    @Test
    void updateStatus_toInReview_clearsResolutionDetails() {
        Report report = Report.builder()
                .id(100L)
                .reporter(reporter)
                .targetType(ReportTargetType.USER)
                .reportedUser(owner)
                .reason(ReportReason.FRAUD)
                .description("Lá»«a Ä‘áº£o")
                .status(ReportStatus.RESOLVED)
                .resolvedBy(admin)
                .resolvedAt(java.time.Instant.now())
                .build();

        when(currentUserService.requireAdmin()).thenReturn(admin);
        when(reportRepository.findWithDetailsById(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(report));
        when(reportRepository.save(any(Report.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> inv.getArgument(0));

        ReportResponse res = reportService.updateStatus(
                100L, new ReportUpdateStatusRequest(ReportStatus.IN_REVIEW, null));

        assertEquals(ReportStatus.IN_REVIEW, res.status());
        assertNull(res.resolvedBy());
        assertNull(res.resolvedAt());
    }
}
===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/ReviewServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ResourceNotFoundException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.response.PageResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.review.ReviewRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.review.ReviewResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Item;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.LoanRequest.LoanStatus;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.Review;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.LoanRequestRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.ReviewRepository;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageImpl;
import org.springframework.data.domain.PageRequest;
import org.springframework.data.domain.Pageable;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Optional;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.*;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class ReviewServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private ReviewRepository reviewRepository;

    @Mock
    private LoanRequestRepository loanRequestRepository;

    @Mock
    private UserRepository userRepository;

    @InjectMocks
    private ReviewService reviewService;

    private User owner;
    private User borrower;
    private User stranger;
    private Item item;
    private LoanRequest returnedLoan;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        owner = User.builder().id(1L).email("owner@unilend.test").fullName("Chá»§ Ä‘á»“").build();
        borrower = User.builder().id(2L).email("borrower@unilend.test").fullName("NgÆ°á»i mÆ°á»£n").build();
        stranger = User.builder().id(3L).email("stranger@unilend.test").fullName("NgÆ°á»i láº¡").build();
        item = Item.builder().id(10L).title("MÃ¡y tÃ­nh Casio").owner(owner).build();
        returnedLoan = LoanRequest.builder()
                .id(100L)
                .item(item)
                .borrower(borrower)
                .status(LoanStatus.RETURNED)
                .build();
    }

    @Test
    void createReview_byBorrower_reviewsOwner() {
        when(loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(returnedLoan));
        when(reviewRepository.existsByLoanIdAndReviewerId(100L, 2L)).thenReturn(false);
        when(reviewRepository.save(any(Review.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> {
            Review r = inv.getArgument(0);
            r.setId(500L);
            return r;
        });

        ReviewResponse res = reviewService.createReview(100L, borrower, new ReviewRequest(5, "Äá»“ ráº¥t tá»‘t!"));

        assertEquals(500L, res.id());
        assertEquals(100L, res.loanId());
        assertEquals(5, res.rating());
        assertEquals("Äá»“ ráº¥t tá»‘t!", res.comment());
        assertEquals(2L, res.reviewer().id());
        assertEquals(1L, res.reviewee().id());
    }

    @Test
    void createReview_byOwner_reviewsBorrower() {
        when(loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(returnedLoan));
        when(reviewRepository.existsByLoanIdAndReviewerId(100L, 1L)).thenReturn(false);
        when(reviewRepository.save(any(Review.class))).thenAnswer(inv -> {
            Review r = inv.getArgument(0);
            r.setId(501L);
            return r;
        });

        ReviewResponse res = reviewService.createReview(100L, owner, new ReviewRequest(4, "Tráº£ Ä‘Ãºng háº¹n"));

        assertEquals(501L, res.id());
        assertEquals(4, res.rating());
        assertEquals(1L, res.reviewer().id());
        assertEquals(2L, res.reviewee().id());
    }

    @Test
    void createReview_loanNotReturned_conflict() {
        returnedLoan.setStatus(LoanStatus.APPROVED);
        when(loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(returnedLoan));

        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> reviewService.createReview(100L, borrower, new ReviewRequest(5, "Ok")));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void createReview_duplicateBySameSide_conflict() {
        when(loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(returnedLoan));
        when(reviewRepository.existsByLoanIdAndReviewerId(100L, 2L)).thenReturn(true);

        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> reviewService.createReview(100L, borrower, new ReviewRequest(5, "ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ láº§n 2")));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.CONFLICT, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void createReview_byNonParticipant_forbidden() {
        when(loanRequestRepository.findWithDetailsByIdForUpdate(100L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(returnedLoan));

        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> reviewService.createReview(100L, stranger, new ReviewRequest(5, "NgÆ°á»i láº¡")));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void createReview_invalidRating_badRequest() {
        BusinessException ex = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> reviewService.createReview(100L, borrower, new ReviewRequest(6, "QuÃ¡ 5 sao")));
        assertEquals(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, ex.getErrorCode());
    }

    @Test
    void getReviewsByUserId_returnsPagedReviews() {
        when(userRepository.existsById(1L)).thenReturn(true);
        Review review = Review.builder()
                .id(500L)
                .loan(returnedLoan)
                .reviewer(borrower)
                .reviewee(owner)
                .rating(5)
                .comment("Ráº¥t nhiá»‡t tÃ¬nh")
                .build();
        when(reviewRepository.findByRevieweeId(eq(1L), any(Pageable.class)))
                .thenReturn(new PageImpl<>(List.of(review), PageRequest.of(0, 10), 1));

        PageResponse<ReviewResponse> page = reviewService.getReviewsByUserId(1L, PageRequest.of(0, 10));

        assertEquals(1, page.content().size());
        assertEquals(5, page.content().get(0).rating());
        assertEquals("Ráº¥t nhiá»‡t tÃ¬nh", page.content().get(0).comment());
    }

    @Test
    void getReviewsByUserId_userNotFound_throwsResourceNotFound() {
        when(userRepository.existsById(999L)).thenReturn(false);

        assertThrows(ResourceNotFoundException.class,
                () -> reviewService.getReviewsByUserId(999L, PageRequest.of(0, 10)));
    }

    @Test
    void getAverageRating_roundsToOneDecimal() {
        when(reviewRepository.getAverageRatingByUserId(1L)).thenReturn(4.6666667);

        assertEquals(4.7, reviewService.getAverageRating(1L));
    }
}

===== backend/src/test/java/com/unilend/backend/service/UserServiceTest.java =====
package com.unilend.backend.service;

import com.unilend.backend.common.security.CurrentUserService;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.BusinessException;
import com.unilend.backend.common.exception.ErrorCode;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.user.ChangePasswordRequest;
import com.unilend.backend.dto.user.UserProfileResponse;
import com.unilend.backend.entity.User;
import com.unilend.backend.repository.UserRepository;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.password.PasswordEncoder;

import java.util.Optional;

import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertEquals;
import static org.junit.jupiter.api.Assertions.assertThrows;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.never;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
class UserServiceTest {

    @Mock
    private UserRepository userRepository;

    @Mock
    private CurrentUserService currentUserService;

    @Mock
    private PasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;

    @Mock
    private ReviewService reviewService;

    @InjectMocks
    private UserService userService;

    private User user;

    @BeforeEach
    void setUp() {
        user = User.builder()
                .id(1L)
                .email("user@unilend.test")
                .fullName("Nguyá»…n VÄƒn A")
                .university("ÄH BÃ¡ch Khoa")
                .build();
    }

    @Test
    void getById_includesAverageRatingAndReviewCount() {
        when(userRepository.findById(1L)).thenReturn(Optional.of(user));
        when(reviewService.getAverageRating(1L)).thenReturn(4.5);
        when(reviewService.getReviewCount(1L)).thenReturn(8L);

        UserProfileResponse profile = userService.getById(1L);

        assertEquals(1L, profile.id());
        assertEquals("Nguyá»…n VÄƒn A", profile.fullName());
        assertEquals(4.5, profile.averageRating());
        assertEquals(8L, profile.reviewCount());
    }

    @Test
    void getMyProfile_includesAverageRatingAndReviewCount() {
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(user);
        when(reviewService.getAverageRating(1L)).thenReturn(5.0);
        when(reviewService.getReviewCount(1L)).thenReturn(2L);

        UserProfileResponse profile = userService.getMyProfile();

        assertEquals(1L, profile.id());
        assertEquals(5.0, profile.averageRating());
        assertEquals(2L, profile.reviewCount());
    }

    @Test
    void changeMyPasswordUsesEncoderAndInvalidatesExistingTokens() {
        user.setPassword("encoded-old-password");
        user.setTokenVersion(4);
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(user);
        when(passwordEncoder.matches("old-password", "encoded-old-password")).thenReturn(true);
        when(passwordEncoder.encode("new-password")).thenReturn("encoded-new-password");

        userService.changeMyPassword(new ChangePasswordRequest("old-password", "new-password"));

        assertEquals("encoded-new-password", user.getPassword());
        assertEquals(5, user.getTokenVersion());
        verify(userRepository).save(user);
    }

    @Test
    void changeMyPasswordRejectsPlaintextStoredPasswordWithoutDirectComparison() {
        user.setPassword("legacy-plaintext");
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(user);
        when(passwordEncoder.matches("legacy-plaintext", "legacy-plaintext")).thenReturn(false);

        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> userService.changeMyPassword(
                        new ChangePasswordRequest("legacy-plaintext", "new-password")));

        assertEquals(ErrorCode.BAD_REQUEST, exception.getErrorCode());
        verify(userRepository, never()).save(user);
    }

    @Test
    void changeMyPasswordRejectsDisabledAccount() {
        user.setEnabled(false);
        when(currentUserService.getCurrentUser()).thenReturn(user);

        BusinessException exception = assertThrows(BusinessException.class,
                () -> userService.changeMyPassword(new ChangePasswordRequest("old", "new-pass")));

        assertEquals(ErrorCode.FORBIDDEN, exception.getErrorCode());
        verify(passwordEncoder, never()).matches(org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyString(),
                org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyString());
        verify(userRepository, never()).save(user);
    }
}

===== backend/docker-compose.yml =====
services:
  db:
    image: mysql:8.4
    restart: unless-stopped
    environment:
      MYSQL_DATABASE: ${MYSQL_DATABASE:-uni_lend}
      MYSQL_USER: ${DB_USERNAME:-unilend}
      MYSQL_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD:?Set DB_PASSWORD in backend/.env}
      MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD: ${MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD:?Set MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD in backend/.env}
    volumes:
      - mysql-data:/var/lib/mysql
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD-SHELL", "mysqladmin ping -h 127.0.0.1 -u root -p\"$$MYSQL_ROOT_PASSWORD\" --silent"]
      interval: 5s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 20

  backend:
    build: .
    restart: unless-stopped
    env_file:
      - .env
    environment:
      DB_URL: jdbc:mysql://db:3306/${MYSQL_DATABASE:-uni_lend}
      DB_USERNAME: ${DB_USERNAME:-unilend}
      DB_PASSWORD: ${DB_PASSWORD:?Set DB_PASSWORD in backend/.env}
      JWT_SECRET: ${JWT_SECRET:?Set JWT_SECRET in backend/.env}
      JWT_EXPIRATION_MS: ${JWT_EXPIRATION_MS:-86400000}
      CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS: ${CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS:-http://localhost:3000}
      UPLOAD_DIR: /app/uploads
    depends_on:
      db:
        condition: service_healthy
    ports:
      - "8080:8080"
    volumes:
      - uploads:/app/uploads

volumes:
  mysql-data:
  uploads:

===== backend/DOCKER.md =====
# Run the backend with Docker Compose

From this directory, create the local environment file and set private values:

```powershell
Copy-Item .env.example .env
```

Edit `.env`: change both database passwords and replace `JWT_SECRET` with a Base64
secret generated by `openssl rand -base64 32`. Do not commit `.env`.

To send verification and password-reset emails, set `MAIL_ENABLED=true` and provide
`SMTP_HOST`, `SMTP_PORT`, `SMTP_USERNAME`, `SMTP_PASSWORD`, `SMTP_AUTH`, and
`SMTP_STARTTLS`. The defaults keep email delivery disabled for local development.
Login and email-request rate limits are configurable with the `RATE_LIMIT_*`
variables in `.env.example`; the current limiter is in-memory and applies per
backend process, so multi-instance deployments need a shared rate-limit store.

Start the API and MySQL:

```sh
docker compose up --build
```

The API listens on `http://localhost:8080`. Swagger UI is available at
`http://localhost:8080/swagger-ui/index.html`; the OpenAPI document is at
`http://localhost:8080/v3/api-docs`.

Persistent Docker volumes keep MySQL data and uploaded item images across restarts.

===== docs/API-FRONTEND.md =====
# Uni-Lend API guide for frontend

This inventory describes the backend API in the current workspace branch,
`feature/be-session-after-reset`. The branch has not been merged into `dev` yet.
Recheck the merged code and update the availability status after each backend
branch merge.

## API base and response format

- Local base URL: `http://localhost:8080`
- Prefer paths with the `/api` prefix.
- Successful responses use an `ApiResponse` envelope:

  ```json
  {
    "success": true,
    "message": "Success",
    "data": {},
    "timestamp": "2026-10-22T00:00:00Z"
  }
  ```

- Error responses include `success: false`, `errorCode`, and `message`;
  validation errors may also include `errors`.
- Paginated endpoints return `data` with `content`, `page`, `size`,
  `totalElements`, `totalPages`, and `last`.
- Send authenticated requests with `Authorization: Bearer <token>`.
- A disabled account cannot log in or use authenticated endpoints.
- A `429 TOO_MANY_REQUESTS` response means the login or email-request rate
  limit was reached. Limits are configurable by the backend.

## Authentication and account security

| Method | Path | Access | Request / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST` | `/api/auth/register` | Public | JSON: `email`, `password`, `fullName`; returns account data and a bearer token |
| `POST` | `/api/auth/login` | Public | JSON: `email`, `password`; returns account data and a bearer token |
| `POST` | `/api/auth/verify-email` | Public | JSON: `token` |
| `POST` | `/api/auth/resend-verification` | Public | JSON: `email`; response does not disclose whether the account exists |
| `POST` | `/api/auth/forgot-password` | Public | JSON: `email`; response does not disclose whether the account exists |
| `POST` | `/api/auth/reset-password` | Public | JSON: `token`, `newPassword` |
| `GET` | `/api/v1/users/me` | Authenticated | Current account summary |

Authentication responses include `tokenType: "Bearer"` and `token`. Email
verification tokens are valid for 24 hours by default; password-reset tokens
are valid for 15 minutes by default. Both are single-use.

### Session behavior relevant to frontend

- After a successful password reset or `PATCH /api/users/me/password`, discard
  the current bearer token and require the user to log in again. The backend
  increments the account token version, so existing tokens stop working.
- Admin lock and unlock actions also invalidate existing tokens. After an
  account is unlocked, the user must log in again to get a fresh token.
- There is no logout endpoint. For logout, remove the token from frontend
  storage; the token otherwise remains valid until expiry or version
  invalidation.
- If an authenticated request returns `401`, clear the stored token and send
  the user to sign in. A `403` can indicate a disabled account or insufficient
  role; show the backend message.

## User profiles

| Method | Path | Access | Request / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/api/users/me` | Authenticated | Current user's profile |
| `GET` | `/api/users/{id}` | Authenticated | View another user's profile |
| `PUT` | `/api/users/me` | Authenticated | JSON: `fullName`, `phone`, `university`, `avatarUrl` |
| `PATCH` | `/api/users/me/password` | Authenticated | JSON: `oldPassword`, `newPassword`; requires current password and invalidates existing tokens |
| `GET` | `/api/users/{id}/reviews?page=0&size=10` | Public | Paginated reviews for a user |

## Admin APIs

| Method | Path | Access | Request / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/api/admin/stats` | Admin | Dashboard statistics |
| `GET` | `/api/admin/users?keyword=&enabled=&page=0&size=10` | Admin | Search/filter users; `enabled` accepts `true` or `false` |
| `PATCH` | `/api/admin/users/{id}/lock` | Admin | Locks a user and invalidates existing tokens |
| `PATCH` | `/api/admin/users/{id}/unlock` | Admin | Unlocks a user; previous tokens remain invalid |

## Categories

| Method | Path | Access | Request / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/api/categories?page=0&size=10` | Public | Paginated category list |
| `GET` | `/api/categories/{id}` | Public | Category details |
| `POST` | `/api/categories` | Admin | JSON: `name`, `description` |
| `PUT` | `/api/categories/{id}` | Admin | Same body as create |
| `DELETE` | `/api/categories/{id}` | Admin | Deletes a category |

## Items and item images

| Method | Path | Access | Request / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/api/items?keyword=&categoryId=&status=&page=0&size=10` | Public | Search and paginate items |
| `GET` | `/api/items/{id}` | Public | Item details, including image data |
| `GET` | `/api/items/me?page=0&size=10` | Authenticated | Current user's items |
| `POST` | `/api/items` | Authenticated | JSON item body: `title`, `description`, optional `categoryId`, optional `status` |
| `PUT` | `/api/items/{id}` | Owner | Same JSON body; cannot manually set `LENT_OUT` |
| `DELETE` | `/api/items/{id}` | Owner | Fails if the item has loan requests |
| `POST` | `/api/items/{id}/images` | Owner | `multipart/form-data`; image parts named `files` or `images` |
| `DELETE` | `/api/items/{id}/images/{imageId}` | Owner | Deletes one image |
| `GET` | `/uploads/**` | Public | Loads a stored image |

## Loan requests and reviews

Loan status values are `PENDING`, `APPROVED`, `REJECTED`, `CANCELLED`,
`OVERDUE`, and `RETURNED`.

| Method | Path | Access | Request / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `POST` | `/api/loan-requests` | Authenticated | JSON: `itemId`, `borrowFrom`, `dueDate`, optional `note` |
| `GET` | `/api/loan-requests/mine?status=&page=0&size=10` | Authenticated | Current user's outgoing requests |
| `GET` | `/api/loan-requests/incoming?status=&page=0&size=10` | Authenticated | Requests for items owned by current user |
| `GET` | `/api/loan-requests/overdue?page=0&size=10` | Admin | Paginated overdue requests |
| `GET` | `/api/loan-requests/{id}` | Participant or admin | Request details |
| `PATCH` | `/api/loan-requests/{id}/approve` | Item owner | Approve a pending request |
| `PATCH` | `/api/loan-requests/{id}/reject` | Item owner | Reject a pending request |
| `PATCH` | `/api/loan-requests/{id}/cancel` | Request borrower | Cancel a pending request |
| `PATCH` | `/api/loan-requests/{id}/return` | Item owner | Confirm return of an approved loan |
| `POST` | `/api/loans/{id}/reviews` | Authenticated participant | JSON review body |
| `GET` | `/api/loans/{id}/reviews` | Public | Reviews for a loan |

The loan and review controllers also accept the `/api/loans` or
`/api/loan-requests` route aliases where applicable.

## Notifications

| Method | Path | Access | Request / notes |
|---|---|---|---|
| `GET` | `/api/notifications?page=0&size=10` | Authenticated | Current user's notifications |
| `GET` | `/api/notifications/unread-count` | Authenticated | Returns the unread count |
| `PATCH` | `/api/notifications/{id}/read` | Authenticated | Marks one notification as read |
| `PATCH` | `/api/notifications/read-all` | Authenticated | Marks all notifications as read |

## Email delivery note

Verification and password-reset email delivery is controlled by backend
environment configuration. If `MAIL_ENABLED=false` (the default), the backend
does not send email; configure SMTP and enable mail in the backend environment
for those flows to deliver messages.

===== frontend/app/admin/categories/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AdminNavigation } from "../../../components/admin-navigation";
import { CategoryForm } from "../../../components/category-form";
import { ConfirmModal } from "../../../components/confirm-modal";
import { useAdminToast } from "../../../components/admin-toast";
import { useAuth } from "../../../components/auth-provider";
import {
  categoryService,
  type CategoryInput,
} from "../../../lib/api/categoryService";
import { ApiClientError } from "../../../lib/api/apiClient";
import type { Category, PageResponse } from "../../../lib/api/types";
import styles from "../admin-page.module.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

function formatDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())
    ? "â€”"
    : new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", { dateStyle: "medium" }).format(date);
}

function isAuthorizationFailure(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ApiClientError &&
    (error.statusCode === 401 || error.statusCode === 403);
}

export default function AdminCategoriesPage() {
  const auth = useAuth();
  const { showToast } = useAdminToast();
  const [result, setResult] = useState<PageResponse<Category> | null>(null);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
  const [editingCategory, setEditingCategory] = useState<Category | null>(null);
  const [formOpen, setFormOpen] = useState(false);
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [deletingCategory, setDeletingCategory] = useState<Category | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const savingRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      mountedRef.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    let active = true;
    async function loadCategories() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        const response = await categoryService.list(
          { page, size: PAGE_SIZE },
          { signal: controller.signal },
        );
        if (active) setResult(response);
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (!active || controller.signal.aborted || isAuthorizationFailure(requestError)) {
          return;
        }
        setError(
          requestError instanceof Error
            ? requestError.message
            : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i danh sÃ¡ch danh má»¥c.",
        );
      } finally {
        if (active && !controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadCategories();
    return () => {
      active = false;
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [page, retry]);

  const retryLoading = useCallback(() => setRetry((current) => current + 1), []);
  const categories = result?.content ?? [];

  function openCreateDialog() {
    setEditingCategory(null);
    setFormError("");
    setFormOpen(true);
  }

  function openEditDialog(category: Category) {
    setEditingCategory(category);
    setFormError("");
    setFormOpen(true);
  }

  function closeFormDialog() {
    if (saving) return;
    setFormOpen(false);
    setFormError("");
  }

  async function handleSave(input: CategoryInput) {
    if (savingRef.current) return;
    if (auth.status !== "authenticated" || !auth.hasRole("ADMIN")) return;

    const categoryBeingEdited = editingCategory;
    savingRef.current = true;
    setSaving(true);
    setFormError("");
    try {
      if (categoryBeingEdited) {
        const updated = await categoryService.update(categoryBeingEdited.id, input);
        if (!mountedRef.current) return;
        setResult((current) =>
          current
            ? {
                ...current,
                content: current.content
                  .map((category) => category.id === updated.id ? updated : category)
                  .sort((left, right) => left.name.localeCompare(right.name, "vi")),
              }
            : current,
        );
        showToast("success", `ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t danh má»¥c ${updated.name}.`);
      } else {
        const created = await categoryService.create(input);
        if (!mountedRef.current) return;
        setPage(0);
        setResult((current) => {
          if (!current || page !== 0) return current;
          return {
            ...current,
            content: [...current.content, created]
              .sort((left, right) => left.name.localeCompare(right.name, "vi"))
              .slice(0, PAGE_SIZE),
            totalElements: current.totalElements + 1,
          };
        });
        showToast("success", `ÄÃ£ thÃªm danh má»¥c ${created.name}.`);
      }
      setFormOpen(false);
      setEditingCategory(null);
      setRetry((current) => current + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      if (!mountedRef.current || isAuthorizationFailure(requestError)) return;
      const action = categoryBeingEdited ? "cáº­p nháº­t" : "thÃªm";
      const detail = requestError instanceof Error
        ? requestError.message
        : "Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.";
      const message = `KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ ${action} danh má»¥c ${input.name}. ${detail}`;
      setFormError(message);
      showToast("error", message);
    } finally {
      savingRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  async function handleDelete() {
    if (!deletingCategory || savingRef.current) return;
    if (auth.status !== "authenticated" || !auth.hasRole("ADMIN")) return;

    const categoryToDelete = deletingCategory;
    savingRef.current = true;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      await categoryService.delete(categoryToDelete.id);
      if (!mountedRef.current) return;
      setResult((current) =>
        current
          ? {
              ...current,
              content: current.content.filter(
                (category) => category.id !== categoryToDelete.id,
              ),
              totalElements: Math.max(0, current.totalElements - 1),
            }
          : current,
      );
      showToast("success", `ÄÃ£ xÃ³a danh má»¥c ${categoryToDelete.name}.`);
      setDeletingCategory(null);
      const remainingElements = Math.max(0, (result?.totalElements ?? 1) - 1);
      const lastPage = Math.max(0, Math.ceil(remainingElements / PAGE_SIZE) - 1);
      setPage((current) => Math.min(current, lastPage));
      setRetry((current) => current + 1);
    } catch (requestError) {
      if (!mountedRef.current || isAuthorizationFailure(requestError)) return;
      const detail = requestError instanceof Error
        ? requestError.message
        : "Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.";
      showToast(
        "error",
        `KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ³a danh má»¥c ${categoryToDelete.name}. ${detail}`,
      );
    } finally {
      savingRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  const pageCount = result?.totalPages ?? 0;

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <AdminNavigation />
      <section className={styles.content} aria-labelledby="categories-title">
        <div className={styles.heading}>
          <div>
            <span className={styles.eyebrow}>Quáº£n trá»‹ ná»™i dung</span>
            <h1 id="categories-title">Danh má»¥c</h1>
            <p>Táº¡o vÃ  cáº­p nháº­t danh má»¥c dÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ phÃ¢n loáº¡i mÃ³n Ä‘á»“.</p>
          </div>
          <button
            className={styles.button}
            onClick={openCreateDialog}
            type="button"
          >
            + ThÃªm danh má»¥c
          </button>
        </div>

        {!error && result && (
          <p className={styles.count} aria-live="polite">
            {result.totalElements} danh má»¥c
          </p>
        )}

        {error ? (
          <div className={styles.state}>
            <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{error}</p>
            <button className={styles.button} onClick={retryLoading} type="button">
              Thá»­ láº¡i
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : loading ? (
          <div
            className={styles.tableWrap}
            aria-label="Äang táº£i danh má»¥c"
            aria-busy="true"
            role="status"
          >
            {Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, index) => (
              <div className={styles.skeleton} key={index} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : categories.length ? (
          <>
            <div className={styles.tableWrap}>
              <table
                className={`${styles.table} admin-categories-table`}
                aria-label="Danh sÃ¡ch danh má»¥c"
              >
                <caption className={styles.visuallyHidden}>Danh sÃ¡ch danh má»¥c</caption>
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th scope="col">TÃªn danh má»¥c</th>
                    <th scope="col">MÃ´ táº£</th>
                    <th scope="col">NgÃ y táº¡o</th>
                    <th scope="col"><span className={styles.visuallyHidden}>Thao tÃ¡c</span></th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {categories.map((category) => (
                    <tr key={category.id}>
                      <td><span className={styles.primaryText}>{category.name}</span></td>
                      <td>
                        {category.description || (
                          <span className={styles.secondaryText}>ChÆ°a cÃ³ mÃ´ táº£</span>
                        )}
                      </td>
                      <td>{formatDate(category.createdAt)}</td>
                      <td>
                        <div className={styles.actions}>
                          <button
                            className={styles.secondaryButton}
                            onClick={() => openEditDialog(category)}
                            type="button"
                          >
                            Sá»­a
                          </button>
                          <button
                            className={styles.dangerButton}
                            onClick={() => setDeletingCategory(category)}
                            type="button"
                          >
                            XÃ³a
                          </button>
                        </div>
                      </td>
                    </tr>
                  ))}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            {pageCount > 1 && (
              <nav className={styles.pagination} aria-label="PhÃ¢n trang danh má»¥c">
                <button
                  disabled={page === 0}
                  onClick={() => setPage((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1))}
                  type="button"
                >
                  â† TrÆ°á»›c
                </button>
                <span>Trang {page + 1} / {pageCount}</span>
                <button
                  disabled={page >= pageCount - 1}
                  onClick={() => setPage((current) => Math.min(pageCount - 1, current + 1))}
                  type="button"
                >
                  Sau â†’
                </button>
              </nav>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="empty-state" role="status">
            <h3>ChÆ°a cÃ³ danh má»¥c</h3>
            <p>HÃ£y thÃªm danh má»¥c Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn Ä‘á»ƒ phÃ¢n loáº¡i mÃ³n Ä‘á»“.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {formOpen && (
        <CategoryForm
          key={editingCategory?.id ?? "new-category"}
          category={editingCategory}
          categories={categories}
          isSaving={saving}
          onCancel={closeFormDialog}
          onSave={(input) => void handleSave(input)}
          serverError={formError}
        />
      )}

      {deletingCategory && (
        <ConfirmModal
          confirmLabel="XÃ¡c nháº­n xÃ³a"
          description={
            <>
              Báº¡n cÃ³ cháº¯c muá»‘n xÃ³a danh má»¥c <strong>{deletingCategory.name}</strong>? Náº¿u danh
              má»¥c Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ sá»­ dá»¥ng, mÃ¡y chá»§ cÃ³ thá»ƒ tá»« chá»‘i thao tÃ¡c nÃ y.
            </>
          }
          destructive
          isPending={saving}
          onCancel={() => {
            if (!saving) setDeletingCategory(null);
          }}
          onConfirm={() => void handleDelete()}
          pendingLabel="Äang xÃ³a..."
          title="XÃ³a danh má»¥c?"
        />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

===== frontend/app/admin/users/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { AdminNavigation } from "../../../components/admin-navigation";
import { ConfirmModal } from "../../../components/confirm-modal";
import { useAdminToast } from "../../../components/admin-toast";
import { useAuth } from "../../../components/auth-provider";
import { adminUserService, type AdminUser } from "../../../lib/api/adminUserService";
import { ApiClientError } from "../../../lib/api/apiClient";
import type { PageResponse } from "../../../lib/api/types";
import styles from "../admin-page.module.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;

function formatDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())
    ? "â€”"
    : new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", { dateStyle: "medium" }).format(date);
}

function isAuthorizationFailure(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ApiClientError &&
    (error.statusCode === 401 || error.statusCode === 403);
}

export default function AdminUsersPage() {
  const auth = useAuth();
  const { showToast } = useAdminToast();
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<PageResponse<AdminUser> | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
  const [pendingUser, setPendingUser] = useState<AdminUser | null>(null);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const mountedRef = useRef(true);
  const savingRef = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    mountedRef.current = true;
    return () => {
      mountedRef.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    const timeoutId = window.setTimeout(() => {
      setSearch(query.trim());
      setPage(0);
    }, 350);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(timeoutId);
  }, [query]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (auth.status !== "authenticated" || !auth.hasRole("ADMIN")) return;

    const controller = new AbortController();
    let active = true;
    async function loadUsers() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        const response = await adminUserService.list(
          { keyword: search || undefined, page, size: PAGE_SIZE },
          { signal: controller.signal },
        );
        if (active) setResult(response);
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (!active || controller.signal.aborted || isAuthorizationFailure(requestError)) {
          return;
        }
        setError(
          requestError instanceof Error
            ? requestError.message
            : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i danh sÃ¡ch ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng.",
        );
      } finally {
        if (active && !controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadUsers();
    return () => {
      active = false;
      controller.abort();
    };
  }, [auth, page, retry, search]);

  const retryLoading = useCallback(() => setRetry((current) => current + 1), []);
  const clearSearch = useCallback(() => setQuery(""), []);

  async function confirmToggleStatus() {
    if (!pendingUser || savingRef.current) return;
    if (auth.status !== "authenticated" || !auth.hasRole("ADMIN")) {
      setPendingUser(null);
      return;
    }

    savingRef.current = true;
    setSaving(true);
    const wasEnabled = pendingUser.enabled;
    try {
      const updatedUser = await adminUserService.setEnabled(
        pendingUser.id,
        !wasEnabled,
      );
      if (!mountedRef.current) return;
      setResult((current) =>
        current
          ? {
              ...current,
              content: current.content.map((user) =>
                user.id === updatedUser.id ? updatedUser : user,
              ),
            }
          : current,
      );
      showToast(
        "success",
        `ÄÃ£ ${updatedUser.enabled ? "má»Ÿ khÃ³a" : "khÃ³a"} tÃ i khoáº£n ${updatedUser.email}.`,
      );
      setPendingUser(null);
    } catch (requestError) {
      if (!mountedRef.current || isAuthorizationFailure(requestError)) return;
      const action = wasEnabled ? "khÃ³a" : "má»Ÿ khÃ³a";
      const detail = requestError instanceof Error
        ? requestError.message
        : "Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.";
      showToast(
        "error",
        `KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ ${action} tÃ i khoáº£n ${pendingUser.email}. ${detail}`,
      );
    } finally {
      savingRef.current = false;
      if (mountedRef.current) setSaving(false);
    }
  }

  const pageCount = result?.totalPages ?? 0;
  const isSearching = query.trim().length > 0;

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <AdminNavigation />
      <section className={styles.content} aria-labelledby="users-title">
        <div className={styles.heading}>
          <div>
            <span className={styles.eyebrow}>Quáº£n trá»‹ há»‡ thá»‘ng</span>
            <h1 id="users-title">NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng</h1>
            <p>TÃ¬m kiáº¿m tÃ i khoáº£n vÃ  quáº£n lÃ½ quyá»n truy cáº­p.</p>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.toolbar}>
          <label className={styles.visuallyHidden} htmlFor="user-search">
            TÃ¬m ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng theo tÃªn hoáº·c email
          </label>
          <div className="admin-search-control">
            <input
              className={styles.search}
              id="user-search"
              onChange={(event) => setQuery(event.target.value)}
              placeholder="TÃ¬m theo tÃªn hoáº·c email..."
              type="search"
              value={query}
            />
            {query && (
              <button
                aria-label="XÃ³a tá»« khÃ³a tÃ¬m kiáº¿m"
                className="admin-clear-search"
                onClick={clearSearch}
                type="button"
              >
                XÃ³a
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
          <span className={styles.count} aria-live="polite">
            {result ? `${result.totalElements} káº¿t quáº£` : ""}
          </span>
        </div>

        {error ? (
          <div className={styles.state}>
            <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{error}</p>
            <button className={styles.button} onClick={retryLoading} type="button">
              Thá»­ láº¡i
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : loading ? (
          <div
            className={styles.tableWrap}
            aria-label="Äang táº£i ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng"
            aria-busy="true"
            role="status"
          >
            {Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, index) => (
              <div className={styles.skeleton} key={index} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : result?.content.length ? (
          <>
            <div className={styles.tableWrap}>
              <table
                className={`${styles.table} admin-users-table`}
                aria-label="Danh sÃ¡ch ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng"
              >
                <caption className={styles.visuallyHidden}>Danh sÃ¡ch tÃ i khoáº£n ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng</caption>
                <thead>
                  <tr>
                    <th scope="col">NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng</th>
                    <th scope="col">Email</th>
                    <th scope="col">Vai trÃ²</th>
                    <th scope="col">Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i</th>
                    <th scope="col">NgÃ y táº¡o</th>
                    <th scope="col"><span className={styles.visuallyHidden}>Thao tÃ¡c</span></th>
                  </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
                  {result.content.map((user) => {
                    const isSelf =
                      auth.status === "authenticated" &&
                      user.email.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("vi") ===
                        auth.user.email.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("vi");
                    const locked = !user.enabled;
                    const helpId = `user-action-help-${user.id}`;

                    return (
                      <tr key={user.id}>
                        <td>
                          <span className="admin-user-identity">
                            <span className="admin-user-avatar" aria-hidden="true">
                              {user.fullName.trim().charAt(0).toLocaleUpperCase("vi") || "?"}
                            </span>
                            <span className={styles.primaryText}>{user.fullName}</span>
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td>{user.email}</td>
                        <td>{user.roles.join(", ") || "USER"}</td>
                        <td>
                          <span className={`${styles.status} ${locked ? styles.statusLocked : styles.statusActive}`}>
                            {locked ? "ÄÃ£ khÃ³a" : "Hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng"}
                          </span>
                        </td>
                        <td>{formatDate(user.createdAt)}</td>
                        <td>
                          {isSelf && user.enabled ? (
                            <span className="admin-self-action" title="Báº¡n khÃ´ng thá»ƒ tá»± khÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n cá»§a mÃ¬nh.">
                              <button
                                aria-describedby={helpId}
                                className={styles.dangerButton}
                                disabled
                                type="button"
                              >
                                KhÃ³a
                              </button>
                              <span className={styles.visuallyHidden} id={helpId}>
                                Báº¡n khÃ´ng thá»ƒ tá»± khÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n cá»§a mÃ¬nh.
                              </span>
                            </span>
                          ) : (
                            <button
                              className={locked ? styles.secondaryButton : styles.dangerButton}
                              onClick={() => setPendingUser(user)}
                              type="button"
                            >
                              {locked ? "Má»Ÿ khÃ³a" : "KhÃ³a"}
                            </button>
                          )}
                        </td>
                      </tr>
                    );
                  })}
                </tbody>
              </table>
            </div>
            {pageCount > 1 && (
              <nav className={styles.pagination} aria-label="PhÃ¢n trang ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng">
                <button
                  disabled={page === 0}
                  onClick={() => setPage((value) => Math.max(0, value - 1))}
                  type="button"
                >
                  â† TrÆ°á»›c
                </button>
                <span>Trang {page + 1} / {pageCount}</span>
                <button
                  disabled={page >= pageCount - 1}
                  onClick={() => setPage((value) => Math.min(pageCount - 1, value + 1))}
                  type="button"
                >
                  Sau â†’
                </button>
              </nav>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className="empty-state" role="status">
            <h3>{isSearching ? "KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y káº¿t quáº£" : "ChÆ°a cÃ³ tÃ i khoáº£n"}</h3>
            <p>
              {isSearching
                ? "KhÃ´ng cÃ³ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng phÃ¹ há»£p vá»›i tá»« khÃ³a nÃ y."
                : "Hiá»‡n chÆ°a cÃ³ tÃ i khoáº£n ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng nÃ o."}
            </p>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>

      {pendingUser && (
        <ConfirmModal
          confirmLabel={pendingUser.enabled ? "XÃ¡c nháº­n khÃ³a" : "XÃ¡c nháº­n má»Ÿ khÃ³a"}
          description={
            <>
              {pendingUser.enabled
                ? "NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng sáº½ khÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p cho Ä‘áº¿n khi Ä‘Æ°á»£c má»Ÿ khÃ³a. TÃ i khoáº£n: "
                : "NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng sáº½ cÃ³ thá»ƒ Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p trá»Ÿ láº¡i. TÃ i khoáº£n: "}
              <strong>{pendingUser.fullName}</strong> ({pendingUser.email})
            </>
          }
          destructive={pendingUser.enabled}
          isPending={saving}
          onCancel={() => {
            if (!saving) setPendingUser(null);
          }}
          onConfirm={() => void confirmToggleStatus()}
          pendingLabel={pendingUser.enabled ? "Äang khÃ³a..." : "Äang má»Ÿ khÃ³a..."}
          title={pendingUser.enabled ? "KhÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ y?" : "Má»Ÿ khÃ³a tÃ i khoáº£n nÃ y?"}
        />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

===== frontend/app/admin/layout.tsx =====
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { RoleGuard } from "../../components/role-guard";

export default function AdminLayout({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  // This guard improves UX only; every admin API must enforce ADMIN authorization server-side.
  return <RoleGuard allowedRoles={["ADMIN"]}>{children}</RoleGuard>;
}

.page {

===== frontend/app/admin/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import Link from "next/link";
import { AdminNavigation } from "../../components/admin-navigation";
import { useAuth } from "../../components/auth-provider";
import { adminStatsService } from "../../lib/api/adminStatsService";
import type { AdminStats } from "../../lib/api/types";
import styles from "./page.module.css";

const loanStatuses = [
  { key: "PENDING", label: "Äang chá»", color: styles.pending },
  { key: "APPROVED", label: "ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t", color: styles.approved },
  { key: "REJECTED", label: "Tá»« chá»‘i", color: styles.rejected },
  { key: "RETURNED", label: "ÄÃ£ tráº£", color: styles.returned },
  { key: "CANCELLED", label: "ÄÃ£ há»§y", color: styles.cancelled },
  { key: "OVERDUE", label: "QuÃ¡ háº¡n", color: styles.overdue },
] as const;

const numberFormat = new Intl.NumberFormat("vi-VN");

function formatNumber(value: number): string {
  return numberFormat.format(value);
}

export default function AdminPage() {
  const auth = useAuth();
  const [stats, setStats] = useState<AdminStats | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (auth.status !== "authenticated" || !auth.hasRole("ADMIN")) return;

    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadStats() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        setStats(await adminStatsService.get({ signal: controller.signal }));
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setError(
          requestError instanceof Error
            ? requestError.message
            : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i sá»‘ liá»‡u quáº£n trá»‹.",
        );
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadStats();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [auth, retry]);

  const retryLoading = useCallback(() => setRetry((current) => current + 1), []);
  const loanTotal = stats
    ? Object.values(stats.loansByStatus).reduce((total, count) => total + count, 0)
    : 0;
  const highestStatusCount = stats
    ? Math.max(0, ...Object.values(stats.loansByStatus))
    : 0;
  const highestCategoryCount = stats
    ? Math.max(0, ...stats.mostBorrowedCategories.map(({ loanCount }) => loanCount))
    : 0;

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <AdminNavigation />
      <section className={styles.content} aria-labelledby="dashboard-title">
        <div className={styles.heading}>
          <div>
            <span className={styles.eyebrow}>Quáº£n trá»‹ há»‡ thá»‘ng</span>
            <h1 id="dashboard-title">Tá»•ng quan</h1>
            <p>Theo dÃµi tÃ¬nh hÃ¬nh hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng cá»§a UniLend.</p>
          </div>
          {stats && (
            <button
              className={styles.refreshButton}
              disabled={loading}
              onClick={retryLoading}
              type="button"
            >
              {loading ? "Äang cáº­p nháº­t..." : "LÃ m má»›i sá»‘ liá»‡u"}
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {error ? (
          <div className={styles.state} role="alert">
            <p>{error}</p>
            <button className={styles.refreshButton} onClick={retryLoading} type="button">
              Thá»­ láº¡i
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : loading && !stats ? (
          <div className={styles.metrics} aria-label="Äang táº£i sá»‘ liá»‡u" aria-busy="true">
            {Array.from({ length: 3 }, (_, index) => (
              <div className={styles.skeleton} key={index} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : stats ? (
          <>
            <div className={styles.metrics} aria-live="polite">
              <article className={styles.metricCard}>
                <span className={styles.metricLabel}>NgÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng</span>
                <strong>{formatNumber(stats.totalUsers)}</strong>
                <Link href="/admin/users">Quáº£n lÃ½ ngÆ°á»i dÃ¹ng <span aria-hidden="true">â†’</span></Link>
              </article>
              <article className={styles.metricCard}>
                <span className={styles.metricLabel}>Äá»“ Ä‘ang cÃ³ trÃªn ná»n táº£ng</span>
                <strong>{formatNumber(stats.totalItems)}</strong>
                <Link href="/admin/items">Quáº£n lÃ½ Ä‘á»“ <span aria-hidden="true">â†’</span></Link>
              </article>
              <article className={styles.metricCard}>
                <span className={styles.metricLabel}>LÆ°á»£t mÆ°á»£n</span>
                <strong>{formatNumber(loanTotal)}</strong>
                <span className={styles.metricFootnote}>Tá»•ng lÆ°á»£t mÆ°á»£n theo má»i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i</span>
              </article>
            </div>

            <div className={styles.charts}>
              <section className={styles.chartCard} aria-labelledby="loan-chart-title">
                <div className={styles.chartHeading}>
                  <div>
                    <h2 id="loan-chart-title">LÆ°á»£t mÆ°á»£n theo tráº¡ng thÃ¡i</h2>
                    <p>PhÃ¢n bá»‘ {formatNumber(loanTotal)} lÆ°á»£t mÆ°á»£n</p>
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div className={styles.chartRows}>
                  {loanStatuses.map(({ key, label, color }) => {
                    const count = stats.loansByStatus[key] ?? 0;
                    const width = highestStatusCount ? (count / highestStatusCount) * 100 : 0;
                    return (
                      <div className={styles.chartRow} key={key}>
                        <span className={styles.chartLabel}>{label}</span>
                        <div
                          className={styles.barTrack}
                          role="img"
                          aria-label={`${label}: ${formatNumber(count)} lÆ°á»£t`}
                        >
                          <span className={`${styles.bar} ${color}`} style={{ width: `${width}%` }} />
                        </div>
                        <span className={styles.chartValue}>{formatNumber(count)}</span>
                      </div>
                    );
                  })}
                </div>
              </section>

              <section className={styles.chartCard} aria-labelledby="category-chart-title">
                <div className={styles.chartHeading}>
                  <div>
                    <h2 id="category-chart-title">Danh má»¥c Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÆ°á»£n nhiá»u</h2>
                    <p>Top danh má»¥c theo lÆ°á»£t mÆ°á»£n</p>
                  </div>
                  <Link href="/admin/categories">Quáº£n lÃ½ danh má»¥c <span aria-hidden="true">â†’</span></Link>
                </div>
                {stats.mostBorrowedCategories.length ? (
                  <div className={styles.chartRows}>
                    {stats.mostBorrowedCategories.map((category) => {
                      const width = highestCategoryCount
                        ? (category.loanCount / highestCategoryCount) * 100
                        : 0;
                      return (
                        <div className={styles.chartRow} key={category.categoryId}>
                          <span className={styles.chartLabel} title={category.categoryName}>
                            {category.categoryName}
                          </span>
                          <div
                            className={styles.barTrack}
                            role="img"
                            aria-label={`${category.categoryName}: ${formatNumber(category.loanCount)} lÆ°á»£t`}
                          >
                            <span className={`${styles.bar} ${styles.categoryBar}`} style={{ width: `${width}%` }} />
                          </div>
                          <span className={styles.chartValue}>{formatNumber(category.loanCount)}</span>
                        </div>
                      );
                    })}
                  </div>
                ) : (
                  <p className={styles.empty}>ChÆ°a cÃ³ lÆ°á»£t mÆ°á»£n theo danh má»¥c.</p>
                )}
              </section>
            </div>
          </>
        ) : null}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}
