===== frontend/app/items/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import Image from "next/image";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { NotificationBell } from "../../components/notification-bell";
import { categoryService } from "../../lib/api/categoryService";
import { resolveImageUrl } from "../../lib/api/imageUrl";
import { itemService } from "../../lib/api/itemService";
import { getItemImageUrls, type Category, type Item, type PageResponse } from "../../lib/api/types";
import styles from "./page.module.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 9;

function ItemCard({ item }: { item: Item }) {
  const imageUrl = getItemImageUrls(item)[0];
  const [failedImageUrl, setFailedImageUrl] = useState("");
  const statusLabel =
    item.status === "AVAILABLE"
      ? "CÃ³ thá»ƒ mÆ°á»£n"
      : item.status === "LENT_OUT"
        ? "Äang Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÆ°á»£n"
        : "Táº¡m ngÆ°ng";

  return (
    <article className={styles.itemCard}>
      <Link className={styles.itemLink} href={`/items/${item.id}`} aria-label={`Xem chi tiáº¿t ${item.title}`}>
        <div className={styles.itemArtwork}>
          {imageUrl && imageUrl !== failedImageUrl ? (
            <Image
              alt={item.title}
              className={styles.cardImage}
              src={resolveImageUrl(imageUrl)}
              fill
              sizes="(max-width: 560px) 100vw, (max-width: 800px) 50vw, 33vw"
              unoptimized
              onError={() => setFailedImageUrl(imageUrl)}
            />
          ) : (
            <>
              <svg viewBox="0 0 64 64" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
                <path d="m32 9 23 12.5v21L32 55 9 42.5v-21L32 9Z" />
                <path d="m9 21.5 23 13 23-13M32 34.5V55m-11-40 23 13" />
              </svg>
              <span className={styles.artworkCircle} />
            </>
          )}
          <span className={styles.statusBadge}>
            <span className={item.status === "AVAILABLE" ? styles.availableDot : styles.unavailableDot} />
            {statusLabel}
          </span>
        </div>
        <div className={styles.itemDetails}>
          <span className={styles.category}>{item.category?.name ?? "ChÆ°a phÃ¢n loáº¡i"}</span>
          <h2>{item.title}</h2>
          {item.description && <p className={styles.description}>{item.description}</p>}
          <div className={styles.owner}>
            <span className={styles.avatar} aria-hidden="true">
              {item.owner.fullName.trim().slice(0, 1).toLocaleUpperCase("vi")}
            </span>
            <span>{item.owner.fullName}</span>
            <span className={styles.ownerCaption}>Â· Sinh viÃªn</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </Link>
    </article>
  );
}

function LoadingGrid() {
  return (
    <div className={styles.grid} aria-label="Äang táº£i danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»“" aria-busy="true">
      {Array.from({ length: 6 }, (_, index) => (
        <div className={styles.skeletonCard} key={index}>
          <div className={`${styles.skeleton} ${styles.skeletonArtwork}`} />
          <div className={styles.skeletonContent}>
            <div className={`${styles.skeleton} ${styles.skeletonCategory}`} />
            <div className={`${styles.skeleton} ${styles.skeletonTitle}`} />
            <div className={`${styles.skeleton} ${styles.skeletonOwner}`} />
          </div>
        </div>
      ))}
    </div>
  );
}

export default function ItemsPage() {
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  const [categoryError, setCategoryError] = useState("");
  const [categoryRetry, setCategoryRetry] = useState(0);
  const [selectedCategory, setSelectedCategory] = useState("");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [itemsPage, setItemsPage] = useState<PageResponse<Item> | null>(null);
  const [itemsError, setItemsError] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadCategories() {
      setCategoryError("");
      try {
        const data = await categoryService.listAll({ signal: controller.signal });
        setCategories(data);
      } catch (error) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setCategoryError(
          error instanceof Error ? error.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i danh má»¥c. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.",
        );
      }
    }

    void loadCategories();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [categoryRetry]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadItems() {
      setLoading(true);
      setItemsError("");
      try {
        const result = await itemService.list(
          {
            page,
            size: PAGE_SIZE,
            ...(query.trim() ? { keyword: query.trim() } : {}),
            ...(selectedCategory ? { categoryId: Number(selectedCategory) } : {}),
          },
          { signal: controller.signal },
        );
        setItemsPage(result);
      } catch (error) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setItemsError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»“.");
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadItems();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [page, query, retry, selectedCategory]);

  const retryLoading = useCallback(() => {
    setRetry((current) => current + 1);
    setCategoryRetry((current) => current + 1);
  }, []);

  function updateQuery(value: string) {
    setQuery(value);
    setPage(0);
  }

  function updateCategory(value: string) {
    setSelectedCategory(value);
    setPage(0);
  }

  const pageCount = itemsPage?.totalPages ?? 0;

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <Link className={styles.brand} href="/" aria-label="UniLend - vá» trang chá»§">
          <span className={styles.brandMark} aria-hidden="true">
            U
          </span>
          <span>
            uni<span className={styles.brandAccent}>lend</span>
          </span>
        </Link>
        <nav className={styles.navigation} aria-label="Äiá»u hÆ°á»›ng chÃ­nh">
          <Link href="/">Trang chá»§</Link>
          <span aria-current="page">Danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»“</span>
        </nav>
        <div className={styles.headerActions}>
          <NotificationBell />
          <Link className={styles.backLink} href="/">Vá» trang chá»§</Link>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className={styles.content} aria-labelledby="page-title">
        <div className={styles.headingRow}>
          <div className={styles.heading}>
            <span className={styles.eyebrow}>KhÃ¡m phÃ¡ UniLend</span>
            <h1 id="page-title">Danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»“</h1>
            <p>TÃ¬m mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ há»¯u Ã­ch vÃ  káº¿t ná»‘i vá»›i nhá»¯ng ngÆ°á»i báº¡n quanh trÆ°á»ng.</p>
          </div>
          <Link className={styles.createLink} href="/items/new">ÄÄƒng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“</Link>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.toolbar}>
          <div className={styles.searchField}>
            <label className={styles.visuallyHidden} htmlFor="item-search">
              TÃ¬m theo tÃªn mÃ³n Ä‘á»“
            </label>
            <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
              <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7" />
              <path d="m16 16 4 4" />
            </svg>
            <input
              id="item-search"
              type="search"
              placeholder="Báº¡n Ä‘ang cáº§n tÃ¬m mÃ³n gÃ¬?"
              value={query}
              onChange={(event) => updateQuery(event.target.value)}
            />
          </div>

          <div className={styles.filterField}>
            <label htmlFor="category-filter">Danh má»¥c</label>
            <select
              id="category-filter"
              value={selectedCategory}
              onChange={(event) => updateCategory(event.target.value)}
              disabled={Boolean(categoryError)}
            >
              <option value="">Táº¥t cáº£ danh má»¥c</option>
              {categories.map((category) => (
                <option key={category.id} value={category.id}>
                  {category.name}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>
        </div>

        {itemsPage && !loading && !itemsError && (
          <p className={styles.resultCount} aria-live="polite">
            {itemsPage.totalElements} mÃ³n Ä‘á»“
          </p>
        )}

        {categoryError || itemsError ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel} role="alert">
            <h2>KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»“</h2>
            <p>{categoryError || itemsError}</p>
            <button className={styles.retryButton} onClick={retryLoading} type="button">
              Thá»­ láº¡i
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : loading ? (
          <LoadingGrid />
        ) : itemsPage?.content.length ? (
          <>
            <div className={styles.grid}>
              {itemsPage.content.map((item) => (
                <ItemCard item={item} key={item.id} />
              ))}
            </div>
            {pageCount > 1 && (
              <nav className={styles.pagination} aria-label="PhÃ¢n trang danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»“">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setPage((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1))}
                  disabled={page === 0}
                  aria-label="Trang trÆ°á»›c"
                >
                  â† <span>TrÆ°á»›c</span>
                </button>
                <span aria-live="polite">
                  Trang {page + 1} / {pageCount}
                </span>
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => setPage((current) => Math.min(pageCount - 1, current + 1))}
                  disabled={page >= pageCount - 1}
                  aria-label="Trang sau"
                >
                  <span>Sau</span> â†’
                </button>
              </nav>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className={styles.statePanel}>
            <span className={styles.emptyIcon} aria-hidden="true">
              âŒ•
            </span>
            <h2>ChÆ°a tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ phÃ¹ há»£p</h2>
            <p>Thá»­ tá»« khÃ³a khÃ¡c hoáº·c chá»n má»™t danh má»¥c khÃ¡c nhÃ©.</p>
            {(query || selectedCategory) && (
              <button
                className={styles.retryButton}
                onClick={() => {
                  updateQuery("");
                  updateCategory("");
                }}
                type="button"
              >
                XÃ³a bá»™ lá»c
              </button>
            )}
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

===== frontend/app/login/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { AuthShell } from "../../components/auth-shell";
import { loginUser, MockAuthError } from "../../lib/mock-auth";

type FormValues = {
  email: string;
  password: string;
};

type FieldName = keyof FormValues;
type FormErrors = Partial<Record<FieldName, string>>;

export default function LoginPage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [values, setValues] = useState<FormValues>({ email: "", password: "" });
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");

  function updateField(field: FieldName, value: string) {
    setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: value }));
    setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: undefined }));
    setFormError("");
  }

  function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const nextErrors: FormErrors = {};

    if (!values.email.trim()) nextErrors.email = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p email.";
    else if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(values.email.trim())) {
      nextErrors.email = "Email chÆ°a Ä‘Ãºng Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng.";
    }
    if (!values.password) nextErrors.password = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p máº­t kháº©u.";

    setErrors(nextErrors);
    setFormError("");
    if (Object.keys(nextErrors).length > 0) return;

    try {
      loginUser(values.email, values.password);
      router.push("/profile");
    } catch (error) {
      setFormError(
        error instanceof MockAuthError
          ? error.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t nÃ y. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.",
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <AuthShell
      eyebrow="ChÃ o má»«ng báº¡n trá»Ÿ láº¡i"
      title="ÄÄƒng nháº­p"
      description="ÄÄƒng nháº­p Ä‘á»ƒ xem há»“ sÆ¡ vÃ  tiáº¿p tá»¥c káº¿t ná»‘i vá»›i cá»™ng Ä‘á»“ng."
    >
      <form className="auth-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
        {formError && <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{formError}</p>}
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor="email">Email</label>
          <input
            autoComplete="email"
            autoFocus
            id="email"
            name="email"
            type="email"
            value={values.email}
            onChange={(event) => updateField("email", event.target.value)}
            aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.email)}
            aria-describedby={errors.email ? "email-error" : undefined}
            placeholder="ban@example.com"
          />
          {errors.email && <span className="field-error" id="email-error">{errors.email}</span>}
        </div>
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor="password">Máº­t kháº©u</label>
          <input
            autoComplete="current-password"
            id="password"
            name="password"
            type="password"
            value={values.password}
            onChange={(event) => updateField("password", event.target.value)}
            aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.password)}
            aria-describedby={errors.password ? "password-error" : undefined}
            placeholder="Nháº­p máº­t kháº©u"
          />
          {errors.password && <span className="field-error" id="password-error">{errors.password}</span>}
        </div>
        <button className="auth-submit" type="submit">ÄÄƒng nháº­p</button>
        <p className="auth-switch">ChÆ°a cÃ³ tÃ i khoáº£n? <Link href="/register">ÄÄƒng kÃ½ ngay</Link></p>
      </form>
    </AuthShell>
  );
}

"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNotifications } from "../../components/notifications-provider";
import {
  notificationService,
  type Notification,
} from "../../lib/api/notificationService";
import type { PageResponse } from "../../lib/api/types";
import styles from "./page.module.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 20;

function formatDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "Thá»i gian khÃ´ng xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh";

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", {
    dateStyle: "medium",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

function getNotificationHref(link?: string | null): string | null {
  return link?.startsWith("/") && !link.startsWith("//") ? link : null;
}

export default function NotificationsPage() {
  const { authenticated, markRead, markAllRead } = useNotifications();
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [result, setResult] = useState<PageResponse<Notification> | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [retry, setRetry] = useState(0);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!authenticated) return;

    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadNotifications() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        const response = await notificationService.list({
          page,
          size: PAGE_SIZE,
        });
        setResult(response);
      } catch (requestError) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setError(
          requestError instanceof Error
            ? requestError.message
            : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.",
        );
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadNotifications();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [authenticated, page, retry]);

  const retryLoading = useCallback(() => {
    setLoading(true);
    setRetry((current) => current + 1);
  }, []);

  async function handleMarkRead(notification: Notification) {
    if (notification.read) return;
    setActionError("");
    setResult((current) =>
      current
        ? {
            ...current,
            content: current.content.map((entry) =>
              entry.id === notification.id ? { ...entry, read: true } : entry,
            ),
          }
        : current,
    );
    try {
      await markRead(notification.id);
    } catch (requestError) {
      setResult((current) =>
        current
          ? {
              ...current,
              content: current.content.map((entry) =>
                entry.id === notification.id ? { ...entry, read: false } : entry,
              ),
            }
          : current,
      );
      setActionError(
        requestError instanceof Error
          ? requestError.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ cáº­p nháº­t tráº¡ng thÃ¡i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleMarkAllRead() {
    if (!result?.content.some((notification) => !notification.read)) return;
    const previous = result;
    setActionError("");
    setResult((current) =>
      current
        ? {
            ...current,
            content: current.content.map((notification) => ({ ...notification, read: true })),
          }
        : current,
    );

    try {
      await markAllRead();
    } catch (requestError) {
      setResult(previous);
      setActionError(
        requestError instanceof Error
          ? requestError.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u táº¥t cáº£ Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c.",
      );
    }
  }

  const pageCount = result?.totalPages ?? 0;

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <Link className={styles.brand} href="/" aria-label="UniLend - vá» trang chá»§">
          <span className={styles.brandMark} aria-hidden="true">U</span>
          <span>uni<span className={styles.brandAccent}>lend</span></span>
        </Link>
        <Link className={styles.backLink} href="/">Vá» trang chá»§</Link>
      </header>

      <section className={styles.content} aria-labelledby="notifications-title">
        <div className={styles.heading}>
          <div>
            <span className={styles.eyebrow}>Cáº­p nháº­t tá»« UniLend</span>
            <h1 id="notifications-title">ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o</h1>
            <p>Theo dÃµi cÃ¡c cáº­p nháº­t má»›i nháº¥t liÃªn quan Ä‘áº¿n hoáº¡t Ä‘á»™ng cá»§a báº¡n.</p>
          </div>
          {authenticated && result?.content.some((notification) => !notification.read) && (
            <button className={styles.secondaryButton} onClick={handleMarkAllRead} type="button">
              ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u táº¥t cáº£ Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c
            </button>
          )}
        </div>

        {!authenticated ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel}>
            <h2>ÄÄƒng nháº­p Ä‘á»ƒ xem thÃ´ng bÃ¡o</h2>
            <p>ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o Ä‘Æ°á»£c liÃªn káº¿t vá»›i tÃ i khoáº£n cá»§a báº¡n.</p>
            <Link className={styles.primaryButton} href="/login">ÄÄƒng nháº­p</Link>
          </div>
        ) : error ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel} role="alert">
            <h2>KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o</h2>
            <p>{error}</p>
            <button className={styles.primaryButton} onClick={retryLoading} type="button">
              Thá»­ láº¡i
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : loading ? (
          <div className={styles.notificationList} aria-label="Äang táº£i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o" aria-busy="true">
            {Array.from({ length: 5 }, (_, index) => (
              <div className={styles.skeleton} key={index} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : result?.content.length ? (
          <>
            {actionError && <p className={styles.actionError} role="alert">{actionError}</p>}
            <ul className={styles.notificationList}>
              {result.content.map((notification) => (
                <li key={notification.id}>
                  <article className={`${styles.notification}${notification.read ? "" : ` ${styles.unread}`}`}>
                    <span className={styles.indicator} aria-hidden="true" />
                    <div className={styles.notificationBody}>
                      <h2>
                        <button
                          className={styles.titleButton}
                          onClick={() => void handleMarkRead(notification)}
                          type="button"
                        >
                          {notification.title}
                        </button>
                      </h2>
                      <p>{notification.message}</p>
                      <time dateTime={notification.createdAt}>
                        {formatDate(notification.createdAt)}
                      </time>
                    </div>
                    {!notification.read && (
                      <button
                        className={styles.readButton}
                        onClick={() => void handleMarkRead(notification)}
                        type="button"
                      >
                        ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c
                      </button>
                    )}
                    {getNotificationHref(notification.link) && (
                      <Link className={styles.openLink} href={getNotificationHref(notification.link) ?? "/notifications"}>
                        Má»Ÿ
                      </Link>
                    )}
                  </article>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
            {pageCount > 1 && (
              <nav className={styles.pagination} aria-label="PhÃ¢n trang thÃ´ng bÃ¡o">
                <button
                  disabled={page === 0}
                  onClick={() => setPage((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1))}
                  type="button"
                >
                  â† TrÆ°á»›c
                </button>
                <span aria-live="polite">Trang {page + 1} / {pageCount}</span>
                <button
                  disabled={page >= pageCount - 1}
                  onClick={() => setPage((current) => Math.min(pageCount - 1, current + 1))}
                  type="button"
                >
                  Sau â†’
                </button>
              </nav>
            )}
          </>
        ) : (
          <div className={styles.statePanel}>
            <span className={styles.emptyIcon} aria-hidden="true">â™§</span>
            <h2>KhÃ´ng cÃ³ thÃ´ng bÃ¡o nÃ o</h2>
            <p>Khi cÃ³ cáº­p nháº­t má»›i, thÃ´ng bÃ¡o sáº½ xuáº¥t hiá»‡n á»Ÿ Ä‘Ã¢y.</p>
          </div>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

===== frontend/app/profile/edit/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, useSyncExternalStore, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { AuthShell } from "../../../components/auth-shell";
import {
  changeCurrentUserPassword,
  getCurrentUser,
  getSessionSnapshot,
  MockAuthError,
  subscribeToAuth,
  updateCurrentUser,
  type UserProfile,
} from "../../../lib/mock-auth";

type ProfileValues = {
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
};

type PasswordValues = {
  currentPassword: string;
  newPassword: string;
  confirmPassword: string;
};

type ProfileField = keyof ProfileValues;
type PasswordField = keyof PasswordValues;
type ProfileErrors = Partial<Record<ProfileField, string>>;
type PasswordErrors = Partial<Record<PasswordField, string>>;

function validateProfile(values: ProfileValues): ProfileErrors {
  const errors: ProfileErrors = {};
  const fullName = values.fullName.trim();
  const email = values.email.trim();

  if (!fullName) errors.fullName = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p há» vÃ  tÃªn.";
  else if (fullName.length < 2) errors.fullName = "Há» vÃ  tÃªn cáº§n cÃ³ Ã­t nháº¥t 2 kÃ½ tá»±.";
  else if (fullName.length > 100) errors.fullName = "Há» vÃ  tÃªn khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 100 kÃ½ tá»±.";

  if (!email) errors.email = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p email.";
  else if (email.length > 254 || !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) {
    errors.email = "Email chÆ°a Ä‘Ãºng Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng.";
  }

  return errors;
}

function validatePassword(values: PasswordValues): PasswordErrors {
  const errors: PasswordErrors = {};

  if (!values.currentPassword) errors.currentPassword = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p máº­t kháº©u hiá»‡n táº¡i.";

  if (!values.newPassword) errors.newPassword = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p máº­t kháº©u má»›i.";
  else if (values.newPassword.length < 8) {
    errors.newPassword = "Máº­t kháº©u má»›i cáº§n cÃ³ Ã­t nháº¥t 8 kÃ½ tá»±.";
  } else if (values.newPassword.length > 128) {
    errors.newPassword = "Máº­t kháº©u má»›i khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 128 kÃ½ tá»±.";
  } else if (values.newPassword === values.currentPassword) {
    errors.newPassword = "Máº­t kháº©u má»›i cáº§n khÃ¡c máº­t kháº©u hiá»‡n táº¡i.";
  }

  if (!values.confirmPassword) errors.confirmPassword = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p láº¡i máº­t kháº©u má»›i.";
  else if (values.confirmPassword !== values.newPassword) {
    errors.confirmPassword = "Máº­t kháº©u nháº­p láº¡i chÆ°a khá»›p.";
  }

  return errors;
}

export default function EditProfilePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const sessionSnapshot = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeToAuth,
    getSessionSnapshot,
    () => "__loading__",
  );
  const profileState = useMemo(() => {
    if (sessionSnapshot === "__loading__") return { status: "loading" as const };
    if (!sessionSnapshot) return { status: "unauthenticated" as const };

    try {
      const user = getCurrentUser();
      return user
        ? { status: "authenticated" as const, user }
        : { status: "unauthenticated" as const };
    } catch (error) {
      return {
        status: "error" as const,
        message: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i thÃ´ng tin há»“ sÆ¡.",
      };
    }
  }, [sessionSnapshot]);
  const user: UserProfile | null =
    profileState.status === "authenticated" ? profileState.user : null;

  const [profileValues, setProfileValues] = useState<ProfileValues | null>(null);
  const [passwordValues, setPasswordValues] = useState<PasswordValues>({
    currentPassword: "",
    newPassword: "",
    confirmPassword: "",
  });
  const [profileErrors, setProfileErrors] = useState<ProfileErrors>({});
  const [passwordErrors, setPasswordErrors] = useState<PasswordErrors>({});
  const [profileMessage, setProfileMessage] = useState("");
  const [profileError, setProfileError] = useState("");
  const [passwordMessage, setPasswordMessage] = useState("");
  const [passwordError, setPasswordError] = useState("");
  const currentProfileValues = profileValues ?? {
    fullName: user?.fullName ?? "",
    email: user?.email ?? "",
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profileState.status === "unauthenticated") router.replace("/login");
  }, [profileState.status, router]);

  function handleProfileSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const errors = validateProfile(currentProfileValues);
    setProfileErrors(errors);
    setProfileMessage("");
    setProfileError("");
    if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return;

    try {
      updateCurrentUser(currentProfileValues);
      setProfileMessage("ThÃ´ng tin há»“ sÆ¡ Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c cáº­p nháº­t.");
    } catch (error) {
      setProfileError(
        error instanceof MockAuthError
          ? error.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ cáº­p nháº­t há»“ sÆ¡. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.",
      );
    }
  }

  function handlePasswordSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const errors = validatePassword(passwordValues);
    setPasswordErrors(errors);
    setPasswordMessage("");
    setPasswordError("");
    if (Object.keys(errors).length > 0) return;

    try {
      changeCurrentUserPassword(passwordValues);
      setPasswordValues({ currentPassword: "", newPassword: "", confirmPassword: "" });
      setPasswordErrors({});
      setPasswordMessage("Máº­t kháº©u Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c thay Ä‘á»•i.");
    } catch (error) {
      setPasswordError(
        error instanceof MockAuthError
          ? error.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»•i máº­t kháº©u. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.",
      );
    }
  }

  if (profileState.status === "loading" || profileState.status === "unauthenticated") {
    return <main className="profile-loading" role="status">Äang táº£i há»“ sÆ¡...</main>;
  }

  return (
    <AuthShell
      eyebrow="CÃ i Ä‘áº·t tÃ i khoáº£n"
      title="Chá»‰nh sá»­a há»“ sÆ¡"
      description="Cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n vÃ  giá»¯ an toÃ n cho tÃ i khoáº£n cá»§a báº¡n."
    >
      {profileState.status === "error" && (
        <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{profileState.message}</p>
      )}

      <section className="profile-edit-section" aria-labelledby="profile-information-title">
        <h2 id="profile-information-title">ThÃ´ng tin cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n</h2>
        <form className="auth-form" onSubmit={handleProfileSubmit} noValidate>
          {profileError && <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{profileError}</p>}
          {profileMessage && <p className="form-success" role="status">{profileMessage}</p>}
          <div className="form-field">
            <label htmlFor="fullName">Há» vÃ  tÃªn</label>
            <input
              autoComplete="name"
              id="fullName"
              maxLength={100}
              name="fullName"
              value={currentProfileValues.fullName}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setProfileValues({ ...currentProfileValues, fullName: event.target.value });
                setProfileErrors((current) => ({ ...current, fullName: undefined }));
                setProfileMessage("");
              }}
              aria-invalid={Boolean(profileErrors.fullName)}
              aria-describedby={profileErrors.fullName ? "fullName-error" : undefined}
              required
            />
            {profileErrors.fullName && (
              <span className="field-error" id="fullName-error">{profileErrors.fullName}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          <div className="form-field">
            <label htmlFor="email">Email</label>
            <input
              autoComplete="email"
              id="email"
              maxLength={254}
              name="email"
              type="email"
              value={currentProfileValues.email}
              onChange={(event) => {
                setProfileValues({ ...currentProfileValues, email: event.target.value });
                setProfileErrors((current) => ({ ...current, email: undefined }));
                setProfileMessage("");
              }}
              aria-invalid={Boolean(profileErrors.email)}
              aria-describedby={profileErrors.email ? "email-error" : undefined}
              required
            />
            {profileErrors.email && (
              <span className="field-error" id="email-error">{profileErrors.email}</span>
            )}
          </div>
          <button className="auth-submit" type="submit">LÆ°u thÃ´ng tin</button>
        </form>
      </section>

      <section className="profile-edit-section" aria-labelledby="password-title">
        <h2 id="password-title">Äá»•i máº­t kháº©u</h2>
        <form className="auth-form" onSubmit={handlePasswordSubmit} noValidate>
          {passwordError && <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{passwordError}</p>}
          {passwordMessage && <p className="form-success" role="status">{passwordMessage}</p>}
          <PasswordField
            error={passwordErrors.currentPassword}
            id="currentPassword"
            label="Máº­t kháº©u hiá»‡n táº¡i"
            onChange={(value) => {
              setPasswordValues((current) => ({ ...current, currentPassword: value }));
              setPasswordErrors((current) => ({ ...current, currentPassword: undefined }));
              setPasswordMessage("");
            }}
            value={passwordValues.currentPassword}
            autoComplete="current-password"
          />
          <PasswordField
            error={passwordErrors.newPassword}
            id="newPassword"
            label="Máº­t kháº©u má»›i"
            onChange={(value) => {
              setPasswordValues((current) => ({ ...current, newPassword: value }));
              setPasswordErrors((current) => ({ ...current, newPassword: undefined }));
              setPasswordMessage("");
            }}
            value={passwordValues.newPassword}
            autoComplete="new-password"
          />
          <PasswordField
            error={passwordErrors.confirmPassword}
            id="confirmPassword"
            label="Nháº­p láº¡i máº­t kháº©u má»›i"
            onChange={(value) => {
              setPasswordValues((current) => ({ ...current, confirmPassword: value }));
              setPasswordErrors((current) => ({ ...current, confirmPassword: undefined }));
              setPasswordMessage("");
            }}
            value={passwordValues.confirmPassword}
            autoComplete="new-password"
          />
          <button className="auth-submit" type="submit">Cáº­p nháº­t máº­t kháº©u</button>
        </form>
      </section>

      <Link className="profile-edit-back" href="/profile">Quay láº¡i há»“ sÆ¡</Link>
    </AuthShell>
  );
}

function PasswordField({
  autoComplete,
  error,
  id,
  label,
  onChange,
  value,
}: {
  autoComplete: "current-password" | "new-password";
  error?: string;
  id: PasswordField;
  label: string;
  onChange: (value: string) => void;
  value: string;
}) {
  const errorId = `${id}-error`;

  return (
    <div className="form-field">
      <label htmlFor={id}>{label}</label>
      <input
        autoComplete={autoComplete}
        id={id}
        name={id}
        type="password"
        value={value}
        onChange={(event) => onChange(event.target.value)}
        aria-invalid={Boolean(error)}
        aria-describedby={error ? errorId : undefined}
        required
      />
      {error && <span className="field-error" id={errorId}>{error}</span>}
    </div>
  );
}

===== frontend/app/profile/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState, useSyncExternalStore } from "react";
import { UserReviewSection } from "../../components/user-review-section";
import {
  getCurrentUser,
  getSessionSnapshot,
  logoutUser,
  subscribeToAuth,
  type UserProfile,
} from "../../lib/mock-auth";

export default function ProfilePage() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [logoutError, setLogoutError] = useState("");
  const sessionSnapshot = useSyncExternalStore(subscribeToAuth, getSessionSnapshot, () => "__loading__");
  const profileState = useMemo(() => {
    if (sessionSnapshot === "__loading__") return { status: "loading" as const };
    if (sessionSnapshot === null) return { status: "unauthenticated" as const };

    try {
      const user = getCurrentUser();
      return user
        ? { status: "authenticated" as const, user }
        : { status: "unauthenticated" as const };
    } catch (loadError) {
      return {
        status: "error" as const,
        message: loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i há»“ sÆ¡.",
      };
    }
  }, [sessionSnapshot]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (profileState.status === "unauthenticated") router.replace("/login");
  }, [profileState, router]);

  const user: UserProfile | null =
    profileState.status === "authenticated" ? profileState.user : null;
  const error = profileState.status === "error" ? profileState.message : logoutError;

  function handleLogout() {
    try {
      logoutUser();
      router.replace("/");
    } catch {
      setLogoutError("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘Äƒng xuáº¥t trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t nÃ y. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.");
    }
  }

  if (profileState.status === "loading" || profileState.status === "unauthenticated") {
    return <main className="profile-loading" role="status">Äang táº£i há»“ sÆ¡...</main>;
  }

  return (
    <main className="auth-page profile-page">
      <header className="auth-header">
        <Link className="brand" href="/" aria-label="UniLend - vá» trang chá»§">
          <span className="brand-mark" aria-hidden="true">u</span>
          <span>uni<span className="brand-accent">lend</span></span>
        </Link>
        <button className="auth-home-link logout-button" onClick={handleLogout} type="button">ÄÄƒng xuáº¥t</button>
      </header>
      <section className="profile-card">
        <span className="section-kicker">TÃ i khoáº£n UniLend</span>
        <div className="profile-identity">
          <span className="profile-avatar" aria-hidden="true">
            {user?.fullName.trim().charAt(0).toLocaleUpperCase("vi") || "U"}
          </span>
          <div>
            <h1>{user?.fullName}</h1>
            <p>ThÃ nh viÃªn cá»™ng Ä‘á»“ng UniLend</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        {error && <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{error}</p>}
        <dl className="profile-details">
          <div><dt>Há» vÃ  tÃªn</dt><dd>{user?.fullName}</dd></div>
          <div><dt>Email</dt><dd>{user?.email}</dd></div>
          <div>
            <dt>Tham gia tá»«</dt>
            <dd>{user ? new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", { day: "numeric", month: "long", year: "numeric" }).format(new Date(user.createdAt)) : ""}</dd>
          </div>
        </dl>
        <div className="profile-actions">
          <Link className="profile-home-link" href="/profile/edit">Sá»­a thÃ´ng tin há»“ sÆ¡</Link>
          <Link className="profile-home-link profile-secondary-link" href="/">KhÃ¡m phÃ¡ mÃ³n Ä‘á»“</Link>
        </div>
        <UserReviewSection title="ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ cá»§a báº¡n" />
      </section>
      <p className="auth-disclaimer">Há»“ sÆ¡ thá»­ nghiá»‡m â€” dá»¯ liá»‡u chá»‰ lÆ°u trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t nÃ y. KhÃ´ng sá»­ dá»¥ng máº­t kháº©u tháº­t.</p>
    </main>
  );
}

===== frontend/app/register/page.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { AuthShell } from "../../components/auth-shell";
import { MockAuthError, registerUser } from "../../lib/mock-auth";

type FormValues = {
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  password: string;
  confirmPassword: string;
};

type FieldName = keyof FormValues;
type FormErrors = Partial<Record<FieldName, string>>;

const initialValues: FormValues = {
  fullName: "",
  email: "",
  password: "",
  confirmPassword: "",
};

function validate(values: FormValues): FormErrors {
  const errors: FormErrors = {};
  const fullName = values.fullName.trim();

  if (!fullName) errors.fullName = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p há» vÃ  tÃªn.";
  else if (fullName.length < 2) errors.fullName = "Há» vÃ  tÃªn cáº§n cÃ³ Ã­t nháº¥t 2 kÃ½ tá»±.";
  else if (fullName.length > 100) errors.fullName = "Há» vÃ  tÃªn khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 100 kÃ½ tá»±.";

  if (!values.email.trim()) errors.email = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p email.";
  else if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(values.email.trim())) {
    errors.email = "Email chÆ°a Ä‘Ãºng Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng.";
  }

  if (!values.password) errors.password = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p máº­t kháº©u.";
  else if (values.password.length < 8) errors.password = "Máº­t kháº©u cáº§n cÃ³ Ã­t nháº¥t 8 kÃ½ tá»±.";

  if (!values.confirmPassword) errors.confirmPassword = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p láº¡i máº­t kháº©u.";
  else if (values.confirmPassword !== values.password) {
    errors.confirmPassword = "Máº­t kháº©u nháº­p láº¡i chÆ°a khá»›p.";
  }

  return errors;
}

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const [values, setValues] = useState(initialValues);
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  const [formError, setFormError] = useState("");
  const [success, setSuccess] = useState(false);

  function updateField(field: FieldName, value: string) {
    setValues((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: value }));
    setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, [field]: undefined }));
    setFormError("");
    setSuccess(false);
  }

  function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const validationErrors = validate(values);
    setErrors(validationErrors);
    setFormError("");
    setSuccess(false);

    if (Object.keys(validationErrors).length > 0) return;

    try {
      registerUser(values);
      setValues(initialValues);
      setSuccess(true);
    } catch (error) {
      setFormError(
        error instanceof MockAuthError
          ? error.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ lÆ°u tÃ i khoáº£n trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.",
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <AuthShell
      eyebrow="Báº¯t Ä‘áº§u cÃ¹ng UniLend"
      title="Táº¡o tÃ i khoáº£n"
      description="Tham gia cá»™ng Ä‘á»“ng sinh viÃªn chia sáº» nhá»¯ng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ há»¯u Ã­ch."
    >
      {success ? (
        <div className="auth-feedback success-feedback" role="status">
          <strong>Táº¡o tÃ i khoáº£n thÃ nh cÃ´ng!</strong>
          <span>TÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÆ°u trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t nÃ y. ÄÄƒng nháº­p Ä‘á»ƒ xem há»“ sÆ¡ cá»§a báº¡n.</span>
          <Link className="auth-submit" href="/login">Äi Ä‘áº¿n Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p</Link>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <form className="auth-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit} noValidate>
          {formError && <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{formError}</p>}
          <div className="form-field">
            <label htmlFor="fullName">Há» vÃ  tÃªn</label>
            <input
              autoComplete="name"
              id="fullName"
              name="fullName"
              value={values.fullName}
              onChange={(event) => updateField("fullName", event.target.value)}
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.fullName)}
              aria-describedby={errors.fullName ? "fullName-error" : undefined}
              placeholder="Nguyá»…n Minh Anh"
            />
            {errors.fullName && <span className="field-error" id="fullName-error">{errors.fullName}</span>}
          </div>
          <div className="form-field">
            <label htmlFor="email">Email</label>
            <input
              autoComplete="email"
              id="email"
              name="email"
              type="email"
              value={values.email}
              onChange={(event) => updateField("email", event.target.value)}
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.email)}
              aria-describedby={errors.email ? "email-error" : undefined}
              placeholder="ban@example.com"
            />
            {errors.email && <span className="field-error" id="email-error">{errors.email}</span>}
          </div>
          <div className="form-field">
            <label htmlFor="password">Máº­t kháº©u</label>
            <input
              autoComplete="new-password"
              id="password"
              name="password"
              type="password"
              value={values.password}
              onChange={(event) => updateField("password", event.target.value)}
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.password)}
              aria-describedby={errors.password ? "password-error" : "password-hint"}
              placeholder="Ãt nháº¥t 8 kÃ½ tá»±"
            />
            {errors.password
              ? <span className="field-error" id="password-error">{errors.password}</span>
              : <span className="field-hint" id="password-hint">Sá»­ dá»¥ng Ã­t nháº¥t 8 kÃ½ tá»±.</span>}
          </div>
          <div className="form-field">
            <label htmlFor="confirmPassword">Nháº­p láº¡i máº­t kháº©u</label>
            <input
              autoComplete="new-password"
              id="confirmPassword"
              name="confirmPassword"
              type="password"
              value={values.confirmPassword}
              onChange={(event) => updateField("confirmPassword", event.target.value)}
              aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.confirmPassword)}
              aria-describedby={errors.confirmPassword ? "confirmPassword-error" : undefined}
              placeholder="Nháº­p láº¡i máº­t kháº©u"
            />
            {errors.confirmPassword && <span className="field-error" id="confirmPassword-error">{errors.confirmPassword}</span>}
          </div>
          <button className="auth-submit" type="submit">Táº¡o tÃ i khoáº£n</button>
          <p className="auth-switch">ÄÃ£ cÃ³ tÃ i khoáº£n? <Link href="/login">ÄÄƒng nháº­p</Link></p>
        </form>
      )}
    </AuthShell>
  );
}

  { name: "Ä‘iá»‡n thoáº¡i", category: "CÃ´ng nghá»‡", owner: "Sang Tráº§n", initials: "TH", icon: "calculator", color: "pink" }
];

function Icon({ name, size = 20 }: { name: ProductIcon | "search" | "arrow" | "spark" | "pin" | "box"; size?: number }) {
  const common = {
    width: size,
    height: size,
    viewBox: "0 0 24 24",
    fill: "none",
    stroke: "currentColor",
    strokeWidth: 1.8,
    strokeLinecap: "round" as const,
    strokeLinejoin: "round" as const,
    "aria-hidden": true as const,
  };

  switch (name) {
    case "search":
      return <svg {...common}><circle cx="11" cy="11" r="7" /><path d="m16 16 4 4" /></svg>;
    case "arrow":
      return <svg {...common}><path d="M5 12h14m-6-6 6 6-6 6" /></svg>;
    case "spark":
      return <svg {...common}><path d="m12 3 1.7 5.3L19 10l-5.3 1.7L12 17l-1.7-5.3L5 10l5.3-1.7L12 3Z" /><path d="m19 16 .8 2.2L22 19l-2.2.8L19 22l-.8-2.2L16 19l2.2-.8L19 16Z" /></svg>;
    case "pin":
      return <svg {...common}><path d="M20 10c0 5-8 11-8 11S4 15 4 10a8 8 0 1 1 16 0Z" /><circle cx="12" cy="10" r="2.5" /></svg>;
    case "box":
      return <svg {...common}><path d="m12 3 9 5-9 5-9-5 9-5Z" /><path d="m3 8 9 5 9-5m-18 0v8l9 5 9-5V8m-9 5v8" /></svg>;
    case "book":
      return <svg {...common}><path d="M12 7v14m0-14C9 4 5.5 4 3 5v13c2.5-1 6-.8 9 2m0-13c3-3 6.5-3 9-2v13c-2.5-1-6-.8-9 2" /><path d="M6 8h3m-3 4h3m6-4h3m-3 4h3" /></svg>;
    case "camera":
      return <svg {...common}><path d="M4 7h3l1.5-2h7L17 7h3a1 1 0 0 1 1 1v11H3V8a1 1 0 0 1 1-1Z" /><circle cx="12" cy="13" r="4" /></svg>;
    case "projector":
      return <svg {...common}><rect x="3" y="6" width="18" height="11" rx="2" /><circle cx="12" cy="11.5" r="2.5" /><path d="m8 17-1 4m9-4 1 4M3 9H1m22 0h-2" /></svg>;
    case "headphones":
      return <svg {...common}><path d="M3 13v-2a9 9 0 0 1 18 0v2" /><path d="M5 13h2v7H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2v-3a2 2 0 0 1 2-2Zm14 0h-2v7h2a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-3a2 2 0 0 0-2-2Z" /></svg>;
    case "plant":
      return <svg {...common}><path d="M12 21v-9m0 3c-5 0-7-3-7-7 4 0 7 2 7 7Zm0-3c0-5 3-8 8-8 0 5-3 8-8 8Zm-6 9h12l-1-6H7l-1 6Z" /></svg>;
    case "calculator":

  }
}

===== frontend/components/admin-modal.tsx =====
"use client";

import {
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  type MouseEvent,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";

type AdminModalProps = {
  children: ReactNode;
  onClose: () => void;
  titleId: string;
};

const FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR = [
  "a[href]",
  "button:not([disabled])",
  "input:not([disabled])",
  "select:not([disabled])",
  "textarea:not([disabled])",
  '[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])',
].join(", ");

export function AdminModal({ children, onClose, titleId }: AdminModalProps) {
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const onCloseRef = useRef(onClose);

  useEffect(() => {
    onCloseRef.current = onClose;
  }, [onClose]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const previousOverflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    const previousFocus = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement
      ? document.activeElement
      : null;
    const dialog = dialogRef.current;
    const focusable = dialog?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR);
    (focusable?.[0] ?? dialog)?.focus();
    document.body.style.overflow = "hidden";

    function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key === "Escape") {
        event.preventDefault();
        onCloseRef.current();
        return;
      }
      if (event.key !== "Tab" || !dialog) return;

      const elements = dialog.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE_SELECTOR);
      const first = elements[0];
      const last = elements[elements.length - 1];
      if (!first || !last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        dialog.focus();
      } else if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        event.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        event.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
      document.body.style.overflow = previousOverflow;
      previousFocus?.focus();
    };
  }, []);

  function handleBackdropClick(event: MouseEvent<HTMLDivElement>) {
    if (event.target === event.currentTarget) onClose();
  }

  return (
    <div
      className="admin-modal-backdrop"
      onMouseDown={handleBackdropClick}
    >
      <section
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
        aria-modal="true"
        className="admin-modal"
        ref={dialogRef}
        role="dialog"
        tabIndex={-1}
      >
        {children}
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/admin-toast.tsx =====
"use client";

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import styles from "./admin-toast.module.css";

type ToastMessage = {
  id: number;
  kind: "success" | "error";
  text: string;
};

type ToastContextValue = {
  showToast: (kind: ToastMessage["kind"], text: string) => void;
};

const ToastContext = createContext<ToastContextValue | null>(null);

export function AdminToastProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [toasts, setToasts] = useState<ToastMessage[]>([]);
  const nextId = useRef(0);
  const timers = useRef(new Map<number, ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>>());

  const showToast = useCallback((kind: ToastMessage["kind"], text: string) => {
    const id = ++nextId.current;
    setToasts((current) => [...current, { id, kind, text }]);
    timers.current.set(
      id,
      setTimeout(() => {
        timers.current.delete(id);
        setToasts((current) => current.filter((toast) => toast.id !== id));
      }, 4500),
    );
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    for (const timer of timers.current.values()) clearTimeout(timer);
    timers.current.clear();
  }, []);

  const dismissToast = useCallback((id: number) => {
    const timer = timers.current.get(id);
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer);
    timers.current.delete(id);
    setToasts((current) => current.filter((toast) => toast.id !== id));
  }, []);

  return (
    <ToastContext.Provider value={{ showToast }}>
      {children}
      {toasts.length > 0 && (
        <div className={styles.toastContainer} aria-label="ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o">
          {toasts.map((toast) => (
            <div
              className={`${styles.toast} ${toast.kind === "success" ? styles.success : styles.error}`}
              key={toast.id}
              role={toast.kind === "error" ? "alert" : "status"}
            >
              <span>{toast.text}</span>
              <button
                aria-label="ÄÃ³ng thÃ´ng bÃ¡o"
                onClick={() => dismissToast(toast.id)}
                type="button"
              >
                Ã—
              </button>
            </div>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </ToastContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useAdminToast(): ToastContextValue {
  const context = useContext(ToastContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error("useAdminToast must be used within AdminToastProvider.");
  return context;
}

===== frontend/components/api-error-notice.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useAdminToast } from "./admin-toast";
import { API_ERROR_EVENT } from "../lib/api/apiClient";

type ApiErrorEvent = CustomEvent<{ message: string }>;

export function ApiErrorNotice() {
  const { showToast } = useAdminToast();

  useEffect(() => {
    function handleApiError(event: Event) {
      const detail = (event as ApiErrorEvent).detail;
      if (detail && typeof detail.message === "string") showToast("error", detail.message);
    }

    window.addEventListener(API_ERROR_EVENT, handleApiError);
    return () => window.removeEventListener(API_ERROR_EVENT, handleApiError);
  }, [showToast]);

  return null;
}

===== frontend/components/auth-provider.tsx =====
"use client";

import {
  createContext,
  useContext,
  useMemo,
  useSyncExternalStore,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import {
  getCurrentUser,
  getSessionSnapshot,
  subscribeToAuth,
  type UserProfile,
} from "../lib/mock-auth";

type AuthState =
  | { status: "loading"; user: null; error: null }
  | { status: "unauthenticated"; user: null; error: null }
  | { status: "authenticated"; user: UserProfile; error: null }
  | { status: "error"; user: null; error: string };

type AuthContextValue = AuthState & {
  isAuthenticated: boolean;
  hasRole: (role: UserProfile["roles"][number]) => boolean;
};

const AuthContext = createContext<AuthContextValue | null>(null);

export function AuthProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const sessionSnapshot = useSyncExternalStore(
    subscribeToAuth,
    getSessionSnapshot,
    () => "__loading__",
  );

  const state = useMemo<AuthState>(() => {
    if (sessionSnapshot === "__loading__") {
      return { status: "loading", user: null, error: null };
    }
    if (!sessionSnapshot) {
      return { status: "unauthenticated", user: null, error: null };
    }

    try {
      const user = getCurrentUser();
      return user
        ? { status: "authenticated", user, error: null }
        : { status: "unauthenticated", user: null, error: null };
    } catch (error) {
      return {
        status: "error",
        user: null,
        error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ¡c thá»±c phiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p.",
      };
    }
  }, [sessionSnapshot]);

  const value = useMemo<AuthContextValue>(
    () => ({
      ...state,
      isAuthenticated: state.status === "authenticated",
      hasRole: (role) => state.status === "authenticated" && state.user.roles.includes(role),
    }),
    [state],
  );

  return <AuthContext.Provider value={value}>{children}</AuthContext.Provider>;
}

export function useAuth(): AuthContextValue {
  const context = useContext(AuthContext);
  if (!context) throw new Error("useAuth must be used within AuthProvider.");
  return context;
}

===== frontend/components/category-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import type { Category } from "../lib/api/types";
import type { CategoryInput } from "../lib/api/categoryService";
import { AdminModal } from "./admin-modal";

type CategoryFormProps = {
  category: Category | null;
  categories: Category[];
  isSaving: boolean;
  serverError: string;
  onCancel: () => void;
  onSave: (input: CategoryInput) => void;
};

type FieldErrors = {
  name?: string;
  description?: string;
};

function normalizeName(value: string): string {
  return value.toLocaleLowerCase("vi");
}

export function CategoryForm({
  category,
  categories,
  isSaving,
  serverError,
  onCancel,
  onSave,
}: CategoryFormProps) {
  const initialName = category?.name ?? "";
  const initialDescription = category?.description ?? "";
  const [name, setName] = useState(initialName);
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(initialDescription);
  const [fieldErrors, setFieldErrors] = useState<FieldErrors>({});
  const isDirty =
    name !== initialName || description !== initialDescription;

  function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const trimmedName = name.trim();
    const trimmedDescription = description.trim();
    const nextErrors: FieldErrors = {};

    if (!trimmedName) nextErrors.name = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p tÃªn danh má»¥c.";
    else if (trimmedName.length > 100) {
      nextErrors.name = "TÃªn danh má»¥c khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 100 kÃ½ tá»±.";
    } else if (
      categories.some(
        (item) =>
          item.id !== category?.id &&
          normalizeName(item.name) === normalizeName(trimmedName),
      )
    ) {
      nextErrors.name = "TÃªn danh má»¥c nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ tá»“n táº¡i.";
    }
    if (trimmedDescription.length > 255) {
      nextErrors.description = "MÃ´ táº£ khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ 255 kÃ½ tá»±.";
    }
    setFieldErrors(nextErrors);
    if (Object.keys(nextErrors).length > 0) return;

    onSave({ name: trimmedName, description: trimmedDescription });
  }

  const titleId = "category-form-title";

  return (
    <AdminModal onClose={onCancel} titleId={titleId}>
      <h2 id={titleId}>{category ? "Chá»‰nh sá»­a danh má»¥c" : "ThÃªm danh má»¥c"}</h2>
      <form className="admin-category-form" onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
        {serverError && <p className="form-alert" role="alert">{serverError}</p>}
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor="category-name">TÃªn danh má»¥c</label>
          <input
            autoFocus
            aria-describedby={fieldErrors.name ? "category-name-error" : undefined}
            aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors.name)}
            className="admin-form-input"
            id="category-name"
            maxLength={100}
            onChange={(event) => setName(event.target.value)}
            value={name}
          />
          {fieldErrors.name && (
            <span className="field-error" id="category-name-error">{fieldErrors.name}</span>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="form-field">
          <label htmlFor="category-description">MÃ´ táº£</label>
          <textarea
            aria-describedby={
              fieldErrors.description ? "category-description-error" : undefined
            }
            aria-invalid={Boolean(fieldErrors.description)}
            className="admin-form-input admin-form-textarea"
            id="category-description"
            maxLength={255}
            onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
            value={description}
          />
          {fieldErrors.description && (
            <span className="field-error" id="category-description-error">
              {fieldErrors.description}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
        <div className="admin-modal-actions">
          <button
            className="admin-secondary-button"
            disabled={isSaving}
            onClick={onCancel}
            type="button"
          >
            Há»§y
          </button>
          <button
            className="admin-primary-button"
            disabled={isSaving || !isDirty}
            type="submit"
          >
            {isSaving ? "Äang lÆ°u..." : category ? "LÆ°u thay Ä‘á»•i" : "ThÃªm danh má»¥c"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </form>
    </AdminModal>
  );
}

type ConfirmModalProps = {

export function ConfirmModal({
  title,

  pendingLabel,

===== frontend/components/confirmation-dialog.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useId, useRef, type ReactNode } from "react";
import styles from "./confirmation-dialog.module.css";

type ConfirmationDialogProps = {
  title: string;
  children: ReactNode;
  confirmLabel: string;
  cancelLabel?: string;
  pendingLabel?: string;
  busy?: boolean;
  tone?: "primary" | "danger";
  onConfirm: () => void;
  onCancel: () => void;
};

export function ConfirmationDialog({
  title,
  children,
  confirmLabel,
  cancelLabel = "Há»§y",
  pendingLabel = "Äang xá»­ lÃ½...",
  busy = false,
  tone = "primary",
  onConfirm,
  onCancel,
}: ConfirmationDialogProps) {
  const titleId = useId();
  const descriptionId = useId();
  const dialogRef = useRef<HTMLElement>(null);
  const cancelButtonRef = useRef<HTMLButtonElement>(null);
  const busyRef = useRef(busy);
  const onCancelRef = useRef(onCancel);
  busyRef.current = busy;
  onCancelRef.current = onCancel;

  useEffect(() => {
    const previouslyFocused = document.activeElement;
    cancelButtonRef.current?.focus();

    function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key === "Escape" && !busyRef.current) {
        event.preventDefault();
        onCancelRef.current();
        return;
      }

      if (event.key !== "Tab") return;
      const buttons = dialogRef.current?.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>("button:not(:disabled)");
      if (!buttons?.length) {
        event.preventDefault();
        return;
      }

      const firstButton = buttons.item(0);
      const lastButton = buttons.item(buttons.length - 1);
      if (!firstButton || !lastButton) return;
      if (event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === firstButton) {
        event.preventDefault();
        lastButton.focus();
      } else if (!event.shiftKey && document.activeElement === lastButton) {
        event.preventDefault();
        firstButton.focus();
      }
    }

    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
      if (previouslyFocused instanceof HTMLElement) previouslyFocused.focus();
    };
  }, []);

  return (
    <div className={styles.backdrop}>
      <section
        aria-describedby={descriptionId}
        aria-labelledby={titleId}
        aria-modal="true"
        className={styles.dialog}
        ref={dialogRef}
        role="alertdialog"
        tabIndex={-1}
      >
        <h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2>
        <div className={styles.description} id={descriptionId}>{children}</div>
        <div className={styles.actions}>
          <button
            className={styles.cancel}
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={onCancel}
            ref={cancelButtonRef}
            type="button"
          >
            {cancelLabel}
          </button>
          <button
            className={tone === "danger" ? styles.danger : styles.primary}
            disabled={busy}
            onClick={onConfirm}
            type="button"
          >
            {busy ? pendingLabel : confirmLabel}
          </button>
        </div>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/ImageUploader.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useEffect, useId, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { resolveImageUrl } from "../lib/api/imageUrl";
import styles from "./ImageUploader.module.css";

export interface ExistingImage {
  id: number;
  url: string;
}

interface ImageUploaderProps {
  value: File[];
  onChange: (files: File[]) => void;
  maxFiles?: number;
  maxSizeMB?: number;
  existingImages?: ExistingImage[];
  onRemoveExisting?: (imageId: number) => void;
  error?: string;
  disabled?: boolean;
}

interface Preview {
  file: File;
  url: string;
}

const MIME_EXTENSIONS: Record<string, readonly string[]> = {
  "image/jpeg": [".jpg", ".jpeg"],
  "image/jpg": [".jpg", ".jpeg"],
  "image/png": [".png"],
};

function fileKey(file: File): string {
  return JSON.stringify([file.name, file.size, file.lastModified]);
}

export function ImageUploader({
  value,
  onChange,
  maxFiles = 5,
  maxSizeMB = 5,
  existingImages = [],
  onRemoveExisting,
  error,
  disabled = false,
}: ImageUploaderProps) {
  const inputId = useId();
  const inputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement>(null);
  const objectUrls = useRef(new Map<File, string>());
  const [previews, setPreviews] = useState<Preview[]>([]);
  const [validationErrors, setValidationErrors] = useState<string[]>([]);
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const remainingSlots = Math.max(0, maxFiles - existingImages.length - value.length);

  useEffect(() => {
    const selectedFiles = new Set(value);
    for (const [file, url] of objectUrls.current) {
      if (!selectedFiles.has(file)) {
        URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
        objectUrls.current.delete(file);
      }
    }

    setPreviews(value.map((file) => {
      let url = objectUrls.current.get(file);
      if (!url) {
        url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
        objectUrls.current.set(file, url);
      }
      return { file, url };
    }));
  }, [value]);

  useEffect(() => () => {
    for (const url of objectUrls.current.values()) {
      URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
    }
    objectUrls.current.clear();
  }, []);

  function addFiles(fileList: FileList | null) {
    if (!fileList) return;

    const accepted: File[] = [];
    const issues: string[] = [];
    const seen = new Set(value.map(fileKey));

    for (const file of Array.from(fileList)) {
      const extension = file.name.slice(file.name.lastIndexOf(".")).toLowerCase();
      const acceptedExtensions = MIME_EXTENSIONS[file.type.toLowerCase()];

      if (!acceptedExtensions || !acceptedExtensions.includes(extension)) {
        issues.push(`${file.name}: chá»‰ cháº¥p nháº­n áº£nh JPG, JPEG hoáº·c PNG Ä‘Ãºng Ä‘á»‹nh dáº¡ng.`);
        continue;
      }
      if (file.size > maxSizeMB * 1024 * 1024) {
        issues.push(`${file.name}: dung lÆ°á»£ng khÃ´ng Ä‘Æ°á»£c vÆ°á»£t quÃ¡ ${maxSizeMB} MB.`);
        continue;
      }
      if (seen.has(fileKey(file))) {
        issues.push(`${file.name}: áº£nh nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c chá»n.`);
        continue;
      }
      if (existingImages.length + value.length + accepted.length >= maxFiles) {
        issues.push(`${file.name}: Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘áº¡t giá»›i háº¡n tá»‘i Ä‘a ${maxFiles} áº£nh.`);
        continue;
      }

      seen.add(fileKey(file));
      accepted.push(file);
    }

    if (accepted.length > 0) onChange([...value, ...accepted]);
    setValidationErrors(issues);
  }

  const displayedError = [error, ...validationErrors].filter(Boolean).join(" ");

  return (
    <section className={styles.upload} aria-label="áº¢nh mÃ³n Ä‘á»“">
      <div className={styles.heading}>
        <div>
          <h2>HÃ¬nh áº£nh mÃ³n Ä‘á»“</h2>
          <p>Táº£i lÃªn tá»‘i Ä‘a {maxFiles} áº£nh, má»—i áº£nh khÃ´ng quÃ¡ {maxSizeMB} MB.</p>
        </div>
        <span className={styles.imageCount}>
          {existingImages.length + value.length}/{maxFiles}
        </span>
      </div>

      <div className={styles.previewGrid}>
        {existingImages.map((image, index) => (
          <figure className={styles.preview} key={image.id}>
            <Image
              alt={`áº¢nh mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ hiá»‡n táº¡i ${index + 1}`}
              className={styles.previewImage}
              src={resolveImageUrl(image.url)}
              fill
              sizes="(max-width: 600px) 40vw, 180px"
              unoptimized
            />
            {index === 0 && <figcaption className={styles.previewLabel}>áº¢nh Ä‘áº¡i diá»‡n</figcaption>}
            {onRemoveExisting && (
              <button
                aria-label={`XÃ³a áº£nh hiá»‡n táº¡i ${index + 1}`}
                className={styles.removeButton}
                disabled={disabled}
                onClick={() => onRemoveExisting(image.id)}
                type="button"
              >
                Ã—
              </button>
            )}
          </figure>
        ))}
        {previews.map((preview, index) => (
          <figure className={styles.preview} key={fileKey(preview.file)}>
            <Image
              alt={`áº¢nh xem trÆ°á»›c: ${preview.file.name}`}
              className={styles.previewImage}
              src={preview.url}
              fill
              sizes="(max-width: 600px) 40vw, 180px"
              unoptimized
            />
            {existingImages.length + index === 0 && (
              <figcaption className={styles.previewLabel}>áº¢nh Ä‘áº¡i diá»‡n</figcaption>
            )}
            <button
              aria-label={`XÃ³a áº£nh ${preview.file.name}`}
              className={styles.removeButton}
              disabled={disabled}
              onClick={() => onChange(value.filter((file) => file !== preview.file))}
              type="button"
            >
              Ã—
            </button>
          </figure>
        ))}
        {remainingSlots > 0 && (
          <button
            aria-describedby={displayedError ? `${inputId}-error` : undefined}
            className={`${styles.addTile}${isDragging ? ` ${styles.dragging}` : ""}`}
            disabled={disabled}
            onClick={() => inputRef.current?.click()}
            onDragEnter={(event) => {
              event.preventDefault();
              if (!disabled) setIsDragging(true);
            }}
            onDragLeave={() => setIsDragging(false)}
            onDragOver={(event) => event.preventDefault()}
            onDrop={(event) => {
              event.preventDefault();
              setIsDragging(false);
              if (!disabled) addFiles(event.dataTransfer.files);
            }}
            type="button"
          >
            <span className={styles.addIcon} aria-hidden="true">ï¼‹</span>
            <span>KÃ©o tháº£ hoáº·c chá»n áº£nh</span>
          </button>
        )}
        <input
          accept=".jpg,.jpeg,.png,image/jpeg,image/png"
          aria-hidden="true"
          className={styles.visuallyHidden}
          disabled={disabled || remainingSlots === 0}
          id={inputId}
          aria-label="Chá»n áº£nh mÃ³n Ä‘á»“"
          multiple
          onChange={(event) => {
            addFiles(event.currentTarget.files);
            event.currentTarget.value = "";
          }}
          ref={inputRef}
          tabIndex={-1}
          type="file"
        />
      </div>

      {displayedError && (
        <p className={`field-error ${styles.error}`} id={`${inputId}-error`} role="alert">
          {displayedError}
        </p>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/item-form.module.css =====
.page {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background: var(--canvas);
  color: var(--ink);
}

.header {
  width: min(1160px, calc(100% - 48px));
  min-height: 82px;
  margin: 0 auto;
  display: flex;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
}

.brand {
