===== frontend/components/item-form.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { useAdminToast } from "./admin-toast";
import { ItemImageUpload } from "./item-image-upload";
import { NotificationBell } from "./notification-bell";
import { useAuth } from "./auth-provider";
import { categoryService } from "../lib/api/categoryService";
import { itemService } from "../lib/api/itemService";
import type { Category, Item, ItemInput } from "../lib/api/types";
import styles from "./item-form.module.css";

type FormErrors = {
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
};

export function ItemForm({ itemId }: { itemId: number | null }) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const auth = useAuth();
  const { showToast } = useAdminToast();
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  const [categoryError, setCategoryError] = useState("");
  const [item, setItem] = useState<Item | null>(null);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState("");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState("");
  const [categoryId, setCategoryId] = useState("");
  const [imageFiles, setImageFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState<Item["status"]>("AVAILABLE");
  const [errors, setErrors] = useState<FormErrors>({});
  const [loadError, setLoadError] = useState("");
  const [saveError, setSaveError] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const editing = itemId !== null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (auth.status === "unauthenticated") router.replace("/login");
  }, [auth.status, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadCategories() {
      try {
        setCategories(await categoryService.list({ signal: controller.signal }));
      } catch (loadCategoriesError) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setCategoryError(
          loadCategoriesError instanceof Error
            ? loadCategoriesError.message
            : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i danh má»¥c.",
        );
      }
    }

    void loadCategories();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editing || itemId === null || !Number.isSafeInteger(itemId) || itemId < 1) return;
    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadItem() {
      setLoadError("");
      try {
        const result = await itemService.getById(itemId, { signal: controller.signal });
        setItem(result);
        setTitle(result.title);
        setDescription(result.description ?? "");
        setCategoryId(result.category ? String(result.category.id) : "");
        setStatus(result.status);
      } catch (loadItemError) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setLoadError(
          loadItemError instanceof Error ? loadItemError.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i thÃ´ng tin mÃ³n Ä‘á»“.",
        );
      }
    }

    void loadItem();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [editing, itemId]);

  function validate(): FormErrors {
    const nextErrors: FormErrors = {};
    const trimmedTitle = title.trim();
    if (!trimmedTitle) nextErrors.title = "Vui lÃ²ng nháº­p tÃªn mÃ³n Ä‘á»“.";
    else if (trimmedTitle.length > 150) nextErrors.title = "TÃªn mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ tá»‘i Ä‘a 150 kÃ½ tá»±.";

    if (description.length > 5000) {
      nextErrors.description = "MÃ´ táº£ tá»‘i Ä‘a 5000 kÃ½ tá»±.";
    }
    return nextErrors;
  }

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    const nextErrors = validate();
    setErrors(nextErrors);
    setSaveError("");
    if (Object.keys(nextErrors).length > 0) return;

    const input: ItemInput = {
      title: title.trim(),
      description: description.trim(),
      ...(categoryId ? { categoryId: Number(categoryId) } : {}),
      ...(editing && status !== "LENT_OUT" ? { status } : {}),
    };

    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (editing && itemId !== null) {
        await itemService.update(itemId, input);
        if (imageFiles.length > 0) await itemService.uploadImages(itemId, imageFiles);
        showToast("success", "ÄÃ£ cáº­p nháº­t mÃ³n Ä‘á»“.");
      } else {
        const createdItem = await itemService.create(input);
        if (imageFiles.length > 0) {
          try {
            await itemService.uploadImages(createdItem.id, imageFiles);
          } catch (uploadError) {
            showToast(
              "error",
              `ÄÃ£ Ä‘Äƒng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ nhÆ°ng táº£i áº£nh tháº¥t báº¡i: ${
                uploadError instanceof Error ? uploadError.message : "Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ táº£i áº£nh láº¡i."
              }`,
            );
            router.push(`/items/${createdItem.id}/edit`);
            return;
          }
        }
        showToast("success", "ÄÃ£ Ä‘Äƒng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ má»›i.");
      }
      router.push("/my");
    } catch (saveError) {
      setSaveError(saveError instanceof Error ? saveError.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ lÆ°u mÃ³n Ä‘á»“.");
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  const invalidItemId = editing && (!Number.isSafeInteger(itemId) || (itemId ?? 0) < 1);
  const loadingItem = editing && !item && !loadError && !invalidItemId;

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <Link className={styles.brand} href="/" aria-label="UniLend - vá» trang chá»§">
          <span className={styles.brandMark} aria-hidden="true">U</span>
          <span>uni<span className={styles.brandAccent}>lend</span></span>
        </Link>
        <div className={styles.headerActions}>
          <NotificationBell />
          <Link className={styles.backLink} href="/my">Quáº£n lÃ½ cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n</Link>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className={styles.content}>
        <Link className={styles.backText} href="/my">â† Quay láº¡i quáº£n lÃ½ cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n</Link>
        <div className={styles.formHeading}>
          <span className={styles.eyebrow}>{editing ? "Cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin" : "Chia sáº» cÃ¹ng cá»™ng Ä‘á»“ng"}</span>
          <h1>{editing ? "Sá»­a mÃ³n Ä‘á»“" : "ÄÄƒng Ä‘á»“ má»›i"}</h1>
          <p>{editing ? "Cáº­p nháº­t thÃ´ng tin Ä‘á»ƒ má»i ngÆ°á»i hiá»ƒu rÃµ hÆ¡n vá» mÃ³n Ä‘á»“." : "ThÃªm thÃ´ng tin mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ä‘á»ƒ báº¡n bÃ¨ quanh trÆ°á»ng cÃ³ thá»ƒ tÃ¬m tháº¥y."}</p>
        </div>

        {auth.status === "loading" || auth.status === "unauthenticated" || loadingItem ? (
          <div className={styles.formSkeleton} aria-label="Äang táº£i thÃ´ng tin" aria-busy="true">
            <span className={styles.visuallyHidden} role="status">Äang táº£i thÃ´ng tin</span>
            <div className={`${styles.skeletonLine} ${styles.skeletonHeading}`} />
            <div className={`${styles.skeletonLine} ${styles.skeletonLabel}`} />
            <div className={`${styles.skeletonLine} ${styles.skeletonField}`} />
            <div className={`${styles.skeletonLine} ${styles.skeletonLabel}`} />
            <div className={`${styles.skeletonLine} ${styles.skeletonArea}`} />
            <div className={`${styles.skeletonLine} ${styles.skeletonField}`} />
          </div>
        ) : auth.status === "error" ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel} role="alert">{auth.error}</div>
        ) : invalidItemId ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel} role="alert">
            <p>MÃ£ mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ khÃ´ng há»£p lá»‡ hoáº·c mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ khÃ´ng cÃ²n tá»“n táº¡i.</p>
            <Link className={styles.secondaryButton} href="/my">Quay láº¡i Ä‘á»“ cá»§a tÃ´i</Link>
          </div>
        ) : loadError ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel} role="alert">
            <p>{loadError}</p>
            <Link className={styles.secondaryButton} href="/my">Quay láº¡i Ä‘á»“ cá»§a tÃ´i</Link>
          </div>
        ) : (
          <form className={styles.formCard} noValidate onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            {saveError && <p className={styles.formError} role="alert">{saveError}</p>}
            <div className={styles.formSectionHeading}>
              <span className={styles.step}>01</span>
              <div>
                <h2>ThÃ´ng tin mÃ³n Ä‘á»“</h2>
                <p>CÃ¡c trÆ°á»ng cÃ³ dáº¥u * lÃ  báº¯t buá»™c.</p>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className={styles.field}>
              <label htmlFor="item-title">TÃªn mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ <span aria-hidden="true">*</span></label>
              <input
                aria-describedby={errors.title ? "title-error" : "title-count"}
                aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.title)}
                autoComplete="off"
                id="item-title"
                maxLength={150}
                placeholder="VÃ­ dá»¥: MÃ¡y chiáº¿u mini"
                value={title}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setTitle(event.target.value);
                  if (errors.title) setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, title: undefined }));
                }}
              />
              <div className={styles.fieldFooter}>
                {errors.title
                  ? <span className={styles.fieldError} id="title-error">{errors.title}</span>
                  : <span />}
                <span className={styles.characterCount} id="title-count">{title.length}/150</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className={styles.field}>
              <label htmlFor="item-description">MÃ´ táº£</label>
              <textarea
                aria-describedby={errors.description ? "description-error" : "description-count"}
                aria-invalid={Boolean(errors.description)}
                id="item-description"
                maxLength={5000}
                placeholder="MÃ´ táº£ tÃ¬nh tráº¡ng, cÃ¡ch sá»­ dá»¥ng hoáº·c lÆ°u Ã½ khi mÆ°á»£n..."
                rows={5}
                value={description}
                onChange={(event) => {
                  setDescription(event.target.value);
                  if (errors.description) setErrors((current) => ({ ...current, description: undefined }));
                }}
              />
              <div className={styles.fieldFooter}>
                {errors.description
                  ? <span className={styles.fieldError} id="description-error">{errors.description}</span>
                  : <span />}
                <span className={styles.characterCount} id="description-count">{description.length}/5000</span>
              </div>
            </div>

            <div className={styles.fieldGrid}>
              <div className={styles.field}>
                <label htmlFor="item-category">Danh má»¥c</label>
                <select
                  id="item-category"
                  value={categoryId}
                  onChange={(event) => setCategoryId(event.target.value)}
                  disabled={Boolean(categoryError)}
                >
                  <option value="">ChÆ°a phÃ¢n loáº¡i</option>
                  {categories.map((category) => (
                    <option key={category.id} value={category.id}>{category.name}</option>
                  ))}
                </select>
                {categoryError && <p className={styles.fieldError} role="alert">{categoryError}</p>}
              </div>

              {editing && (
                <div className={styles.field}>
                  <label htmlFor="item-status">Tráº¡ng thÃ¡i</label>
                  <select
                    id="item-status"
                    value={status}
                    disabled={status === "LENT_OUT"}
                    onChange={(event) => {
                      const nextStatus = event.target.value;
                      if (nextStatus === "AVAILABLE" || nextStatus === "UNAVAILABLE") {
                        setStatus(nextStatus);
                      }
                    }}
                  >
                    <option value="AVAILABLE">CÃ³ thá»ƒ mÆ°á»£n</option>
                    <option value="UNAVAILABLE">Táº¡m ngÆ°ng</option>
                    {status === "LENT_OUT" && <option value="LENT_OUT">Äang Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÆ°á»£n</option>}
                  </select>
                  {status === "LENT_OUT" && (
                    <p className={styles.fieldHint}>KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»•i tráº¡ng thÃ¡i khi mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ä‘ang Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÆ°á»£n.</p>
                  )}
                </div>
              )}
            </div>

            <div className={styles.imageUpload}>
              <ItemImageUpload
                disabled={submitting}
                existingImages={item?.imageUrls ?? []}
                maxImages={5}
                onChange={setImageFiles}
                onRemoveExisting={(imageUrl) => {
                  if (!item) return;
                  const image = item.images.find((entry) => entry.imageUrl === imageUrl);
                  if (!image) {
                    showToast("error", "KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y thÃ´ng tin áº£nh Ä‘á»ƒ xÃ³a. Vui lÃ²ng táº£i láº¡i trang.");
                    return;
                  }
                  void itemService.deleteImage(item.id, image.id)
                    .then(setItem)
                    .catch((deleteError: unknown) => {
                      showToast(
                        "error",
                        deleteError instanceof Error ? deleteError.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ³a áº£nh.",
                      );
                    });
                }}
                value={imageFiles}
              />
            </div>

            <div className={styles.formActions}>
              <Link className={styles.cancelLink} href="/my">Há»§y</Link>
              <button className={styles.submitButton} disabled={submitting} type="submit">
                {submitting ? "Äang lÆ°u..." : editing ? "LÆ°u thay Ä‘á»•i" : "ÄÄƒng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </section>
    </main>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/item-image-gallery.tsx =====
"use client";

import Image from "next/image";
import { useState } from "react";
import { resolveImageUrl } from "../lib/api/imageUrl";
import styles from "./item-image-gallery.module.css";

type ItemImageGalleryProps = {
  images: string[];
  title: string;
};

export function ItemImageGallery({ images, title }: ItemImageGalleryProps) {
  const [selectedIndex, setSelectedIndex] = useState(0);
  const activeIndex = Math.min(selectedIndex, images.length - 1);
  const selectedImage = images[activeIndex];

  if (images.length === 0) {
    return (
      <div className={styles.emptyGallery} aria-label={`ChÆ°a cÃ³ áº£nh cho ${title}`}>
        <svg viewBox="0 0 64 64" aria-hidden="true">
          <rect x="8" y="12" width="48" height="40" rx="5" />
          <circle cx="23" cy="26" r="5" />
          <path d="m10 45 14-13 9 8 6-5 15 14" />
        </svg>
        <span>ChÆ°a cÃ³ hÃ¬nh áº£nh</span>
      </div>
    );
  }

  return (
    <section
      className={styles.gallery}
      aria-label={`ThÆ° viá»‡n áº£nh ${title}`}
      onKeyDown={(event) => {
        if (event.key === "ArrowLeft") {
          event.preventDefault();
          setSelectedIndex((activeIndex - 1 + images.length) % images.length);
        } else if (event.key === "ArrowRight") {
          event.preventDefault();
          setSelectedIndex((activeIndex + 1) % images.length);
        }
      }}
      tabIndex={0}
    >
      <div className={styles.mainImage}>
        <Image
          alt={`${title} - áº£nh ${activeIndex + 1}`}
          className={styles.image}
          src={resolveImageUrl(selectedImage)}
          fill
          priority
          sizes="(max-width: 800px) 100vw, 55vw"
          unoptimized
        />
        {images.length > 1 && (
          <>
            <button
              aria-label="Xem áº£nh trÆ°á»›c"
              className={`${styles.navigationButton} ${styles.previousButton}`}
              onClick={() => setSelectedIndex((activeIndex - 1 + images.length) % images.length)}
              type="button"
            >
              â€¹
            </button>
            <button
              aria-label="Xem áº£nh tiáº¿p theo"
              className={`${styles.navigationButton} ${styles.nextButton}`}
              onClick={() => setSelectedIndex((activeIndex + 1) % images.length)}
              type="button"
            >
              â€º
            </button>
          </>
        )}
        <span className={styles.imageCounter}>
          {activeIndex + 1} / {images.length}
        </span>
      </div>
      {images.length > 1 && (
        <div className={styles.thumbnails} role="group" aria-label="Chá»n áº£nh">
          {images.map((image, index) => (
            <button
              aria-label={`Xem áº£nh ${index + 1}`}
              aria-current={index === activeIndex ? "true" : undefined}
              className={`${styles.thumbnail}${index === activeIndex ? ` ${styles.selected}` : ""}`}
              key={`${image}-${index}`}
              onClick={() => setSelectedIndex(index)}
              type="button"
            >
              <Image
                alt=""
                className={styles.image}
                src={resolveImageUrl(image)}
                fill
                sizes="100px"
                unoptimized
              />
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/ItemForm.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import { ImageUploader, type ExistingImage } from "./ImageUploader";
import { categoryService } from "../lib/api/categoryService";
import { itemService } from "../lib/api/itemService";
import type { Category, Item, ItemInput } from "../lib/api/types";
import styles from "./ItemForm.module.css";

interface ItemFormProps {
  item?: Item;
}

export function ItemForm({ item }: ItemFormProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const [persistedItem, setPersistedItem] = useState<Item | null>(item ?? null);
  const [categories, setCategories] = useState<Category[]>([]);
  const [title, setTitle] = useState(item?.title ?? "");
  const [description, setDescription] = useState(item?.description ?? "");
  const [categoryId, setCategoryId] = useState(
    item?.category ? String(item.category.id) : "",
  );
  const [files, setFiles] = useState<File[]>([]);
  const [removedImageIds, setRemovedImageIds] = useState<number[]>([]);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [isLoadingCategories, setIsLoadingCategories] = useState(true);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();
    async function loadCategories() {
      try {
        setCategories(await categoryService.listAll({ signal: controller.signal }));
      } catch (loadError) {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setError(
            loadError instanceof Error
              ? loadError.message
              : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i danh má»¥c mÃ³n Ä‘á»“.",
          );
        }
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setIsLoadingCategories(false);
      }
    }

    void loadCategories();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, []);

  const currentItem = persistedItem;
  const isEditing = Boolean(currentItem);
  const currentImages: ExistingImage[] = (currentItem?.images ?? [])
    .filter((image) => !removedImageIds.includes(image.id))
    .map((image) => ({ id: image.id, url: image.imageUrl }));

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    setError("");
    setIsSubmitting(true);
    let savedItem = currentItem;

    const input: ItemInput = {
      title: title.trim(),
      description: description.trim(),
      ...(categoryId ? { categoryId: Number(categoryId) } : {}),
    };

    try {
      if (savedItem) {
        savedItem = await itemService.update(savedItem.id, input);
        setPersistedItem(savedItem);
      } else {
        savedItem = await itemService.create(input);
        setPersistedItem(savedItem);
      }

      const pendingImageIds = [...removedImageIds];
      for (const imageId of pendingImageIds) {
        savedItem = await itemService.deleteImage(savedItem.id, imageId);
        setRemovedImageIds((current) => current.filter((id) => id !== imageId));
        setPersistedItem(savedItem);
      }

      if (files.length > 0) {
        savedItem = await itemService.uploadImages(savedItem.id, files);
        setFiles([]);
        setPersistedItem(savedItem);
      }

      router.push(`/items/${savedItem.id}`);
    } catch (submitError) {
      const message =
        submitError instanceof Error
          ? submitError.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ lÆ°u mÃ³n Ä‘á»“. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.";
      setError(
        savedItem
          ? `MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c lÆ°u nhÆ°ng chÆ°a hoÃ n táº¥t viá»‡c cáº­p nháº­t áº£nh: ${message}`
          : message,
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <form className={styles.form} onSubmit={(event) => void handleSubmit(event)}>
      <h1>{isEditing ? "Chá»‰nh sá»­a mÃ³n Ä‘á»“" : "ÄÄƒng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ má»›i"}</h1>
      <p className={styles.intro}>
        ThÃªm thÃ´ng tin vÃ  hÃ¬nh áº£nh Ä‘á»ƒ má»i ngÆ°á»i dá»… dÃ ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ cá»§a báº¡n.
      </p>

      <div className={styles.fields}>
        <div className={styles.field}>
          <label htmlFor="item-title">TÃªn mÃ³n Ä‘á»“</label>
          <input
            autoComplete="off"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            id="item-title"
            maxLength={150}
            onChange={(event) => setTitle(event.target.value)}
            required
            value={title}
          />
        </div>
        <div className={styles.field}>
          <label htmlFor="item-description">MÃ´ táº£</label>
          <textarea
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            id="item-description"
            maxLength={5000}
            onChange={(event) => setDescription(event.target.value)}
            value={description}
          />
        </div>
        <div className={styles.field}>
          <label htmlFor="item-category">Danh má»¥c</label>
          <select
            disabled={isLoadingCategories || isSubmitting}
            id="item-category"
            onChange={(event) => setCategoryId(event.target.value)}
            value={categoryId}
          >
            <option value="">ChÆ°a phÃ¢n loáº¡i</option>
            {categories.map((category) => (
              <option key={category.id} value={category.id}>
                {category.name}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>
      </div>

      <ImageUploader
        disabled={isSubmitting}
        existingImages={currentImages}
        maxFiles={5}
        maxSizeMB={5}
        onChange={setFiles}
        onRemoveExisting={(imageId) =>
          setRemovedImageIds((current) => [...current, imageId])
        }
        value={files}
      />

      {error && <p className={`form-alert ${styles.error}`} role="alert">{error}</p>}

      <div className={styles.actions}>
        <button className={styles.submit} disabled={isSubmitting} type="submit">
          {isSubmitting ? "Äang lÆ°u mÃ³n Ä‘á»“..." : isEditing ? "LÆ°u thay Ä‘á»•i" : "ÄÄƒng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“"}
        </button>
        <Link className={styles.cancel} href="/items">Há»§y</Link>
      </div>
    </form>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/notification-bell.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useNotifications } from "./notifications-provider";
import styles from "./notification-bell.module.css";

function formatNotificationDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "Vá»«a xong";

  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", {
    dateStyle: "short",
    timeStyle: "short",
  }).format(date);
}

function getNotificationHref(link?: string | null): string {
  return link?.startsWith("/") && !link.startsWith("//") ? link : "/notifications";
}

export function NotificationBell() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const containerRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [actionError, setActionError] = useState("");
  const {
    notifications,
    unreadCount,
    loading,
    error,
    authenticated,
    refresh,
    markRead,
    markAllRead,
  } = useNotifications();
  const recentNotifications = notifications.slice(0, 5);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;

    function handlePointerDown(event: PointerEvent) {
      if (!containerRef.current?.contains(event.target as Node)) setOpen(false);
    }

    function handleKeyDown(event: KeyboardEvent) {
      if (event.key === "Escape") setOpen(false);
    }

    document.addEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown);
    document.addEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    return () => {
      document.removeEventListener("pointerdown", handlePointerDown);
      document.removeEventListener("keydown", handleKeyDown);
    };
  }, [open]);

  async function openNotification(id: number, isRead: boolean, href: string) {
    setActionError("");
    try {
      if (!isRead) await markRead(id);
      setOpen(false);
      router.push(href);
    } catch (actionError) {
      setActionError(
        actionError instanceof Error
          ? actionError.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ cáº­p nháº­t tráº¡ng thÃ¡i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o.",
      );
    }
  }

  async function handleMarkAllRead() {
    setActionError("");
    try {
      await markAllRead();
    } catch (actionError) {
      setActionError(
        actionError instanceof Error
          ? actionError.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u thÃ´ng bÃ¡o Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c.",
      );
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className={styles.container} ref={containerRef}>
      <button
        aria-controls="notifications-menu"
        aria-expanded={open}
        aria-label={unreadCount > 0 ? `ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o, ${unreadCount} chÆ°a Ä‘á»c` : "ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o"}
        className={styles.bellButton}
        onClick={() => {
          setOpen((current) => !current);
          setActionError("");
        }}
        type="button"
      >
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true">
          <path d="M18 8a6 6 0 0 0-12 0c0 7-3 7-3 9h18c0-2-3-2-3-9m-8 12a2 2 0 0 0 4 0" />
        </svg>
        {unreadCount > 0 && (
          <span className={styles.badge} aria-hidden="true">
            {unreadCount > 99 ? "99+" : unreadCount}
          </span>
        )}
      </button>

      {open && (
        <section
          className={styles.dropdown}
          id="notifications-menu"
          aria-label="ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o gáº§n Ä‘Ã¢y"
        >
          <header className={styles.dropdownHeader}>
            <div>
              <h2>ThÃ´ng bÃ¡o</h2>
              {authenticated && <span>{unreadCount} chÆ°a Ä‘á»c</span>}
            </div>
            {authenticated && unreadCount > 0 && (
              <button className={styles.textButton} onClick={handleMarkAllRead} type="button">
                ÄÃ¡nh dáº¥u Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘á»c
              </button>
            )}
          </header>

          {!authenticated ? (
            <div className={styles.messageState}>
              <p>ÄÄƒng nháº­p Ä‘á»ƒ xem thÃ´ng bÃ¡o cá»§a báº¡n.</p>
              <Link href="/login" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>ÄÄƒng nháº­p</Link>
            </div>
          ) : error ? (
            <div className={styles.messageState} role="alert">
              <p>{error}</p>
              <button className={styles.textButton} onClick={() => void refresh()} type="button">
                Thá»­ láº¡i
              </button>
            </div>
          ) : loading && recentNotifications.length === 0 ? (
            <div className={styles.skeletonList} aria-label="Äang táº£i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o" aria-busy="true">
              <span /><span /><span />
            </div>
          ) : recentNotifications.length === 0 ? (
            <p className={styles.messageState}>KhÃ´ng cÃ³ thÃ´ng bÃ¡o nÃ o.</p>
          ) : (
            <ul className={styles.list}>
              {recentNotifications.map((notification) => (
                <li key={notification.id}>
                  <button
                    className={`${styles.notification}${notification.read ? "" : ` ${styles.unread}`}`}
                    onClick={() =>
                      void openNotification(
                        notification.id,
                        notification.read,
                        getNotificationHref(notification.link),
                      )
                    }
                    type="button"
                  >
                    <span className={styles.unreadIndicator} aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span className={styles.notificationCopy}>
                      <strong>{notification.title}</strong>
                      <span className={styles.message}>{notification.message}</span>
                      <time dateTime={notification.createdAt}>
                        {formatNotificationDate(notification.createdAt)}
                      </time>
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          )}

          {actionError && <p className={styles.actionError} role="alert">{actionError}</p>}
          <footer className={styles.dropdownFooter}>
            <Link href="/notifications" onClick={() => setOpen(false)}>
              Xem táº¥t cáº£ thÃ´ng bÃ¡o
            </Link>
          </footer>
        </section>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/notifications-provider.tsx =====
"use client";

import {
  createContext,
  useCallback,
  useContext,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
  type ReactNode,
} from "react";
import {
  AUTH_TOKEN_CHANGE_EVENT,
  getAuthToken,
} from "../lib/api/apiClient";
import {
  notificationService,
  type Notification,
} from "../lib/api/notificationService";

const POLLING_INTERVAL = 30_000;
const NOTIFICATION_PAGE_SIZE = 10;

type NotificationsContextValue = {
  notifications: Notification[];
  unreadCount: number;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string;
  authenticated: boolean;
  refresh: () => Promise<void>;
  markRead: (id: number) => Promise<void>;
  markAllRead: () => Promise<void>;
};

const NotificationsContext = createContext<NotificationsContextValue | null>(null);

export function NotificationsProvider({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [notifications, setNotifications] = useState<Notification[]>([]);
  const [unreadCount, setUnreadCount] = useState(0);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [authenticated, setAuthenticated] = useState(false);
  const requestInProgress = useRef(false);
  const hasLoaded = useRef(false);

  const refresh = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!getAuthToken() || requestInProgress.current) return;

    requestInProgress.current = true;
    if (!hasLoaded.current) setLoading(true);
    try {
      const [page, count] = await Promise.all([
        notificationService.list({ page: 0, size: NOTIFICATION_PAGE_SIZE }),
        notificationService.unreadCount(),
      ]);
      setNotifications(page.content);
      setUnreadCount(count);
      setError("");
      hasLoaded.current = true;
    } catch (requestError) {
      setError(
        requestError instanceof Error
          ? requestError.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i thÃ´ng bÃ¡o. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.",
      );
    } finally {
      requestInProgress.current = false;
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let intervalId: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined;
    let active = true;

    function stopPolling() {
      if (intervalId) clearInterval(intervalId);
      intervalId = undefined;
      setAuthenticated(false);
      setNotifications([]);
      setUnreadCount(0);
      setError("");
      setLoading(false);
      hasLoaded.current = false;
    }

    function startPolling() {
      if (!getAuthToken()) {
        stopPolling();
        return;
      }

      setAuthenticated(true);
      void refresh();
      if (!intervalId) {
        intervalId = setInterval(() => {
          void refresh();
        }, POLLING_INTERVAL);
      }
    }

    queueMicrotask(() => {
      if (active) startPolling();
    });
    window.addEventListener(AUTH_TOKEN_CHANGE_EVENT, startPolling);

    return () => {
      active = false;
      if (intervalId) clearInterval(intervalId);
      window.removeEventListener(AUTH_TOKEN_CHANGE_EVENT, startPolling);
    };
  }, [refresh]);

  const markRead = useCallback(
    async (id: number) => {
      const previousNotifications = notifications;
      const wasUnread = previousNotifications.some((notification) => notification.id === id && !notification.read);
      setNotifications((current) =>
        current.map((notification) =>
          notification.id === id ? { ...notification, read: true } : notification,
        ),
      );
      if (wasUnread) setUnreadCount((current) => Math.max(0, current - 1));

      try {
        await notificationService.markRead(id);
      } catch (requestError) {
        setNotifications(previousNotifications);
        if (wasUnread) setUnreadCount((current) => current + 1);
        throw requestError;
      }
    },
    [notifications],
  );

  const markAllRead = useCallback(async () => {
    const previousNotifications = notifications;
    const previousUnreadCount = unreadCount;
    setNotifications((current) =>
      current.map((notification) => ({ ...notification, read: true })),
    );
    setUnreadCount(0);

    try {
      await notificationService.markAllRead();
    } catch (requestError) {
      setNotifications(previousNotifications);
      setUnreadCount(previousUnreadCount);
      throw requestError;
    }
  }, [notifications, unreadCount]);

  const value = useMemo(
    () => ({
      notifications,
      unreadCount,
      loading,
      error,
      authenticated,
      refresh,
      markRead,
      markAllRead,
    }),
    [authenticated, error, loading, markAllRead, markRead, notifications, refresh, unreadCount],
  );

  return (
    <NotificationsContext.Provider value={value}>
      {children}
    </NotificationsContext.Provider>
  );
}

export function useNotifications(): NotificationsContextValue {
  const context = useContext(NotificationsContext);
  if (!context) {
    throw new Error("useNotifications must be used within NotificationsProvider.");
  }
  return context;
}

===== frontend/components/personal-management.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { ConfirmationDialog } from "./confirmation-dialog";
import { useAdminToast } from "./admin-toast";
import { useAuth } from "./auth-provider";
import { NotificationBell } from "./notification-bell";
import { ReviewDialog } from "./review-dialog";
import { itemService } from "../lib/api/itemService";
import { loanService } from "../lib/api/loanService";
import { reviewService } from "../lib/api/reviewService";
import { userService } from "../lib/api/userService";
import type { Item, Loan, PageResponse } from "../lib/api/types";
import styles from "./personal-management.module.css";

const PAGE_SIZE = 10;
const VIETNAM_DATE_FORMATTER = new Intl.DateTimeFormat("en-US", {
  day: "2-digit",
  month: "2-digit",
  timeZone: "Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh",
  year: "numeric",
});

type Tab = "items" | "outgoing" | "incoming";
type LoanAction = "approve" | "reject" | "return";

const tabs: { id: Tab; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "items", label: "Äá»“ cá»§a tÃ´i" },
  { id: "outgoing", label: "YÃªu cáº§u tÃ´i Ä‘Ã£ gá»­i" },
  { id: "incoming", label: "YÃªu cáº§u gá»­i Ä‘áº¿n tÃ´i" },
];

const statusLabels: Record<Loan["status"], string> = {
  PENDING: "Chá» duyá»‡t",
  APPROVED: "ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t",
  REJECTED: "ÄÃ£ tá»« chá»‘i",
  RETURNED: "ÄÃ£ tráº£",
  CANCELLED: "ÄÃ£ há»§y",
  OVERDUE: "QuÃ¡ háº¡n",
};

const actionDetails: Record<LoanAction, { title: string; message: string; confirm: string }> = {
  approve: {
    title: "Duyá»‡t yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n?",
    message: "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Ã¡nh dáº¥u Ä‘ang cho mÆ°á»£n vÃ  ngÆ°á»i gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u sáº½ nháº­n Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÃ´ng bÃ¡o.",
    confirm: "Duyá»‡t yÃªu cáº§u",
  },
  reject: {
    title: "Tá»« chá»‘i yÃªu cáº§u?",
    message: "NgÆ°á»i gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c thÃ´ng bÃ¡o vá» quyáº¿t Ä‘á»‹nh nÃ y.",
    confirm: "Tá»« chá»‘i yÃªu cáº§u",
  },
  return: {
    title: "XÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘Ã£ tráº£ Ä‘á»“?",
    message: "MÃ³n Ä‘á»“ sáº½ Ä‘Æ°á»£c chuyá»ƒn vá» tráº¡ng thÃ¡i cÃ³ thá»ƒ mÆ°á»£n.",
    confirm: "XÃ¡c nháº­n Ä‘Ã£ tráº£",
  },
};

function formatDate(value: string | null): string {
  if (!value) return "ChÆ°a cáº­p nháº­t";
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "ChÆ°a cáº­p nháº­t";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", {
    day: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
  }).format(date);
}

function getOverdueDays(dueDate: string | null): number | null {
  const match = dueDate?.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/);
  if (!match) return null;

  const dueDateUtc = Date.UTC(Number(match[1]), Number(match[2]) - 1, Number(match[3]));
  if (new Date(dueDateUtc).toISOString().slice(0, 10) !== dueDate) return null;

  const todayParts = VIETNAM_DATE_FORMATTER.formatToParts(new Date());
  const todayPart = (type: Intl.DateTimeFormatPartTypes) =>
    Number(todayParts.find((part) => part.type === type)?.value);
  const todayUtc = Date.UTC(todayPart("year"), todayPart("month") - 1, todayPart("day"));

  return Math.max(0, Math.floor((todayUtc - dueDateUtc) / 86_400_000));
}

function ItemStatus({ item }: { item: Item }) {
  const label =
    item.status === "AVAILABLE"
      ? "CÃ³ thá»ƒ mÆ°á»£n"
      : item.status === "LENT_OUT"
        ? "Äang Ä‘Æ°á»£c mÆ°á»£n"
        : "Táº¡m ngÆ°ng";
  const tone =
    item.status === "AVAILABLE"
      ? styles.statusAvailable
      : item.status === "LENT_OUT"
        ? styles.statusPending
        : styles.statusMuted;
  return <span className={`${styles.badge} ${tone}`}>{label}</span>;
}

function LoanStatus({ loan }: { loan: Loan }) {
  const { status } = loan;
  const tone =
    status === "PENDING"
      ? styles.statusPending
      : status === "APPROVED"
        ? styles.statusApproved
        : status === "RETURNED"
          ? styles.statusAvailable
          : status === "OVERDUE"
            ? styles.statusOverdue
            : status === "REJECTED"
              ? styles.statusRejected
              : styles.statusMuted;
  const overdueDays = status === "OVERDUE" ? getOverdueDays(loan.dueDate) : null;
  return (
    <span className={`${styles.badge} ${tone}`}>
      {statusLabels[status]}
      {overdueDays !== null && ` Â· trá»… ${overdueDays} ngÃ y`}
    </span>
  );
}

export function PersonalManagement() {
  const router = useRouter();
  const auth = useAuth();
  const { showToast } = useAdminToast();
  const [tab, setTab] = useState<Tab>("items");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);
  const [itemsPage, setItemsPage] = useState<PageResponse<Item> | null>(null);
  const [loansPage, setLoansPage] = useState<PageResponse<Loan> | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [reload, setReload] = useState(0);
  const [confirmation, setConfirmation] = useState<{ loan: Loan; action: LoanAction } | null>(null);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);
  const [apiUserId, setApiUserId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [reviewedLoans, setReviewedLoans] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  const [reviewCheckError, setReviewCheckError] = useState("");
  const [reviewCheckRetry, setReviewCheckRetry] = useState(0);
  const [reviewingLoan, setReviewingLoan] = useState<Loan | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (auth.status === "unauthenticated") router.replace("/login");
  }, [auth.status, router]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (auth.status !== "authenticated") return;
    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadPage() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        if (tab === "items") {
          setItemsPage(await itemService.listMine(
            { page, size: PAGE_SIZE },
            { signal: controller.signal },
          ));
        } else {
          const result =
            tab === "outgoing"
              ? await loanService.listMine({ page, size: PAGE_SIZE }, { signal: controller.signal })
              : await loanService.listIncoming({ page, size: PAGE_SIZE }, { signal: controller.signal });
          setLoansPage(result);
        }
      } catch (loadError) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u quáº£n lÃ½.");
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadPage();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [auth.status, page, reload, tab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (auth.status !== "authenticated") return;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    setApiUserId(null);
    userService.getMe({ signal: controller.signal })
      .then((profile) => {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) {
          setApiUserId(profile.id);
          setReviewCheckError("");
        }
      })
      .catch((profileError: unknown) => {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setReviewCheckError(
          profileError instanceof Error
            ? profileError.message
            : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh tÃ i khoáº£n Ä‘á»ƒ kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡.",
        );
      });
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [auth.status, reviewCheckRetry]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!apiUserId || tab === "items" || !loansPage) return;
    const controller = new AbortController();
    const returnedLoans = loansPage.content.filter((loan) => loan.status === "RETURNED");
    setReviewCheckError("");

    async function checkReviews() {
      try {
        const entries = await Promise.all(returnedLoans.map(async (loan) => {
          const reviews = await reviewService.listByLoan(
            loan.id,
            { signal: controller.signal },
          );
          return [loan.id, reviews.some((review) => review.reviewer?.id === apiUserId)] as const;
        }));
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setReviewedLoans(Object.fromEntries(entries));
      } catch (reviewError) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setReviewCheckError(
          reviewError instanceof Error ? reviewError.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡ Ä‘Ã£ gá»­i.",
        );
      }
    }

    void checkReviews();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [apiUserId, loansPage, reviewCheckRetry, tab]);

  const changeTab = useCallback((nextTab: Tab) => {
    setTab(nextTab);
    setPage(0);
    setError("");
  }, []);

  async function runAction() {
    if (!confirmation) return;
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      const { loan, action } = confirmation;
      if (action === "approve") await loanService.approve(loan.id);
      else if (action === "reject") await loanService.reject(loan.id);
      else await loanService.markReturned(loan.id);

      showToast("success", action === "approve"
        ? "ÄÃ£ duyá»‡t yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n."
        : action === "reject"
          ? "ÄÃ£ tá»« chá»‘i yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n."
          : "ÄÃ£ xÃ¡c nháº­n ngÆ°á»i mÆ°á»£n tráº£ Ä‘á»“.");
      setConfirmation(null);
      setReload((current) => current + 1);
    } catch (actionError) {
      showToast(
        "error",
        actionError instanceof Error ? actionError.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ cáº­p nháº­t yÃªu cáº§u.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  async function submitReview(rating: number, comment: string) {
    if (!reviewingLoan) return;
    await reviewService.create(reviewingLoan.id, {
      rating,
      ...(comment ? { comment } : {}),
    });
    setReviewedLoans((current) => ({ ...current, [reviewingLoan.id]: true }));
    setReviewingLoan(null);
    showToast("success", "ÄÃ£ gá»­i Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡. Cáº£m Æ¡n báº¡n!");
  }

  const currentPage = tab === "items" ? itemsPage : loansPage;
  const totalPages = currentPage?.totalPages ?? 0;
  const pageCount = Math.max(totalPages, 1);

  return (
    <main className={styles.page}>
      <header className={styles.header}>
        <Link className={styles.brand} href="/" aria-label="UniLend - vá» trang chá»§">
          <span className={styles.brandMark} aria-hidden="true">U</span>
          <span>uni<span className={styles.brandAccent}>lend</span></span>
        </Link>
        <nav className={styles.navigation} aria-label="Äiá»u hÆ°á»›ng chÃ­nh">
          <Link href="/">Trang chá»§</Link>
          <Link href="/items">KhÃ¡m phÃ¡ Ä‘á»“</Link>
          <span aria-current="page">Quáº£n lÃ½ cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n</span>
        </nav>
        <div className={styles.headerActions}>
          <NotificationBell />
          <Link className={styles.profileLink} href="/profile">Há»“ sÆ¡</Link>
        </div>
      </header>

      <section className={styles.content} aria-labelledby="management-title">
        <div className={styles.heading}>
          <div>
            <span className={styles.eyebrow}>KhÃ´ng gian cá»§a báº¡n</span>
            <h1 id="management-title">Quáº£n lÃ½ cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n</h1>
            <p>Theo dÃµi mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ báº¡n chia sáº» vÃ  nhá»¯ng yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n trong cá»™ng Ä‘á»“ng.</p>
          </div>
          <Link className={styles.primaryButton} href="/items/new">ï¼‹ ÄÄƒng Ä‘á»“ má»›i</Link>
        </div>

        <div className={styles.tabs} role="tablist" aria-label="Ná»™i dung quáº£n lÃ½">
          {tabs.map((entry) => (
            <button
              aria-selected={tab === entry.id}
              aria-controls="management-panel"
              className={tab === entry.id ? styles.activeTab : ""}
              id={`tab-${entry.id}`}
              key={entry.id}
              onClick={() => changeTab(entry.id)}
              role="tab"
              type="button"
            >
              {entry.label}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>

        {auth.status === "error" ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel} role="alert">{auth.error}</div>
        ) : auth.status !== "authenticated" ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel} role="status">Äang xÃ¡c thá»±c phiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p...</div>
        ) : error ? (
          <div className={styles.statePanel} role="alert">
            <h2>KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u</h2>
            <p>{error}</p>
            <button className={styles.secondaryButton} onClick={() => setReload((value) => value + 1)} type="button">
              Thá»­ láº¡i
            </button>
          </div>
        ) : loading ? (
          <div className={styles.loadingList} aria-label="Äang táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u" aria-busy="true">
            <span className={styles.visuallyHidden} role="status">Äang táº£i dá»¯ liá»‡u</span>
            {Array.from({ length: 4 }, (_, index) => (
              <div className={styles.loadingSkeleton} key={index} />
            ))}
          </div>
        ) : tab === "items" ? (
          <section
            aria-labelledby={`tab-${tab}`}
            className={styles.listPanel}
            id="management-panel"
            role="tabpanel"
          >
            {(itemsPage?.content.length ?? 0) > 0 ? (
              <div className={styles.itemList}>
                {itemsPage?.content.map((item) => (
                  <article className={styles.itemRow} key={item.id}>
                    <div className={styles.itemMark} aria-hidden="true">â†—</div>
                    <div className={styles.itemInfo}>
                      <span className={styles.itemCategory}>{item.category?.name ?? "ChÆ°a phÃ¢n loáº¡i"}</span>
                      <h2>{item.title}</h2>
                      <p>ÄÄƒng ngÃ y {formatDate(item.createdAt)}</p>
                    </div>
                    <ItemStatus item={item} />
                    <Link className={styles.editLink} href={`/items/${item.id}/edit`}>Sá»­a Ä‘á»“</Link>
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className={styles.emptyState}>
                <span className={styles.emptyIcon} aria-hidden="true">ï¼‹</span>
                <h2>Báº¡n chÆ°a Ä‘Äƒng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ nÃ o</h2>
                <p>Chia sáº» mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ã­t dÃ¹ng Ä‘á»ƒ giÃºp báº¡n bÃ¨ quanh trÆ°á»ng cÃ³ thÃªm lá»±a chá»n.</p>
                <Link className={styles.primaryButton} href="/items/new">ÄÄƒng mÃ³n Ä‘á»“ Ä‘áº§u tiÃªn</Link>
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        ) : (
          <section
            aria-labelledby={`tab-${tab}`}
            className={styles.listPanel}
            id="management-panel"
            role="tabpanel"
          >
            {(loansPage?.content.length ?? 0) > 0 ? (
              <div className={styles.loanList}>
                {loansPage?.content.map((loan) => (
                  <article className={styles.loanCard} key={loan.id}>
                    <div className={styles.loanTopline}>
                      <LoanStatus loan={loan} />
                      <span className={styles.requestDate}>Gá»­i ngÃ y {formatDate(loan.requestedAt)}</span>
                    </div>
                    <h2>{loan.item.title}</h2>
                    <p className={styles.loanPerson}>
                      {tab === "incoming" ? "NgÆ°á»i gá»­i" : "Chá»§ mÃ³n Ä‘á»“"}:{" "}
                      <strong>{tab === "incoming" ? loan.borrower.fullName : loan.owner.fullName}</strong>
                    </p>
                    <div className={styles.loanDates}>
                      <span>MÆ°á»£n tá»« <strong>{formatDate(loan.borrowFrom)}</strong></span>
                      <span>Háº¡n tráº£ <strong>{formatDate(loan.dueDate)}</strong></span>
                    </div>
                    {loan.note && <p className={styles.loanNote}>â€œ{loan.note}â€</p>}
                    {tab === "incoming" && (
                      <div className={styles.loanActions}>
                        {loan.status === "PENDING" && (
                          <>
                            <button
                              className={styles.approveButton}
                              onClick={() => setConfirmation({ loan, action: "approve" })}
                              type="button"
                            >
                              Duyá»‡t
                            </button>
                            <button
                              className={styles.rejectButton}
                              onClick={() => setConfirmation({ loan, action: "reject" })}
                              type="button"
                            >
                              Tá»« chá»‘i
                            </button>
                          </>
                        )}
                        {(loan.status === "APPROVED" || loan.status === "OVERDUE") && (
                          <button
                            className={styles.approveButton}
                            onClick={() => setConfirmation({ loan, action: "return" })}
                            type="button"
                          >
                            ÄÃ£ tráº£
                          </button>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {loan.status === "RETURNED" && reviewedLoans[loan.id] !== true && (
                      <div className={styles.loanActions}>
                        <button
                          className={styles.reviewButton}
                          onClick={() => setReviewingLoan(loan)}
                          type="button"
                        >
                          â˜… ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    )}
                    {loan.status === "RETURNED" && reviewedLoans[loan.id] === true && (
                      <p className={styles.reviewedLabel}>âœ“ Báº¡n Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡ giao dá»‹ch nÃ y</p>
                    )}
                  </article>
                ))}
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className={styles.emptyState}>
                <span className={styles.emptyIcon} aria-hidden="true">âŒ</span>
                <h2>{tab === "incoming" ? "ChÆ°a cÃ³ yÃªu cáº§u nÃ o gá»­i Ä‘áº¿n" : "Báº¡n chÆ°a gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n"}</h2>
                <p>
                  {tab === "incoming"
                    ? "Khi cÃ³ báº¡n gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n Ä‘á»“ cá»§a báº¡n, thÃ´ng tin sáº½ xuáº¥t hiá»‡n táº¡i Ä‘Ã¢y."
                    : "KhÃ¡m phÃ¡ danh sÃ¡ch Ä‘á»“ vÃ  gá»­i yÃªu cáº§u mÆ°á»£n mÃ³n báº¡n cáº§n nhÃ©."}
                </p>
                {tab === "outgoing" && <Link className={styles.primaryButton} href="/items">KhÃ¡m phÃ¡ Ä‘á»“</Link>}
              </div>
            )}
          </section>
        )}
        {reviewCheckError && tab !== "items" && (
          <p className={styles.reviewCheckError} role="alert">
            KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ kiá»ƒm tra Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡: {reviewCheckError}{" "}
            <button onClick={() => setReviewCheckRetry((value) => value + 1)} type="button">
              Thá»­ láº¡i
            </button>
          </p>
        )}

        {!loading && !error && totalPages > 1 && (
          <nav className={styles.pagination} aria-label="PhÃ¢n trang">
            <button disabled={page === 0} onClick={() => setPage((value) => value - 1)} type="button">
              â† TrÆ°á»›c
            </button>
            <span>Trang {page + 1} / {pageCount}</span>
            <button disabled={page + 1 >= pageCount} onClick={() => setPage((value) => value + 1)} type="button">
              Sau â†’
            </button>
          </nav>
        )}
      </section>

      {confirmation && (
        <ConfirmationDialog
          busy={submitting}
          cancelLabel="Äá»ƒ sau"
          confirmLabel={actionDetails[confirmation.action].confirm}
          onCancel={() => setConfirmation(null)}
          onConfirm={() => void runAction()}
          pendingLabel="Äang xá»­ lÃ½..."
          title={actionDetails[confirmation.action].title}
        >
          {actionDetails[confirmation.action].message}
        </ConfirmationDialog>
      )}
      {reviewingLoan && (
        <ReviewDialog
          itemTitle={reviewingLoan.item.title}
          revieweeName={tab === "incoming" ? reviewingLoan.borrower.fullName : reviewingLoan.owner.fullName}
          onClose={() => setReviewingLoan(null)}
          onSubmit={submitReview}
        />
      )}
    </main>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/review-dialog.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useState, type FormEvent } from "react";
import styles from "./review-dialog.module.css";

export function ReviewDialog({
  itemTitle,
  revieweeName,
  onClose,
  onSubmit,
}: {
  itemTitle: string;
  revieweeName: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onSubmit: (rating: number, comment: string) => Promise<void>;
}) {
  const [rating, setRating] = useState(0);
  const [hoveredRating, setHoveredRating] = useState(0);
  const [comment, setComment] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(event: FormEvent<HTMLFormElement>) {
    event.preventDefault();
    if (rating < 1 || rating > 5) {
      setError("Vui lÃ²ng chá»n tá»« 1 Ä‘áº¿n 5 sao.");
      return;
    }
    if (comment.trim().length > 1000) {
      setError("Nháº­n xÃ©t tá»‘i Ä‘a 1000 kÃ½ tá»±.");
      return;
    }

    setError("");
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      await onSubmit(rating, comment.trim());
    } catch (submitError) {
      setError(
        submitError instanceof Error
          ? submitError.message
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ gá»­i Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.",
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  const visibleRating = hoveredRating || rating;

  return (
    <div className={styles.backdrop}>
      <section
        aria-labelledby="review-dialog-title"
        aria-modal="true"
        className={styles.dialog}
        onKeyDown={(event) => {
          if (event.key === "Escape" && !submitting) onClose();
        }}
        role="dialog"
        tabIndex={-1}
      >
        <button
          aria-label="ÄÃ³ng cá»­a sá»• Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡"
          className={styles.closeButton}
          disabled={submitting}
          onClick={onClose}
          type="button"
        >
          Ã—
        </button>
        <span className={styles.eyebrow}>Giao dá»‹ch Ä‘Ã£ hoÃ n táº¥t</span>
        <h2 id="review-dialog-title">ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡ {revieweeName}</h2>
        <p className={styles.itemTitle}>{itemTitle}</p>

        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <fieldset className={styles.ratingField}>
            <legend>Tráº£i nghiá»‡m cá»§a báº¡n tháº¿ nÃ o?</legend>
            <div className={styles.stars} onMouseLeave={() => setHoveredRating(0)}>
              {[1, 2, 3, 4, 5].map((value) => (
                <button
                  aria-label={`${value} sao`}
                  aria-pressed={rating === value}
                  className={value <= visibleRating ? styles.selectedStar : styles.star}
                  key={value}
                  onClick={() => setRating(value)}
                  onMouseEnter={() => setHoveredRating(value)}
                  type="button"
                >
                  â˜…
                </button>
              ))}
            </div>
            <span className={styles.ratingHint} aria-live="polite">
              {rating ? `${rating} trÃªn 5 sao` : "Chá»n sá»‘ sao"}
            </span>
          </fieldset>

          <label className={styles.commentLabel} htmlFor="review-comment">
            Nháº­n xÃ©t <span>(khÃ´ng báº¯t buá»™c)</span>
          </label>
          <textarea
            id="review-comment"
            maxLength={1000}
            onChange={(event) => setComment(event.target.value)}
            placeholder="Chia sáº» thÃªm vá» tráº£i nghiá»‡m cá»§a báº¡n..."
            rows={4}
            value={comment}
          />
          <span className={styles.characterCount}>{comment.length}/1000</span>

          {error && <p className={styles.error} role="alert">{error}</p>}

          <div className={styles.actions}>
            <button
              className={styles.cancelButton}
              disabled={submitting}
              onClick={onClose}
              type="button"
            >
              Äá»ƒ sau
            </button>
            <button className={styles.submitButton} disabled={submitting} type="submit">
              {submitting ? "Äang gá»­i..." : "Gá»­i Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡"}
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </section>
    </div>
  );
}

===== frontend/components/role-guard.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from "react";
import { useRouter } from "next/navigation";
import type { UserRole } from "../lib/mock-auth";
import { useAuth } from "./auth-provider";

type RoleGuardProps = {
  children: ReactNode;
  allowedRoles: UserRole[];
};

export function RoleGuard({ children, allowedRoles }: RoleGuardProps) {
  const router = useRouter();
  const auth = useAuth();

  const authorized =
    auth.status === "authenticated" &&
    allowedRoles.some((role) => auth.user.roles.includes(role));

  useEffect(() => {
    if (auth.status === "unauthenticated") router.replace("/login");
    else if (auth.status === "authenticated" && !authorized) router.replace("/forbidden");
  }, [auth.status, authorized, router]);

  if (auth.status === "loading" || (auth.status === "authenticated" && !authorized)) {
    return (
      <main className="profile-loading" role="status">
        {auth.status === "loading" ? "Äang kiá»ƒm tra quyá»n truy cáº­p..." : "Äang chuyá»ƒn hÆ°á»›ng..."}
      </main>
    );
  }

  if (auth.status === "unauthenticated") {
    return <main className="profile-loading" role="status">Äang chuyá»ƒn Ä‘áº¿n Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p...</main>;
  }

  if (auth.status === "error") {
    return (
      <main className="profile-loading" role="alert">
        KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ xÃ¡c thá»±c: {auth.error}
      </main>
    );
  }

  return <>{children}</>;
}

===== frontend/components/role-navigation.tsx =====
"use client";

import Link from "next/link";
import { useAuth } from "./auth-provider";

export function RoleNavigation({
  className,
  registerClassName,
}: {
  className?: string;
  registerClassName?: string;
}) {
  const auth = useAuth();
  if (auth.status === "loading" || auth.status === "error") return null;

  if (auth.status === "unauthenticated") {
    return (
      <>
        <Link className={className} href="/login">ÄÄƒng nháº­p</Link>
        <Link className={registerClassName} href="/register">ÄÄƒng kÃ½</Link>
      </>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <Link className={className} href="/profile">Há»“ sÆ¡</Link>
      <Link className={className} href="/my">Quáº£n lÃ½ cÃ¡ nhÃ¢n</Link>
      {auth.hasRole("ADMIN") && (
        <>
          <Link className={className} href="/admin">Quáº£n trá»‹</Link>
          <Link className={className} href="/admin/items">Quáº£n lÃ½ Ä‘á»“</Link>
        </>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

.page {

  color: var(--green);

  font-weight: 800;
  line-height: 1;

  margin: 0;
  font-size: clamp(21px, 5vw, 25px);

===== frontend/components/user-review-section.tsx =====
"use client";

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { reviewService } from "../lib/api/reviewService";
import { userService } from "../lib/api/userService";
import type { PageResponse, Review, UserRatingResponse } from "../lib/api/types";
import styles from "./user-review-section.module.css";

const REVIEW_PAGE_SIZE = 10;

function formatDate(value: string): string {
  const date = new Date(value);
  if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) return "NgÃ y khÃ´ng xÃ¡c Ä‘á»‹nh";
  return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("vi-VN", {
    day: "numeric",
    month: "short",
    year: "numeric",
  }).format(date);
}

export function UserReviewSection({
  userId,
  title = "ÄÃ¡nh giÃ¡",
}: {
  userId?: number;
  title?: string;
}) {
  const [ratingSummary, setRatingSummary] = useState<UserRatingResponse | null>(null);
  const [reviewPage, setReviewPage] = useState<PageResponse<Review> | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [page, setPage] = useState(0);

  useEffect(() => {
    const controller = new AbortController();

    async function loadReviews() {
      setLoading(true);
      setError("");
      try {
        let rating: UserRatingResponse;
        let resolvedUserId = userId;
        if (resolvedUserId === undefined) {
          const user = await userService.getMe({ signal: controller.signal });
          resolvedUserId = user.id;
          rating = user;
        } else {
          rating = await userService.getRating(resolvedUserId, { signal: controller.signal });
        }
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setRatingSummary(rating);
        const result = await reviewService.listByUser(
          resolvedUserId,
          { page, size: REVIEW_PAGE_SIZE },
          { signal: controller.signal },
        );
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setReviewPage(result);
      } catch (loadError) {
        if (controller.signal.aborted) return;
        setError(loadError instanceof Error ? loadError.message : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ táº£i Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡.");
      } finally {
        if (!controller.signal.aborted) setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    void loadReviews();
    return () => controller.abort();
  }, [page, userId]);

  return (
    <section className={styles.section} aria-labelledby="user-reviews-title">
      <div className={styles.heading}>
        <div>
          <span className={styles.eyebrow}>Cá»™ng Ä‘á»“ng nÃ³i gÃ¬</span>
          <h2 id="user-reviews-title">{title}</h2>
        </div>
        {ratingSummary && (
          <div className={styles.summary} aria-label={`Äiá»ƒm trung bÃ¬nh ${ratingSummary.averageRating} trÃªn 5 tá»« ${ratingSummary.reviewCount} Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡`}>
            <span className={styles.summaryStars} aria-hidden="true">â˜…</span>
            <strong>{ratingSummary.averageRating.toFixed(1)}</strong>
            <span>({ratingSummary.reviewCount} Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡)</span>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>

      {error ? (
        <p className={styles.state} role="alert">{error}</p>
      ) : loading ? (
        <p className={styles.state} role="status">Äang táº£i Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡...</p>
      ) : (reviewPage?.content.length ?? 0) === 0 ? (
        <p className={styles.state}>ChÆ°a cÃ³ Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡ nÃ o.</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className={styles.reviewList}>
            {reviewPage?.content.map((review) => (
              <article className={styles.review} key={review.id}>
                <div className={styles.reviewTopline}>
                  <div className={styles.reviewer}>
                    <span className={styles.avatar} aria-hidden="true">
                      {review.reviewer?.fullName.trim().charAt(0).toLocaleUpperCase("vi") || "U"}
                    </span>
                    <div>
                      <strong>{review.reviewer?.fullName ?? "ThÃ nh viÃªn UniLend"}</strong>
                      {review.itemTitle && <span>{review.itemTitle}</span>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <time dateTime={review.createdAt}>{formatDate(review.createdAt)}</time>
                </div>
                <div className={styles.rating} aria-label={`${review.rating} trÃªn 5 sao`}>
                  <span aria-hidden="true">{"â˜…".repeat(review.rating)}{"â˜†".repeat(5 - review.rating)}</span>
                  <strong>{review.rating}.0</strong>
                </div>
                {review.comment && <p className={styles.comment}>{review.comment}</p>}
              </article>
            ))}
          </div>
          {(reviewPage?.totalPages ?? 0) > 1 && (
            <nav className={styles.pagination} aria-label="PhÃ¢n trang Ä‘Ã¡nh giÃ¡">
              <button disabled={page === 0} onClick={() => setPage((value) => value - 1)} type="button">
                â† TrÆ°á»›c
              </button>
              <span>Trang {page + 1} / {reviewPage?.totalPages}</span>
              <button
                disabled={page + 1 >= (reviewPage?.totalPages ?? 0)}
                onClick={() => setPage((value) => value + 1)}
                type="button"
              >
                Sau â†’
              </button>
            </nav>
          )}
        </>
      )}
    </section>
  );
}

===== frontend/lib/api/apiClient.ts =====
import axios, {
  AxiosError,
  type InternalAxiosRequestConfig,
} from "axios";
import type { ApiResponse } from "./types";

export const AUTH_TOKEN_KEY = "unilend.accessToken";
export const API_ERROR_EVENT = "unilend:api-error";
export const AUTH_TOKEN_CHANGE_EVENT = "unilend:auth-token-change";

const apiClient = axios.create({
  baseURL: (process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "http://localhost:8080").replace(/\/$/, ""),
  timeout: 10000,
  withCredentials: true,
  headers: {
    "Content-Type": "application/json",
  },
});

export class ApiClientError extends Error {
  readonly statusCode: number | undefined;

  constructor(message: string, statusCode?: number) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "ApiClientError";
    this.statusCode = statusCode;
  }
}

function readCookie(name: string): string | null {
  if (typeof document === "undefined") return null;

  const cookie = document.cookie
    .split("; ")
    .find((entry) => entry.startsWith(`${encodeURIComponent(name)}=`));

  if (!cookie) return null;

  try {
    return decodeURIComponent(cookie.slice(cookie.indexOf("=") + 1)) || null;
  } catch {
    return cookie.slice(cookie.indexOf("=") + 1) || null;
  }
}

export function getAuthToken(): string | null {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;

  return (
    window.localStorage.getItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY) ??
    window.localStorage.getItem("accessToken") ??
    window.localStorage.getItem("token") ??
    readCookie(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY) ??
    readCookie("accessToken") ??
    readCookie("token")
  );
}

export function setAuthToken(token: string): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") {
    throw new Error("Access tokens can only be stored in a browser.");
  }

  window.localStorage.setItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, token);
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(AUTH_TOKEN_CHANGE_EVENT));
}

export function clearAuthToken(): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

  window.localStorage.removeItem(AUTH_TOKEN_KEY);
  window.localStorage.removeItem("accessToken");
  window.localStorage.removeItem("token");

  for (const name of [AUTH_TOKEN_KEY, "accessToken", "token"]) {
    document.cookie = `${encodeURIComponent(name)}=; Max-Age=0; path=/; SameSite=Lax`;
  }
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(AUTH_TOKEN_CHANGE_EVENT));
}

function isRecord(value: unknown): value is Record<string, unknown> {
  return typeof value === "object" && value !== null;
}

function responseMessage(data: unknown): string | undefined {
  if (!isRecord(data)) return undefined;

  if (typeof data.message === "string" && data.message.trim()) {
    return data.message;
  }

  if (Array.isArray(data.errors)) {
    const firstError = data.errors.find(
      (error): error is Record<string, unknown> =>
        isRecord(error) && typeof error.message === "string",
    );
    if (firstError && typeof firstError.message === "string") return firstError.message;
  }

  return undefined;
}

function friendlyMessage(statusCode?: number, serverMessage?: string): string {
  if (serverMessage && serverMessage !== "Success") return serverMessage;

  switch (statusCode) {
    case 400:
      return "ThÃ´ng tin gá»­i lÃªn chÆ°a há»£p lá»‡. Vui lÃ²ng kiá»ƒm tra láº¡i.";
    case 401:
      return "PhiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n. Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p láº¡i.";
    case 403:
      return "Báº¡n khÃ´ng cÃ³ quyá»n thá»±c hiá»‡n thao tÃ¡c nÃ y.";
    case 404:
      return "KhÃ´ng tÃ¬m tháº¥y dá»¯ liá»‡u báº¡n yÃªu cáº§u.";
    case 500:
      return "MÃ¡y chá»§ Ä‘ang gáº·p sá»± cá»‘. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i sau.";
    default:
      return "ÄÃ£ xáº£y ra lá»—i. Vui lÃ²ng thá»­ láº¡i.";
  }
}

function handleAuthorizationFailure(statusCode: number | undefined, message: string): void {
  if (typeof window === "undefined") return;

  if (statusCode === 401) {
    clearAuthToken();
    if (window.location.pathname !== "/login") {
      window.location.replace("/login");
    }
  } else if (statusCode === 403) {
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent(API_ERROR_EVENT, { detail: { message } }));
  }
}

apiClient.interceptors.request.use((config: InternalAxiosRequestConfig) => {
  const token = getAuthToken();
  if (token) config.headers.set("Authorization", `Bearer ${token}`);
  return config;
});

apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
  (response) => {
    const data: unknown = response.data;

    if (isRecord(data) && typeof data.success === "boolean") {
      const normalizedResponse: ApiResponse<unknown> = {
        success: data.success,
        message: typeof data.message === "string" ? data.message : "",
        data: data.data,
        statusCode:
          typeof data.statusCode === "number" ? data.statusCode : response.status,
      };

      if (!normalizedResponse.success) {
        const message = friendlyMessage(
          normalizedResponse.statusCode,
          normalizedResponse.message,
        );
        handleAuthorizationFailure(normalizedResponse.statusCode, message);
        throw new ApiClientError(message, normalizedResponse.statusCode);
      }

      response.data = normalizedResponse;
    }

    return response;
  },
  (error: AxiosError<unknown>) => {
    const statusCode = error.response?.status;
    const serverMessage = responseMessage(error.response?.data);

    const message =
      statusCode === undefined
        ? axios.isCancel(error)
          ? "YÃªu cáº§u Ä‘Ã£ bá»‹ há»§y."
          : "KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ káº¿t ná»‘i Ä‘áº¿n mÃ¡y chá»§. Vui lÃ²ng kiá»ƒm tra káº¿t ná»‘i máº¡ng."
        : friendlyMessage(statusCode, serverMessage);

    handleAuthorizationFailure(statusCode, message);

    return Promise.reject(new ApiClientError(message, statusCode));
  },
);

export default apiClient;

===== frontend/lib/api/categoryService.ts =====
import type { AxiosRequestConfig } from "axios";
import apiClient from "./apiClient";
import type { ApiResponse, Category, PageResponse } from "./types";

export interface CategoryInput {
  name: string;
  description?: string;
}

export interface CategoryListParams {
  page?: number;
  size?: number;
}

export const categoryService = {
  async list(
    params: CategoryListParams = {},
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<PageResponse<Category>> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<PageResponse<Category>>>(
      "/api/categories",
      { ...config, params },
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async listAll(
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<Category[]> {
    const firstPage = await this.list({ page: 0, size: 50 }, config);
    if (firstPage.totalPages <= 1) return firstPage.content;

    const remainingPages = await Promise.all(
      Array.from({ length: firstPage.totalPages - 1 }, (_, index) =>
        this.list({ page: index + 1, size: 50 }, config),
      ),
    );
    return firstPage.content.concat(
      ...remainingPages.map((page) => page.content),
    );
  },

  async create(input: CategoryInput): Promise<Category> {
    const response = await apiClient.post<ApiResponse<Category>>("/api/categories", input);
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async update(id: number, input: CategoryInput): Promise<Category> {
    const response = await apiClient.put<ApiResponse<Category>>(
      `/api/categories/${id}`,
      input,
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async delete(id: number): Promise<void> {
    await apiClient.delete<ApiResponse<void>>(`/api/categories/${id}`);
  },
};

===== frontend/lib/api/imageUrl.ts =====
export function resolveImageUrl(imageUrl: string): string {
  const apiBaseUrl = (
    process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_API_BASE_URL ?? "http://localhost:8080"
  ).replace(/\/$/, "");

  return new URL(imageUrl, `${apiBaseUrl}/`).toString();
}

===== frontend/lib/api/itemService.ts =====
import type { AxiosRequestConfig } from "axios";
import apiClient from "./apiClient";
import type { ApiResponse, Item, ItemInput, PageResponse } from "./types";

export interface ItemSearchParams {
  keyword?: string;
  categoryId?: number;
  status?: Item["status"];
  page?: number;
  size?: number;
}

export const itemService = {
  async list(
    params: ItemSearchParams = {},
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<PageResponse<Item>> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<PageResponse<Item>>>("/items", {
      ...config,
      params,
    });
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async listMine(
    params: { page?: number; size?: number } = {},
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<PageResponse<Item>> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<PageResponse<Item>>>("/items/me", {
      ...config,
      params,
    });
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async getById(
    id: number,
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<Item> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<Item>>(`/items/${id}`, config);
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async create(input: ItemInput): Promise<Item> {
    const response = await apiClient.post<ApiResponse<Item>>("/items", input);
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async update(id: number, input: ItemInput): Promise<Item> {
    const response = await apiClient.put<ApiResponse<Item>>(`/items/${id}`, input);
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async uploadImages(id: number, files: File[]): Promise<Item> {
    const formData = new FormData();
    for (const file of files) {
      formData.append("files", file);
    }

    const response = await apiClient.post<ApiResponse<Item>>(
      `/items/${id}/images`,
      formData,
      { headers: { "Content-Type": null } },
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async deleteImage(id: number, imageId: number): Promise<Item> {
    const response = await apiClient.delete<ApiResponse<Item>>(
      `/items/${id}/images/${imageId}`,
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async delete(id: number): Promise<void> {
    await apiClient.delete<ApiResponse<void>>(`/items/${id}`);
  },
};
