import type { AxiosRequestConfig } from "axios";
import apiClient from "./apiClient";
import type {
  ApiResponse,
  CreateLoanInput,
  Loan,
  PageResponse,
} from "./types";

export const loanService = {
  async createRequest(input: CreateLoanInput): Promise<Loan> {
    const response = await apiClient.post<ApiResponse<Loan>>("/loan-requests", input);
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async listMine(
    params: { page?: number; size?: number } = {},
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<PageResponse<Loan>> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<PageResponse<Loan>>>("/loan-requests/mine", {
      ...config,
      params,
    });
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async listIncoming(
    params: { page?: number; size?: number } = {},
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<PageResponse<Loan>> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<PageResponse<Loan>>>(
      "/loan-requests/incoming",
      { ...config, params },
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async approve(id: number): Promise<Loan> {
    const response = await apiClient.patch<ApiResponse<Loan>>(`/loan-requests/${id}/approve`);
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async reject(id: number): Promise<Loan> {
    const response = await apiClient.patch<ApiResponse<Loan>>(`/loan-requests/${id}/reject`);
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async markReturned(id: number): Promise<Loan> {
    const response = await apiClient.patch<ApiResponse<Loan>>(`/loan-requests/${id}/return`);
    return response.data.data;
  },
};

===== frontend/lib/api/notificationService.ts =====
import apiClient from "./apiClient";
import type { ApiResponse, PageResponse } from "./types";

export interface Notification {
  id: number;
  title: string;
  message: string;
  createdAt: string;
  read: boolean;
  link?: string | null;
}

export interface NotificationListParams {
  page?: number;
  size?: number;
}

export const notificationService = {
  async list(params: NotificationListParams = {}): Promise<PageResponse<Notification>> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<PageResponse<Notification>>>(
      "/notifications",
      { params },
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async markRead(id: number): Promise<void> {
    await apiClient.patch<ApiResponse<Notification>>(`/notifications/${id}/read`);
  },

  async markAllRead(): Promise<void> {
    await apiClient.patch<ApiResponse<void>>("/notifications/read-all");
  },

  async unreadCount(): Promise<number> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<
      ApiResponse<number | { unreadCount: number }>
    >("/notifications/unread-count");
    const data = response.data.data;
    return typeof data === "number" ? data : data.unreadCount;
  },
};

===== frontend/lib/api/reviewService.ts =====
import type { AxiosRequestConfig } from "axios";
import apiClient from "./apiClient";
import type { ApiResponse, PageResponse, Review } from "./types";

export const reviewService = {
  async create(
    loanId: number,
    input: { rating: number; comment?: string },
  ): Promise<Review> {
    const response = await apiClient.post<ApiResponse<Review>>(
      `/loan-requests/${loanId}/reviews`,
      input,
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async listByLoan(
    loanId: number,
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<Review[]> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<Review[]>>(
      `/loan-requests/${loanId}/reviews`,
      config,
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },

  async listByUser(
    userId: number,
    params: { page?: number; size?: number } = {},
    config?: Pick<AxiosRequestConfig, "signal">,
  ): Promise<PageResponse<Review>> {
    const response = await apiClient.get<ApiResponse<PageResponse<Review>>>(
      `/users/${userId}/reviews`,
      { ...config, params },
    );
    return response.data.data;
  },
};

export interface ApiResponse<T> {

export interface AdminStats {

  totalItems: number;

    categoryId: number;

    loanCount: number;
  }[];

  createdAt: string;

  description: string | null;

  images?: ItemImage[];

  };

  createdAt: string;
}

export function getItemImageUrls(item: Pick<Item, "imageUrls" | "images">): string[] {
  if (item.images?.length) {
    return item.images.map((image) => image.imageUrl);
  }
  return item.imageUrls ?? [];
}

===== frontend/lib/mock-auth.ts =====
export type UserRole = "USER" | "ADMIN";

export type UserProfile = {
  id: string;
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  createdAt: string;
  roles: UserRole[];
};

type StoredUser = Omit<UserProfile, "roles"> & { roles?: UserRole[]; password: string };

const USERS_KEY = "unilend.mock.users";
const SESSION_KEY = "unilend.mock.session";
const AUTH_CHANGE_EVENT = "unilend:auth-change";

function isStoredUser(value: unknown): value is StoredUser {
  if (typeof value !== "object" || value === null) return false;

  return (
    "id" in value &&
    typeof value.id === "string" &&
    "fullName" in value &&
    typeof value.fullName === "string" &&
    "email" in value &&
    typeof value.email === "string" &&
    "password" in value &&
    typeof value.password === "string" &&
    "createdAt" in value &&
    typeof value.createdAt === "string" &&
    !Number.isNaN(Date.parse(value.createdAt)) &&
    (!("roles" in value) ||
      (Array.isArray(value.roles) &&
        value.roles.every((role) => role === "USER" || role === "ADMIN")))
  );
}

export class MockAuthError extends Error {
  constructor(message: string) {
    super(message);
    this.name = "MockAuthError";
  }
}

function readUsers(): StoredUser[] {
  const value = localStorage.getItem(USERS_KEY);
  if (!value) return [];

  try {
    const users: unknown = JSON.parse(value);
    if (!Array.isArray(users) || !users.every(isStoredUser)) throw new Error("Invalid user list");
    return users;
  } catch {
    throw new MockAuthError("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»c dá»¯ liá»‡u tÃ i khoáº£n trÃªn trÃ¬nh duyá»‡t. Vui lÃ²ng xÃ³a dá»¯ liá»‡u UniLend vÃ  thá»­ láº¡i.");
  }
}

export function subscribeToAuth(callback: () => void): () => void {
  window.addEventListener("storage", callback);
  window.addEventListener(AUTH_CHANGE_EVENT, callback);

  return () => {
    window.removeEventListener("storage", callback);
    window.removeEventListener(AUTH_CHANGE_EVENT, callback);
  };
}

export function getSessionSnapshot(): string | null {
  return localStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
}

export function registerUser(input: {
  fullName: string;
  email: string;
  password: string;
}): void {
  const users = readUsers();
  const email = input.email.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("vi");

  if (users.some((user) => user.email === email)) {
    throw new MockAuthError("Email nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c Ä‘Äƒng kÃ½. HÃ£y Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p hoáº·c dÃ¹ng email khÃ¡c.");
  }

  const user: StoredUser = {
    id: crypto.randomUUID(),
    fullName: input.fullName.trim(),
    email,
    password: input.password,
    createdAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    roles: ["USER"],
  };

  localStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify([...users, user]));
}

export function loginUser(emailInput: string, password: string): UserProfile {
  const email = emailInput.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("vi");
  const user = readUsers().find((candidate) => candidate.email === email && candidate.password === password);

  if (!user) {
    throw new MockAuthError("Email hoáº·c máº­t kháº©u chÆ°a chÃ­nh xÃ¡c.");
  }

  const profile: UserProfile = {
    id: user.id,
    fullName: user.fullName,
    email: user.email,
    createdAt: user.createdAt,
    roles: user.roles ?? ["USER"],
  };

  localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(profile));
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(AUTH_CHANGE_EVENT));
  return profile;
}

export function getCurrentUser(): UserProfile | null {
  const value = localStorage.getItem(SESSION_KEY);
  if (!value) return null;

  try {
    const session: unknown = JSON.parse(value);
    if (
      typeof session !== "object" ||
      session === null ||
      !("id" in session) ||
      typeof session.id !== "string"
    ) {
      throw new Error("Invalid session");
    }

    const user = readUsers().find((candidate) => candidate.id === session.id);
    if (!user) {
      localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
      return null;
    }

    return {
      id: user.id,
      fullName: user.fullName,
      email: user.email,
      createdAt: user.createdAt,
      roles: user.roles ?? ["USER"],
    };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof MockAuthError) throw error;
    throw new MockAuthError("KhÃ´ng thá»ƒ Ä‘á»c phiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p. HÃ£y Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p láº¡i.");
  }
}

export function updateCurrentUser(input: { fullName: string; email: string }): UserProfile {
  const currentUser = getCurrentUser();
  if (!currentUser) {
    throw new MockAuthError("PhiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n. Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p láº¡i.");
  }

  const fullName = input.fullName.trim();
  const email = input.email.trim().toLocaleLowerCase("vi");
  const users = readUsers();

  if (users.some((user) => user.id !== currentUser.id && user.email === email)) {
    throw new MockAuthError("Email nÃ y Ä‘Ã£ Ä‘Æ°á»£c sá»­ dá»¥ng bá»Ÿi má»™t tÃ i khoáº£n khÃ¡c.");
  }

  const updatedUsers = users.map((user) =>
    user.id === currentUser.id ? { ...user, fullName, email } : user,
  );
  const updatedProfile: UserProfile = {
    ...currentUser,
    fullName,
    email,
  };

  localStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updatedUsers));
  localStorage.setItem(SESSION_KEY, JSON.stringify(updatedProfile));
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(AUTH_CHANGE_EVENT));
  return updatedProfile;
}

export function changeCurrentUserPassword(input: {
  currentPassword: string;
  newPassword: string;
}): void {
  const currentUser = getCurrentUser();
  if (!currentUser) {
    throw new MockAuthError("PhiÃªn Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p Ä‘Ã£ háº¿t háº¡n. Vui lÃ²ng Ä‘Äƒng nháº­p láº¡i.");
  }

  const users = readUsers();
  const user = users.find((candidate) => candidate.id === currentUser.id);
  if (!user || user.password !== input.currentPassword) {
    throw new MockAuthError("Máº­t kháº©u hiá»‡n táº¡i chÆ°a chÃ­nh xÃ¡c.");
  }

  const updatedUsers = users.map((candidate) =>
    candidate.id === currentUser.id
      ? { ...candidate, password: input.newPassword }
      : candidate,
  );

  localStorage.setItem(USERS_KEY, JSON.stringify(updatedUsers));
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(AUTH_CHANGE_EVENT));
}

export function logoutUser(): void {
  localStorage.removeItem(SESSION_KEY);
  window.dispatchEvent(new Event(AUTH_CHANGE_EVENT));
}

===== README.md =====
# Uni-Lend

Uni-Lend is a web application for managing lending and borrowing activities.

## Project Structure

```text
uni-lend/
â”œâ”€â”€ frontend/    # Next.js application
â”œâ”€â”€ backend/     # Spring Boot application
â”œâ”€â”€ .gitignore
â””â”€â”€ README.md
```

## Technologies

### Frontend

* Next.js
* React
* TypeScript
* Tailwind CSS

### Backend

* Java
* Spring Boot
* Spring Data JPA
* MySQL

## Running the Project

### Frontend

```bash
cd frontend
npm install
npm run dev
```

Frontend runs at:

```text
http://localhost:3000
```

### Backend

```bash
cd backend
./mvnw spring-boot:run
```

On Windows:

```powershell
.\mvnw.cmd spring-boot:run
```

Backend runs at:

```text
http://localhost:8080
```

The backend requires a MySQL connection and a Base64-encoded `JWT_SECRET` of at
least 32 random bytes. On Windows, generate a key in PowerShell with:

```powershell
$bytes = New-Object byte[] 32
$rng = [Security.Cryptography.RandomNumberGenerator]::Create()
$rng.GetBytes($bytes)
[Convert]::ToBase64String($bytes)
$rng.Dispose()
```

Create `backend/.env` with your database settings and generated key:

```dotenv
DB_URL=jdbc:mysql://localhost:3306/uni_lend
DB_USERNAME=your_mysql_username
DB_PASSWORD=your_mysql_password
JWT_SECRET=paste_the_generated_base64_key_here
CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS=http://localhost:3000
```

The application uses a built-in JWT issuer (`http://unilend.local`); no
external `JWT_ISSUER_URI` or `JWT_AUDIENCE` is needed. `JWT_EXPIRATION_MS` is
optional and defaults to 86400000 milliseconds (24 hours).
`CORS_ALLOWED_ORIGINS` accepts a comma-separated list of frontend origins and
defaults to `http://localhost:3000`. Keep credentials and signing keys in
`backend/.env`, which is ignored by Git; never commit them.

`POST /api/auth/login` accepts an email and password and returns a bearer token.
The account must already exist, be enabled, and have a BCrypt-encoded password
in the database. For registration, email verification, password reset, and the
frontend API inventory, see [`docs/API-FRONTEND.md`](docs/API-FRONTEND.md). Send the returned
token in the `Authorization` header as `Bearer <token>` for authenticated
endpoints.
Unauthenticated access is limited to `POST /api/auth/login`, public item and
category GET endpoints, and `GET /uploads/**` for displaying item images. Other
endpoints require a valid bearer token. Tokens must have a valid HS256
signature, issuer (default `http://unilend.local`), and expiration, and their
`sub` claim must match the user's email in the database. Item ownership and
admin role checks continue to use the user record in the database.
